### [1] [Harmonic and intermodulation emission from direct-to-device satellites - Harmonics of the Starlink 2.6 GHz downlink and the products around them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14336)
*Balthasar Indermuehle,Benjamin Winkel,Federico Di Vruno,Gyula Jozsa,Liroy Lourenco*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了星链直连设备（DTD）卫星的非线性发射产物对射电天文观测的干扰，发现其二次至四次谐波及互调产物显著超出射电天文保护标准，尤其在星座规模扩大时干扰加剧，表明仅靠单颗卫星的ITU-R限值无法保障射电天文安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Direct-to-device (DTD)卫星的非线性发射产物可能落入射电天文被动频段，威胁敏感观测；需量化其谐波与互调干扰水平，并评估现行ITU-R限值对射电天文的实际保护能力。

Method: 利用澳大利亚望远镜致密阵列（ATCA）单天线自相关数据，追踪DTD及其他卫星过境；测量2620–2630 MHz下行链路的谐波（H2–H4）及邻近互调产物；结合接收机测试与仿真，分析谐波来源、空间分布及星座级等效功率通量密度。

Result: H2–H4谐波源自卫星本体，强度随阶数下降约16 dB；H2在32%非指向积分中仍被探测到；H2辐射功率达-38.9 dBW/4kHz，已达ITU SM.329限值；8640颗卫星星座在Effelsberg和ATCA台址使H2、H5及2690–2700 MHz带外发射分别超保护标准65 dB、25 dB和>100 dB。

Conclusion: DTD卫星谐波辐射广泛且强，必须直接限制其空间辐射功率而非仅天线端口功率；现行单星ITU-R限值不足以保护射电天文，亟需针对星座整体制定更严格的辐射限值。

Abstract: Direct-to-device (DTD) satellites carry mobile base stations transmitting in terrestrial mobile bands. Their non-linear emission products can fall into radio astronomy bands, including passive bands. We measure the harmonics of the Starlink DTD downlink at 2620-2630 MHz and the intermodulation products around them, search for products next to the carriers, and compare them with the ITU-R unwanted-emission limits and radio astronomy protection criteria. We use autocorrelations of single Australia Telescope Compact Array antennas on tracked passes of DTD and other satellites with the 16 cm and 4 cm receivers. Harmonic ratios and receiver tests show that the harmonics are satellite-generated. We simulate the aggregate equivalent power flux density of a constellation at Effelsberg and the ATCA. The harmonics H2 to H4 at 5240, 7860 and 10480 MHz fall by about 16 dB per order, show no trend with elevation, and originate on the satellite. H2 is also detected in 32% of integrations more than 10 degrees from any DTD satellite. Inside the harmonic zones the spectra follow the n-fold convolution of the carriers; outside them, intermodulation products of order n+2 and higher lie on average 19 dB below the zone and switch between -23 and -15 dB within seconds. Next to the carriers no satellite emission is detected to 33 dB below the carriers. H2, >=-38.9 dBW in 4 kHz isotropic-equivalent, is at or above the SM.329 space-station limit; H3 and H4 meet it. For 8640 satellites the aggregate exceeds the protection criteria by 65 and 25 dB at the second and fifth harmonics, and by >100 dB for out-of-band emission at the mask level in 2690-2700 MHz. Harmonics are received far outside the serving beams. Their radiated level must be limited directly, not through the power at the antenna port. Compliance with per-satellite ITU-R limits does not protect radio astronomy from the aggregate of a constellation.

</details>


### [2] [ELISA: A Bayesian X/$γ$-ray Spectral Analysis Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14587)
*Wang-Chen Xue,Sheng-Lun Xie,Chao Zheng,Shao-Lin Xiong,Xiao-Bo Li,Yong Chen,Peng Zhang,Yan-Qiu Zhang,Jia-Cong Liu,Chen-Wei Wang,Wen-Jun Tan,Yue Wang,Jin-Peng Zhang,Zheng-Hang Yu*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ELISA——一个用于X/γ射线天文学光谱分析的开源Python工具包，结合JAX加速前向模型与统一拟合接口，支持快速优化和现代贝叶斯采样，并内置诊断功能以提升采样效率和模型可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有X/γ射线光谱拟合中，贝叶斯方法虽能更好处理计数统计、背景不确定性及参数简并性，但端到端可复现实现仍不便捷。

Method: 开发了基于JAX加速的前向模型与统一拟合接口的开源Python工具包ELISA，集成数据加载、建模、推断及模型验证与比较全流程，并内置收敛检查与预测评估诊断功能。

Result: 在Insight-HXMT蟹状星云观测光谱拟合示例中，ELISA的采样效率显著优于XSPEC默认MCMC实现。

Conclusion: ELISA为X/γ射线天文光谱分析提供了高效、可复现、易部署的贝叶斯分析框架，推动标准化与可验证建模实践。

Abstract: Forward-folded X/$γ$-ray spectral fitting increasingly benefits from Bayesian workflows to better capture counting statistics, background uncertainties, and complex parameter degeneracies, while fully reproducible end-to-end implementations are still not always straightforward to deploy in routine analyses. We present ELISA (Efficient Library for Spectral Analysis), an open-source Python toolkit that streamlines the full spectral analysis loop for X/$γ$-ray astronomy: data loading, model construction, inference, and model validation and comparison. ELISA couples a JAX-accelerated forward model with a unified fitting interface, supporting both rapid optimization and modern Bayesian sampling. Built-in diagnostics make convergence checks and predictive assessment a standard part of the workflow, motivating principled model refinement when needed. Using a spectral fitting example of the Insight-HXMT Crab observation, we find substantial improvements in sampling efficiency compared with XSPEC's default MCMC implementation. ELISA is available at https://github.com/wcxve/elisa and on PyPI at https://pypi.org/project/astro-elisa.

</details>


### [3] [Mid-IR Water-Vapor Seeing: Implications for ELT/METIS High-Contrast Imaging from VLTI Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14801)
*Abhirami S Raghu,Prashant Pathak,Olivier Absil,Benjamin Courtney-Barrer,Gilles Orban de Xivry,Julien Woillez,Alexis Matter,Philippe Bério,Roy van Boekel,Bernhard Brandl*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了大气水汽（WV）对极大望远镜（ELT）中红外高对比度成像（HCI）的影响，利用GRAVITY仪器的K波段数据量化水汽引起的色散波前误差，并发现可 precipitable water vapor (PWV) 是主导因素；结果表明在N波段波前误差达175 nm RMS，为METIS等下一代中红外仪器提供了水汽效应评估与校正的定量依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中红外波段虽有利于岩石系外行星直接成像，但地面极大望远镜受大气水汽引起的色散波前误差严重干扰，而传统自适应光学系统难以校正，亟需定量理解并抑制该效应。

Method: 基于VLTI/GRAVITY的K波段条纹跟踪数据，提取群延迟与相位延迟之差，反演望远镜上方差分水汽柱密度Σ；分析Σ的RMS（σ_Σ）与PWV、天顶距、视宁度、风速等参数的相关性；结合功率谱密度分析Σ的时间-空间变化特性，并外推至ELT尺度及L/N波段的波前误差。

Result: PWV与σ_Σ相关性最强；随PWV升高，Σ的时变增强；在ELT尺度下，相对于K波段，L波段和N波段波前误差分别达~15 nm和~175 nm RMS；垂直水汽分布影响PWV–σ_Σ关系。

Conclusion: 水汽引起的色散波前误差是中红外HCI的关键限制因素，本工作建立了可量化的PWV–波前误差映射关系，为METIS等仪器的观测策略优化与实时校正提供了物理基础。

Abstract: Direct imaging and characterization of rocky exoplanets remains one of the central challenges in exoplanetary science. The mid-infrared (mid-IR) offers a favorable high-contrast imaging (HCI) regime, as the planet-star contrast is more moderate than at shorter wavelengths. However, mid-IR observations with extremely large ground-based telescopes (ELTs) are strongly affected by spatio-temporal fluctuations in atmospheric water vapor (WV), which introduce chromatic wavefront errors that are largely invisible to adaptive optics systems operating at shorter wavelengths. This poses a significant challenge for mid-IR HCI instruments such as METIS, which aims to detect thermal emission from rocky exoplanets in the $N$-band (8--13 $μ$ m). To quantify WV-induced wavefront errors, we analyze $K$-band fringe-tracker data from the GRAVITY instrument at the VLTI and derive the optical path difference between group-delay and phase-delay measurements. This difference provides a direct measure of the differential WV column density ($Σ$) above the telescopes. We examine the dependence of the RMS differential WV column density ($σ_Σ$) on precipitable water vapor (PWV), airmass, seeing, and wind speed across multiple VLTI configurations. Among these parameters, PWV shows the strongest correlation with $σ_Σ$. We further explore the influence of the vertical PWV distribution on this relationship. Next, we use power-spectral-density analysis to quantify how increasing PWV modulates $Σ$ variability. The wavefront error associated with this phenomenon on ELT scale is approximately 15~nm RMS in the $L$-band and 175~nm RMS in the $N$-band, relative to the $K$-band. These results provide a quantitative framework for assessing and mitigating atmospheric WV effects in next-generation mid-IR HCI instruments.

</details>


### [4] [Modelling the Point Spread Function of ILMT images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14819)
*Naveen Dukiya,Bhavya Ailawadhi,Talat Akhunov,Monalisa Dubey,Sara Filali,Paul Hickson,Priyanshi Kumari,Gokul Singh Mehra,Kuntal Misra,Vibhore Negi,Kumar Pranshu,Jean Surdej,Sarvesh Kumar Yadav,Archana Gupta*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种建模国际液体镜望远镜（ILMT）实测点扩散函数（PSF）的方法，考虑了液体镜特性、仪器与大气因素及TDI跟踪模式引起的PSF空间变化，并利用PSFEx成功建模4k×4k图像块中二阶X-Y方向的PSF变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ILMT的PSF受液体镜特性、仪器设计和大气条件等多重因素影响，且在TDI模式下沿赤纬方向因星迹弯曲和差分运动而变化，在赤经方向也受天空条件影响，需建立准确的空间变化PSF模型。

Method: 使用PSFEx工具对4k×4k图像块进行建模，引入二阶X-Y空间变异性来刻画PSF随视场位置的变化，并结合ILMT光学系统（含5元校正镜）和TDI观测模式的特点进行PSF表征。

Result: 所提方法能稳健地再现ILMT图像中PSF及其在整个视场内的变化规律，验证了对复杂PSF空间变异性的有效建模能力。

Conclusion: 该PSF建模方法适用于ILMT这类特殊液体镜望远镜的数据处理，为后续高精度测光和形态分析提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: We have devised an approach to model the empirical Point Spread Function (PSF) of the images taken with the 4-meter International Liquid Mirror Telescope (ILMT). The characteristics of the ILMT PSF are influenced by the nature of the Liquid Mirror, as well as by instrument specific and atmospheric factors. As the telescope uses Time Delay Integration (TDI) mode for tracking the passing objects, the PSF varies along the declination axis due to differential motion and star-trail curvature. The 5-element optical corrector homogenizes the PSF to some degree, but does not completely eliminate the disturbing effects mentioned above. The PSF in the RA direction is changing due to the sky conditions. We show that we can successfully model the PSF of 4k$\times$4k chunks using PSFEx, accounting for second-order X-Y variability. Our software robustly reproduces the PSF and its variation across the field.

</details>


### [5] [From Sky to Science: Time Domain Astronomy with the ILMT Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14827)
*Kuntal Misra,Bhavya Ailawadhi,Talat Akhunov,Monalisa Dubey,Naveen Dukiya,Sara Filali,Paul Hickson,Priyanshi Kumari,Gokul Singh Mehra,Vibhore Negi,Kumar Pranshu,Jean Surdej,Sarvesh Kumar Yadav*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: PyLMT transient detection pipeline has detected ~10,000 transients and variable objects since November 2023 using ILMT images, covering diverse astrophysical phenomena such as supernovae, AGNs, variable stars, and Solar System objects.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To enable near real-time detection and characterization of optical transients and variable objects across a broad range of astrophysical phenomena using the ILMT survey.

Method: Utilization of the PyLMT transient detection pipeline on ILMT images for near real-time detection and classification of transients and variable sources.

Result: ~10,000 transients and variable objects detected since November 2023, spanning supernovae, variable stars, AGN variability, asteroids, and comets.

Conclusion: The ILMT survey, powered by the PyLMT pipeline, demonstrates strong potential for advancing studies of diverse astrophysical transients and variables, with highlighted science cases validating its broad scientific utility.

Abstract: The PyLMT transient detection pipeline has been operational since November 2023 and has detected $\sim$10,000 transients and variable objects in ILMT images in near real time. The survey has enabled the discovery and characterisation of a range of bright and faint optical transients. The detected population spans a broad diversity of astrophysical phenomena, including supernovae, variable stars, active galactic nuclei (AGN) variability, and Solar System objects such as asteroids and comets. We provide a concise overview of the detection of a variety of transient and variable classes identified with the ILMT, emphasising the survey's potential contributions to a broad class of astrophysical sources. We highlight representative science cases, including supernovae, AGNs, variable stars, and asteroids, to demonstrate the scientific potential of the ILMT survey.

</details>


### [6] [Occultation polarimetry as a probe of unresolved polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15811)
*A. G. Tevzadze*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于掩星观测的偏振测量理论框架，能够从无法分辨的全斯托克斯光变曲线中直接提取空间偏振混合程度的模型无关定量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低偏振可能源于局部偏振弱或强偏振区域的空间抵消；传统观测难以区分二者，而掩星过程可通过移动遮挡边界差异性采样源，打破空间简并。

Method: 针对静止、空间非相干源的单调掩星过程，建立斯托克斯参数变化与遮挡边界曲率采样之间的理论关系，并推导归一化斯托克斯矢量的精确方差分解。

Result: 偏振亏损被精确分解为三项非负贡献：局地偏振亏损、边缘样本内混合、及其平均偏振态间混合；最后一项可直接测量，给出总空间混合的严格下界；协方差张量进一步刻画了线/圆偏振等分辨偏振变化的方向与关联。

Conclusion: 掩星偏振测量是一种强大观测技术，能从不可分辨源的斯托克斯光变中提取模型无关、定量的空间偏振混合信息，揭示当前角分辨率下不可见的偏振结构特征。

Abstract: Context: Low polarization in an unresolved source may reflect weak local polarization or spatial cancellation between strongly polarized regions. An occultation can break this spatial degeneracy by differentially sampling the source as the occulting boundary moves. Aims: We develop a general theoretical framework for occultation polarimetry and determine what information can be inferred directly from unresolved full Stokes light curves, without reconstructing a polarization image or prescribing a source model. Methods: For a stationary, spatially incoherent source undergoing monotonic occultation, we relate Stokes variations to the polarization sampled by an arbitrarily curved occulting boundary and derive an exact variance decomposition of the normalized Stokes vector. Results: The polarization deficit decomposes exactly into three non-negative contributions: local polarization deficit, mixing within limb samples, and mixing between their mean polarization states. The last is directly measurable and provides a sharp, model-independent lower bound on total spatial mixing. The associated covariance tensor further characterizes the direction and correlations of the resolved polarization variations, including those between linear and circular polarization. Finite sampling preserves the bound, which can be strengthened through complementary occultation trajectories. Conclusions: Occultation polarimetry provides a powerful observational technique for extracting quantitative, model independent constraints on spatial polarization mixing directly from unresolved Stokes light curves. It offers a means to reveal both the extent and the form of polarization structure otherwise inaccessible at the available angular resolution.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了哈勃太空望远镜上宇宙起源光谱仪（COS）远紫外模式的长期径向速度（RV）稳定性，利用白矮星WD 0308-565长达15年的观测数据，发现其RV稳定性可达<3 km/s（中等信噪比）和600 m/s（高信噪比），且绝对RV精度与地基光学结果一致，为未来空间紫外高精度RV观测（如宜居世界天文台）提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估COS在远紫外波段进行长期精密径向速度测量的可行性与稳定性，拓展空间紫外天文学的科学能力，并为未来UV高分辨率光谱任务提供参考。

Method: 利用白矮星WD 0308-565在COS约17年运行期内的多历元远紫外光谱观测，分析其径向速度长期稳定性；结合信噪比与观测历元数，统计RV弥散（σ_V）；并与地基光学RV结果比对验证绝对精度。

Result: COS FUV模式在中等信噪比下实现<3 km/s的15年RV稳定性；高信噪比下达600 m/s；其绝对RV精度与地基光学一致；识别出适合逼近仪器RV极限的观测策略与天体类型。

Conclusion: COS具备开展空间紫外精密RV测量的能力，虽非专为RV设计，但已展现出实用稳定性；该工作开辟了空间UV-RV新方向，并为下一代空间UV光谱仪（如HWO）的稳定性目标提供实证基础。

Abstract: We investigate the long-term radial velocity (RV) stability of one of the Far-UV (FUV) modes on the Cosmic Origins Spectrograph (COS). We do so by using a standard white dwarf, WD~0308-565, that has been observed over a large fraction of COS's operational lifetime of 17 years. We demonstrate that for modest signal-to-noise COS can return an RV stability of $<$3 km s$^{-1}$ over a baseline of 15 years. For higher SNR and a smaller number of epochs, we obtain $σ_{\mathrm V}=600$ m s$^{-1}$. We also show that the absolute radial velocity accuracy for this target is consistent with results from ground-based optical spectroscopy. We discuss some observational strategies and astrophysical objects that may be well suited to pushing to the very RV limit of the instrument. Utilizing COS for precision RV work opens a new scientific avenue for space-based UV astronomy as COS approaches 20 years of operations and hints at what may be possible with the stability of a high-resolution UV spectrograph on future missions such as the Habitable Worlds Observatory.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍了使用无人机携带的90 GHz单频源对西蒙斯天文台小口径望远镜（SATs）进行远场波束校准的方法，并与行星观测得到的带平均波束进行对比，验证了频率分辨校准方案的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为建模系统频率依赖行为、追踪光学非理想性，并评估频率分辨波束校准方案的可行性。

Method: 利用无人机搭载的90 GHz单频源对SATs进行远场扫描测量，获取单频波束响应；再与4月和12月两次行星（带平均）观测波束对比，分析频率缩放行为及一致性。

Result: 两台SAT在90 GHz附近波束符合衍射极限预期，仅一台望远镜近旁瓣有轻微偏差；中心晶圆的合成无人机带平均波束与实测行星波束一致，ℓ < 500时平均偏差小于4%。

Conclusion: 该频率分辨的无人机校准方法可行，可有效支持高精度CMB观测中波束建模与系统误差控制。

Abstract: We present a far-field beam calibration of the Simons Observatory (SO) Small Aperture Telescopes (SATs) using scanned single-frequency measurements of a drone-mounted source emitting in the 90 GHz band, where the SATs have an angular resolution of $\sim$30$'$. We then compare the results from two campaigns in April and December 2025 to the band-averaged beams from planet observations by the SATs. Measuring the telescope beam response at different frequencies across the observing bands enables modeling the system's frequency-dependent behavior. This helps track optical non-idealities. The comparison with planet beams informs the frequency resolution necessary to accurately reconstruct the band-averaged beams in this way; it uses only the average drone and planet products, as it is intended to demonstrate the feasibility of the frequency-resolved calibration scheme rather than to set the precise beam covariance and instrument requirements. We find that the beams of the two SATs near 90 GHz follow the frequency scaling expected for diffraction-limited optics, with only modest deviations observed in the nearest lobes of one telescope. We establish consistency between the real band-averaged planet beams and the synthesized drone band-averaged beams for the center wafers of both telescopes, with an average deviation smaller than 4% for multipoles $\ell < 500$.

</details>


### [9] [Deep learning methods for gamma event selection in TAIGA-IACT image analysis in stereo-mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16269)
*E. O. Gres,A. P. Kryukov,P. A. Volchigov,A. P. Demichev,J. J. Dubenskaya,D. P. Zhurov,S. P. Polyakov,E. B. Postnikov,A. J. Razumov*

Motivation: 在TAIGA实验中，多台IACT协同观测可提升初级伽马射线物理参数重建精度，但伽马事例重建质量高度依赖于强子背景抑制性能，因此伽马事件选择是关键步骤。

Conclusion: 基于神经网络的伽马事件选择方法适用于多望远镜立体观测场景，可提升伽马射线天文数据分析的准确性和效率。

Abstract: In astrophysical complex TAIGA there are four Imaging Atmospheric Cherenkov telescopes (IACTs), located at a distance of 300-500 m from each other, due to which extensive air showers (EAS) generated by cosmic and gamma radiation are recorded by a single telescope or by multiple telescopes simultaneously (stereo-mode). The analysis of EAS images from two or more Cherenkov telescopes together is of particular interest, since for this approach the reconstruction of physical parameters of the primary gamma ray is much more accurate than in mono-observations. However, the gamma-ray event reconstruction strongly depends on the quality of the hadron background suppression of the classifier, therefore, the gamma event selection stage is one of the important ones in the further analysis of IACT data. We propose and study several neural network methods for gamma event selection in joint TAIGA-IACT image analysis. Neural network methods we suggested involves a single-stage analysis of IACT images from two telescopes, while the second method considers the formation of autoencoder-derived essential features that contain the EAS physical parameters, including the type of primary particle.

Abstract: Using bibliometric data from publication records, we investigate the institutional broadening of Japanese astronomy and astrophysics over 1996-2025. The number of Japan-affiliated publications increased from 588 to 2,156, and the number of unique participating Japanese institutions increased from 123 to 227, indicating a nominal broadening of the institutional base. However, institution-publication links remained concentrated among leading institutions, and the effective number of institutions, a frequency-weighted diversity measure, increased more gradually than the number of unique participating institutions. These results show that Japanese astronomy and astrophysics broadened institutionally, but not through a complete equalization of institutional participation. The observed change is best interpreted as an expansion of the participating institutional base around a persistent core of leading institutions.

Abstract: Wide-field narrowband imaging provides an efficient approach for mapping low-surface-brightness line emission over large angular scales. We present initial performance tests of Multi-Application Survey Telescope Array (MASTA) for narrowband imaging. Using commissioning data obtained with the QHY461 and COSMOS-8K cameras, we characterize the basic reduction procedure, photometric calibration, image quality, and point-source detection limits. The measured surface-brightness and point-source detection limits become deeper with increasing exposure time, roughly following the expected background-limited scaling. Under the current observing and reduction conditions, the COSMOS-8K camera generally reaches deeper surface-brightness limits, particularly in the red filters, while QHY461 has smaller pixel size and provides better sampling of the telescope point spread function (PSF). As demonstrations, we perform continuum subtraction for the nearby galaxy M106 and the supernova remnant Cygnus Loop using adjacent narrowband filters and reveal the Hα and [O III] structures in M106 and filamentary structures in the Cygnus Loop. We further compare the MASTA surface brightness Hα map of M106 with an independent SITELLE measurement. After accounting for [N II] contamination and the wavelength-dependent response of the 6544 Å filter in the f /1.7 converging beam, the corrected median ratio is 0.973. These tests indicate that MASTA is a promising facility for wide-field narrowband imaging. Further improvements in calibration frames, including flux calibration, field-dependent bandpass characterization, and spatially varying PSF modeling and matching will be required for quantitative studies of diffuse ionized gas.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统T矩阵方法因计算负担重，难以处理含大量组分的复杂非均匀/非对称粒子模型，导致在星际介质和系外行星大气等关键场景中依赖不准确的简化假设，影响消光曲线和透射光谱的正确解释。

Method: 采用基于Borghese、Denti & Saija原始框架的新T矩阵实现（Nano-particle Transition Matrix code），并针对现代硬件架构进行优化，以加速大规模粒子模型的电磁散射计算。

Result: 显著降低了复杂粒子模型（如ISM尘埃和热木星大气气溶胶）的T矩阵计算成本，使高保真度模拟成为可能，并可直接支撑JWST与ARIEL获取的系外行星透射光谱的物理反演。

Abstract: The Transition Matrix formalism (T-matrix) offers an optimal solution to the problem of radiation scattering and absorption by particles characterized by a high degree of chemical and structural complexity. However, its application to particle models composed by large numbers of constituents has been classically limited by the substantial computational burden of the method. As a consequence, the investigation of light scattering and absorption processes, in cases where particle asymmetry and non-homogeneity play an important role, typically need to rely on simplifying assumptions which affect the correct interpretation of transmission spectra and extinction curves. This problem has a huge impact on many fields of scientific application. In particular, it has a critical role in astrophysics, because the ability to model highly non homogeneous particles is a fundamental requirement to understand the role of extinction in different phases of the interstellar medium (ISM), as well as to probe the physics of light transmission in planetary atmospheres within the Solar System and beyond. In this work we describe the application of a new implementation of the T-matrix formalism, based on the framework originally developed by Borghese, Denti & Saija and named Nano-particle Transition Matrix code, which leverages the power of modern hardware architectures to reduce the computational costs related to the solution of relevant particle models for the investigation of dust in the ISM and of hot-Jupyter class planets atmospheric aerosols. This new implementation of the T-matrix formalism can handle calculations of increasing complexity with a direct impact in the interpretation of the transmission spectra of transiting exoplanets produced by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) and of those expected from the Atmospheric Remote-sensing Infrared Exoplanet Large survey (ARIEL).

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间域巡天产生异步、不规则采样的多波段图像序列，需在每次新观测后即时分类（如快速识别强引力透镜超新星以触发后续观测），现有方法（如RNN）建模能力受限。

Method: 提出OASIS分类框架，对比两种Transformer变体：因子化注意力（分离建模空间与时间交互）和联合注意力（统一建模）；使用与Bag et al. (2026b)相同的训练设置，并引入选择性读出机制优化预测。

Conclusion: 因子化注意力更适合该异步时空图像流分类任务，所提架构可复用于时域天文及其他需在线预测的动态空间特征分析场景。

Abstract: Time-domain surveys often produce asynchronous image sequences across irregular observation times and photometric bands. In many applications, predictions are needed after each new observation, particularly when transient sources must be identified early to trigger follow-up. Strongly lensed supernovae (LSNe), for which follow-up is particularly time-critical, provide a useful example. We formulate this setting as On-the-fly ASynchronous Image Stream (OASIS) classification and explore reusable transformer architectures for causal, on-the-fly classification. We demonstrate these architectures on the lensed-transient classification problem studied by Bag et al. (2026a,b), distinguishing LSNe Ia from unlensed astrophysical transients. We compare them with the recurrent baseline adopted by Bag et al. (2026b), which combines a ConvLSTM branch for image processing with a separate LSTM branch for encoding observation times. The baseline maps asynchronous observations onto a fixed band-by-observation grid, filling unobserved bands with zeros. Using a common training setup, we compare two transformer-based approaches to modeling interactions among spatio-temporal tokens: factorized attention, which separates spatial and temporal interactions, and joint attention, which models them together. On the demonstration dataset, factorized architectures achieve mean losses of $0.095$--$0.100$, compared with $0.128$ for the recurrent baseline and $0.107$ or higher for joint architectures. A selective readout further reduces the best loss to $0.089$ for a factorized model. These results suggest that factorized attention suits this dataset better, without implying universal architectural superiority. We release the architectures as reusable foundations for time-domain astronomy and other applications involving evolving spatial features that require on-the-fly predictions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 所提方法为光谱（或时间）分辨率的无标定估计提供了可靠、通用且实用的新途径，拓展了数据立方体元数据分析能力。

Abstract: We present a new method to estimate the spectral resolution of a radio data cube from its noise properties. We assume that the data cube is the result of the convolution of a spectrally-uncorrelated cube with a constant convolution kernel K. We demonstrate that K and the resolution, defined as K's integral, can be estimated thanks to the analytical relation between the autocorrelation of the noise spectra extracted from the cube and the power spectrum of the kernel. We discuss a number of algorithms that make this method robust in the realistic case of a finite number of spectra and channels, real signal and artefacts, and we present the results obtained for a number of datasets from the literature. We further highlight that this method is applicable to any data cube that meets our assumptions independently of the observing frequency or the exact nature of the cube's "third" axis, e.g., it could be used to measure the time resolution of transient-search data cubes. We make this method available as part of the open-source software tool spectre.

</details>

To address this issue, we formulate filament fragment association as a graph-based learning problem, where individual fragments are represented as nodes and candidate pairwise relationships are modeled as edges to infer whether fragments belong to the same filament. Furthermore, we propose a novel graph neural network (GNN) that incorporates multiplicative aggregation layers to enhance feature aggregation in the graph, replacing conventional additive aggregation with a multiplicative mechanism to better capture information between fragments. Abstract: The distribution of energetic neutral atom (ENA) differential flux density at the heliospheric boundary is the key to revealing the interaction mechanism between the solar wind and the interstellar medium. The results show that under different sampling times, the source distribution inverted by the CNN algorithm has a higher degree of agreement with the ideally expected distribution, and the CNN algorithm exhibits better stability and accuracy than the traditional algorithm under the conditions of low signal-to-noise ratio and short staring time. Abstract: We studied the detector response for a liquid-argon time-projection chamber (LArTPC) Compton camera targeting MeV gamma-ray astronomy by a Geant4-based Monte Carlo simulation. The simulation, implemented within ComptonSoft, incorporates electron-ion recombination, charge diffusion, and induction on the electrode pixels. We evaluated the angular resolution measure (ARM) and the energy reconstruction error as functions of electronic noise, pixel pitch, and the recombination correction method. In this work, we discuss the potential for such an instrument and the science it enables in complement to current long-term plans of the global astronomical community and in preparation for the next generation of observatories.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of optical turbulence (OT) is useful to optimise telescope scheduling and maximising observing efficiency, as the performance of adaptive optics systems strongly depends on turbulence conditions. While various numerical weather prediction and machine learning models are developed to predict these complex turbulence properties, properly evaluating their added value requires well-defined baseline reference models. This study establishes baseline performance thresholds for key astroclimatic parameters using two distinct models: one forecasting the parameter average over a specific timescale, and the other forecasting the temporal evolution over the same specific timescale. We apply these frameworks to extensive, multi-year datasets from the sites of the Very Large Telescope (VLT) and the Large Binocular Telescope (LBT) and discuss the possibility of extrapolating reference values of forecast performances for other sites based on their optical turbulence statistical characteristics. Our results show that baseline predictive accuracy varies according to the observing site, the specific parameter, the chosen timescale, and the forecasting approach. Therefore, an OT forecasting model's accuracy cannot be quantified in absolute terms, but relatively to the baseline.

We find that all the selected GCs follow a well defined AMR, with the exception of NGC6642 and Pal1. While the former is likely a member of the LKH merger event, thus putting chronological evidence for the existence of this progenitor on even more robust ground, for the latter we propose an alternative scenario according to which it formed in-situ, but in a metal-depleted region of the Galaxy, possibly due to the interaction with an unspecified merger event. This is statistically best-fit by 4 components, which we associate with significant events of the MW merger history. Cluster ages were finally calibrated to a more accurate absolute age scale based on asteroseismic measurements of the mass of red giant stars in NGC6791, and the resulting AMR was fit to compute the look-back time for the beginning of cluster formation in the MW. The fit yields t_GC,i=13.59+0.33-0.32 Gyr for initial cluster formation, and tU=13.79+/-0.34 Gyr for the age of the Universe. This value translates to H0=68.72+3.95-3.49(stat.)+2.65-2.32(sys.) km s-1 Mpc-1, where the systematic error comes from a realistic estimate of the zero point uncertainty on the GC absolute ages derived from stellar models.

The innermost ($a\le0.04$~pc) disc stars evolve into an S-star-like population. Dynamical friction from the cluster reduces the orbital size of a $2000\,M_\odot$ IMBH initially at $0.15$~pc by more than a factor of two within $10$~Myr. Prograde IMBHs tend to align with the stellar disc, whereas a $2000\,M_\odot$ retrograde IMBH can anti-align with the radially overlapping disc stars. Abstract: We introduce and describe the Globular cluster Observations for Luminosity-Dispersion distances (GOLD) project, which has the principal aim of determining independent distances based on the globular cluster (GC) absolute magnitude-velocity dispersion ($M_V - σ$) relation calibrated with Milky Way GCs with Gaia distances. The project comprises observations of GCs in 19 early- and late-type galaxies taken with FLAMES/GIRAFFE on ESO's Very Large Telescope. So far, 17 galaxies have been observed. The spectra cover the wavelength range 8483-9000 AA, including the calcium triplet region at a spectral resolution of $R\sim18,000$. The spectra provide line-of-sight velocities with typical uncertainties of $Δv = 1-2$ km s$^{-1}$. Using primarily velocities to separate extragalactic sources from foreground stars, we present here our initial sample of 1062 GCs and 101 ultra-compact dwarf galaxies (UCDs). For more than 650 of these objects we present the first velocity measurement. We discuss the available photometry and size measurements, and show that our velocities are in good agreement with previous studies. Our velocity catalogues are made publicly available. Beyond the principal aim of obtaining precision distances, additional science to be explored with these data includes host galaxy halo dynamics and clustering analysis, mass-to light ratios of GCs and UCDs in the sample, the stellar initial mass function and black hole occupation fraction of the GCs, and chemical abundances from high-dispersion spectra.

For star-forming galaxies, the total 0.5-8 keV luminosity follows $\log L_X \sim 39.5 + 0.9\log\mathrm{SFR}$, with an intrinsic scatter of about 0.9 dex. The 0.5-2 keV thermal luminosity follows $\log L_X \sim 38.7 + 1.1\log\mathrm{SFR}$, with an intrinsic scatter of about 0.7 dex. The thermal luminosity remains well correlated with SFR down to $\sim 10^{-5}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$ and is consistent with previous relations for star-forming galaxies. By contrast, the flatter and more scattered total X-ray luminosity-SFR relation suggests that unresolved X-ray binaries likely dominate at low SFR, while thermal X-ray emission remains a robust tracer of stellar feedback.

Abstract: Favored carriers of the Diffuse Interstellar Bands (DIBs) are carbonaceous molecules whose constituents have been presumably expelled by dying stars along with those of dust grains. The parallel evolution of DIB carriers and grains and their potential interplay are unknown. We assembled published R(V) measurements of 505 hot stars. We inverted a catalog of extinction data based on Gaia XP spectra and NIR photometry, and used the resulting new 3D extinction map to locate the opaque areas responsible for R(V) for each star. Using archived spectra, we measured their 5780, 5797 and 8620 A DIBs. In parallel, we compared local and line-of-sight (LOS) R(V)s from recent 3D maps with local and LOS Gaia DIB strengths from our recent map. Results: R(V)s from hot stars reveal variations on large spatial scales, confirming with independent data the published 3D pattern. Gaia DIB strength maps show large spatial scale variations similar to those of R(V) maps, with higher abundance of DIB carriers corresponding to higher R(V). LOS Gaia DIB strengths and R(V)s from 3D maps also correlate positively in the R(V) range 2.3-3.4, but there is no connection for higher R(V), likely due to the disappearance of DIB carriers in dense clouds. Despite averaging effects along the LOS to hot stars, we find differences in R(V)-DIB relationships among the three DIBs, with an evolution from negative to positive correlation from the zeta- to the sigma-type DIBs. This can be understood in the frame of the bimodal evolution of R(V) with the extinction, with the zeta (resp. sigma) DIB carrier disappearance in the decreasing (resp. increasing) phase of R(V). Various combinations of DIB strengths and DIB ratios may allow to define the range of R(V), but the relationship between DIBs and R(V) also appears to depend on the wavelength range used to determine R(V).

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管磁场在匹配恒星-暗晕质量关系等观测约束中看似不重要，但它显著影响星系周介质气体和宇宙线传播，是不可或缺的非热成分；然而其如何演化至观测到的微高斯量级仍不清楚。

Method: 采用FIRE-2磁流体动力学（MHD）模拟，涵盖银河系质量与矮星系模型，并对比含/不含宇宙线（CR）的情形，系统分析磁场在宇宙时间尺度上的演化规律及物理驱动机制。

Result: 发现宇宙线对磁场性质影响很小；z~0时饱和磁场强度由晕质量主导；所有模型均趋近于晕质量无关的磁能-湍能比f_B ~ 1–10%；磁场演化呈现普适三阶段（膨胀→放大→饱和），各阶段由冻结磁通或湍流驱动，并存在B ∝ ρ^{2/3} 和 ρ^{1/2} 的标度关系。

Conclusion: 磁场演化与星系形成协同进行，其三阶段特征具普适性；冻结磁通是主导机制，中间放大阶段可能源于结构形成、反馈与湍流共同激发的动力学过程，数值模拟中湍流可能是关键触发因素。

Abstract: Over the last decade, magnetic fields (B-fields) have become a common ingredient in galaxy formation simulations. Although seemingly unimportant in matching observational constraints such as the $M_*-M_{\rm halo}$ relations, the B-field influences the circumgalactic medium (CGM) gas and determines cosmic ray (CR) propagation, making it an indispensable non-thermal component. Nevertheless, the intricate physics of B-fields, particularly how they evolve to the observed $μ$G-level field, remains largely elusive. In this work, we systematically address the evolution of B-fields over cosmic time using the FIRE-2 (Feedback In Realistic Environments) magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) simulations. We present results for both Milky Way (MW)-mass and dwarf galaxies, with and without CRs. We find that CRs have little impact on B-field properties, while the halo mass controls the saturated B-field strength at $z\sim 0$. However, all runs reach a halo-independent magnetic-to-turbulent ratio of $f_{\rm B}\sim 1-10\%$, though the methods used to measure turbulence could affect this value. The feature is universal, though the timings depend on the initial location and star formation history of the gas. Ordinary radio supernovae and tidal disruption events are strongly disfavoured by the luminosity and 30-yr recovery, while refractive scintillation would itself require an ultracompact nuclear emitter. The persistent radio counterpart is best explained by accretion onto the central black hole. CO emission is also confined to this interval and is associated with CNM-rich regions. The observed phase structures are consistent with a dynamically compressed multiphase interaction layer in which compression increases the gas density, shortens the radiative cooling time, and drives the evolution from WNM through thermally unstable gas toward the CNM. The present interaction may both create new CNM, and further compress or grow pre-existing dense structures. Our results provide the missing link between previous evidence for dynamical interaction and molecular cloud formation in Draco, and identify CNM formation as a key step in transforming gas accreting toward the Galactic disk into cold atomic and molecular clouds.

Bayesian model comparison shows that the DC14-based halo density profiles are statistically preferred over gNFW for a substantial fraction of the sample. The inferred inner slopes from gNFW fits nevertheless follow the DC14 expectation and reproduce the expected dependence on the stellar-to-halo mass ratio, despite this not being explicitly imposed. In contrast, SFH-related quantities (e.g. formation time and 'burstiness' indicators) exhibit no independent predictive power on the DM halo inner slope once the dependence on stellar mass is accounted for. We identify central MBHs that are undergrown relative to other MBHs in similar mass galaxies and investigate their accretion, merger, and orbital histories within the simulations. Our results demonstrate that MBH assembly is dominated by in-situ growth, which in turn is impacted by the dynamical histories of MBHs within assembling galaxies. Abstract: Rapid advances in AI have opened a new era for physics, enabling unprecedented capabilities in research and discovery. However, these advances also bring challenges in verifying, trusting, and reusing the results they so rapidly produce. Formalization is a necessary part of the solution to these challenges. In this paper, as well as giving an overview of the role of AI and formalization in physics, we make the case that: (1) formalization is an essential layer for AI-driven discoveries in both computational and theoretical physics; (2) AI is lowering the barrier to, and increasing the efficiency of, formalization in physics, though it remains weaker at choosing definitions and designing libraries than at writing proofs; (3) the speed AI brings to formalization in physics currently comes at the cost of trust, credit, and community; (4) shared, community-reviewed formal libraries such as Physlib should be central in making progress in this field, both as a trusted, reusable foundation for physics results and as the environment in which AI systems can reason about physics; (5) formalizing physics raises challenges beyond those in mathematics, most importantly that a checked proof does not establish that its definitions faithfully capture the intended physics. We also give our perspectives on near-term and long-term directions in these areas.

Within the scanned domain, these regions span DM masses $m_χ\simeq0.19$-$4.5$ TeV, mediator masses $M_{Z_B}\simeq0.05$-$5.0$ TeV, and gauge couplings $g_B\simeq0.004$-$0.74$, with correlated values of these parameters. Abstract: Most of the mass of the visible universe originates from the strong interaction. Quantitatively reproducing the origin of the nucleon mass is one of the important challenges in fundamental science. In this paper, we explore the possibility of evaluating the ``gluon condensate term'' which is particularly difficult to determine among the contributions to the nucleon mass, using high-energy scattering experiments. The matched couplings give benchmark vacuum lifetimes of $1.62\times10^{12}\,\mathrm{s}$ and $2.80\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{s}$, while reheating normalized to the baryon abundance determines the primary spectrum. Abstract: An eV-scale sterile Majorana neutrino can suppress neutrinoless double-beta decay through destructive interference with the active-neutrino contribution, weakening the resulting constraints on sterile-neutrino parameters. The beta-decay bound restricts the light-neutrino masses and hence the cancellation between the active and sterile contributions to $m_{ββ}$. Abstract: We calculate the masses of the $J^P=\frac{1}{2}^-$ hidden-charm pentaquark states in the $SU(3)$ decuplet within the heavy pentaquark chiral perturbation theory (HPChPT) up to the next-to-leading order (NLO). Abstract: We present BSMScanner, a framework in which a beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) parameter scan is specified as a set of declarative YAML documents rather than written as a program. The user declares the free parameters and their priors, the mass matrices and the diagonalisations that act on them, the observables, the theory-validity conditions and the likelihood terms. The framework validates this specification, resolves it into a dependency graph and compiles it once into a typed C++ evaluation kernel, which is then driven by an independently chosen scanning engine: uniform sampling, two differential-evolution engines, or a staged basin-discovery orchestrator, optionally followed by Markov-chain posterior refinement. Flavour physics matrix diagonalisation, PMNS and CKM construction in fixed conventions, tabulated oscillation likelihoods and a library of common flavour observables are provided by the framework and imported by name, so that a model file contains only model-specific definitions; quantities without a closed form, such as a relic density, enter through a C++ or Python plugin interface. We describe the specification schema, the observable and likelihood library, the scanning engines and their configuration, and demonstrate the framework on six benchmark models with $4$ to $26$ free parameters, giving for each the specification, the scan configuration, the resulting best fit and analytic cross-checks of the result.

</details>

Abstract: Recently, the possibility of having sterile Majorana neutrinos charged under $B-L$ gauge symmetry in the bulk of the dark dimension has been explored. The resulting framework organizes covalent-bond configurations as tending to lie below threshold and creation-bond configurations as tending to appear above threshold, while the annihilation bond can shift a pre-existing molecular configuration either downward or upward through mixing with a nearby compact state. We use the Schwinger-Keldysh closed-time-path formalism of real-time finite-temperature field theory to study these effects. For the $φ^6$ model, we derive the effective action and the corresponding Langevin equation. We further extend the formalism to the two scalar fields case, obtaining the coupled Langevin equations. These results provide a theoretical framework for the bubble nucleation simulation of the non-equilibrium phase transition process, laying the foundation for reliable predictions of baryogenesis and gravitational wave signals.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The single production of first-generation $\mathrm{SU}(2)_L$ doublet vector-like leptons (VLLs) at the Compact Linear Collider (CLIC) with $\sqrt{s}=3~\mathrm{TeV}$ is studied. The neutral and charged VLL states are investigated through $e^+e^-\rightarrowν_e\bar N+\barν_eN$ and $e^+e^-\rightarrow e^\pm E^\mp$, respectively. The neutral VLL is searched for in the $N\rightarrow W^\mp e^\pm$ channel with $W^\pm\rightarrow jj$, leading to the $e^\pm+2j+\slashed{E}_T$ final state. The charged VLL is explored through $E^\pm\rightarrow e^\pm h$ ($h\rightarrow b\bar b$) and $E^\pm\rightarrow e^\pm Z$ ($Z\rightarrow q\bar q$), yielding the $e^+e^-+2b$ and $e^+e^-+2j$ signatures. Signal and background simulations include initial-state radiation, beamstrahlung, and detector effects. Cut-based analyses are performed for integrated luminosities of $1$, $3$, and $5~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$ to obtain the exclusion and discovery sensitivities in the VLL mass--mixing parameter space. The neutral-VLL channel gives the best sensitivity. At $1~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, the $2σ$ exclusion reaches $\varepsilon\simeq0.013$ at $M_N=1.3~\mathrm{TeV}$ and $\varepsilon\simeq0.065$ at $M_N=2.9~\mathrm{TeV}$, while the $5σ$ discovery reach corresponds to $\varepsilon\simeq0.02$--$0.10$. At $5~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, the charged-VLL channels probe masses up to about $2.9~\mathrm{TeV}$ and $2.8~\mathrm{TeV}$ in the $e^\pm h$ and $e^\pm Z$ modes, respectively. These results demonstrate that a 3 TeV CLIC can provide sensitive probes of first-generation doublet VLLs over a wide mass range, including scenarios with small VLL--electron mixing.

### [95] [Decay-Assisted Leptogenesis during Preheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17084)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轻子生成模型常假设右手性中微子在产生完成后才开始衰变，忽略了预热早期动力学过程中产生与衰变的耦合效应；本文旨在通过更自洽的量子动力学框架揭示该耦合对轻子不对称性的影响。

Method: 采用量子玻尔兹曼方程框架，模拟预热阶段 inflaton 振荡背景下右手性中微子的非平衡产生（受泡利阻塞限制）与同步衰变过程，追踪其占据数演化及轻子数生成。

Conclusion: 预热阶段右手性中微子的产生与衰变不可割裂处理；动态耦合机制能大幅提升轻子生成效率，为解释观测重子不对称性提供更可行的非热机制。

Abstract: We revisit the non-thermal leptogenesis mechanism during preheating using the quantum Boltzmann equations. This framework allows us to study the production and decay of heavy right-handed neutrinos in a consistent manner. Due to their fermionic nature, right-handed neutrino production can saturate after a few inflaton oscillations when their occupation numbers approach unity. Right-handed neutrino decays can deplete their occupation numbers, allowing more particles to be produced and generating a larger lepton asymmetry. Compared with the case where right-handed neutrinos decay only after production has finished, we find that the final baryon asymmetry can be enhanced by two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [96] [Concurrence Dynamics in Self-Gravitating Mixed Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16508)
*Raoul Serao,Gianpaolo Torre,Antonio Capolupo*

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究牛顿自引力是否能在量子系统（特别是混合中微子）中产生可观测的量子纠缠，从而为量子引力效应提供潜在检验途径。

Method: 基于质量算符间的引力耦合构建双粒子哈密顿量，分离出局部项与有效伊辛相互作用项；对初态为可分味态的情形，解析求解协和度；并将其与先前得到的约化态纯度建立对应关系；分析马约拉纳相的影响。

Result: 协和度以解析形式给出：在零与仅依赖混合角的最大值之间振荡，引力耦合决定振荡周期；协和度与约化态纯度严格一一对应；协和度不依赖马约拉纳相，故狄拉克与马约拉纳描述下纠缠动力学完全相同。

Conclusion: 牛顿自引力可通过有效伊辛相互作用在混合中微子系统中产生可定量刻画的纠缠；该纠缠特性由混合角主导、与马约拉纳相无关，且有清晰的信息论诠释（通过纯度），从而明确了引力诱导纠缠的本质特征与解析结构。

Abstract: We study the entanglement generated by Newtonian self-gravity in a two-particle system of mixed neutrinos and quantify it by means of the concurrence. Starting from the gravitational coupling between the mass operators, we show that the two-particle Hamiltonian splits into local single-particle terms and an effective Ising interaction in the mass basis, the latter being solely responsible for the generation of correlations. For initially separable flavor states the concurrence is obtained in closed analytic form: it oscillates between zero and a maximum fixed by the mixing angle alone, while the gravitational coupling sets the recurrence time. We further establish a one-to-one correspondence between the concurrence and the reduced-state purity previously derived for this model, thus providing an independent quantum-information description of the same gravitationally induced correlations. Finally, we show that the concurrence does not depend on the Majorana phase, so that Dirac and Majorana parametrizations yield identical entanglement dynamics. These results isolate the genuinely entangling sector of the gravitational evolution and provide a compact analytic characterization of gravity-induced quantum correlations in mixed neutrino systems.

</details>


### [97] [Dark Matter Stability from residual flavour symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17210)
*Giacomo Landini,Simone Marciano,Federico Mescia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的框架，其中暗物质稳定性自然源于连续味对称性自发破缺后残留的离散Z_N对称性，无需人为引入稳定化对称性；以SU(2)_F ⊗ U(1)_F Froggatt-Nielsen对称性为例构建原型模型，成功再现轻子质量谱与PMNS混合矩阵，并将最轻惰性中微子作为稳定暗物质候选者；在freeze-in机制下研究其唯象学，表明连续味对称性可统一解决味物理难题与暗物质起源、稳定性和产生问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决暗物质稳定性需人为引入额外对称性的理论缺陷，并统一解释味物理难题与暗物质基本性质。

Method: 构建基于连续味对称性（Abelian与非Abelian）自发破缺后残留离散Z_N对称性的模型无关框架；以SU(2)_F ⊗ U(1)_F Froggatt-Nielsen对称为例构造具体模型；结合轻子质量谱、PMNS矩阵拟合及freeze-in机制下的暗物质产生分析。

Result: 证明残留Z_N对称性可自然保障暗物质稳定性；原型模型同时复现观测到的带电轻子质量谱和PMNS混合矩阵；最轻惰性中微子成为稳定暗物质候选者；freeze-in机制下，由高维算符介导的暗物质产生过程与参数空间兼容。

Conclusion: 连续味对称性不仅可解决味物理谜题，还能自洽地提供暗物质的起源、稳定性及产生机制，无需额外假设，具有高度统一性与自然性。

Abstract: We propose a model-independent framework in which Dark Matter stability emerges naturally from residual discrete $\mathbb{Z}_N$ symmetries left unbroken by the spontaneous breaking of continuous flavour symmetries, both Abelian and non-Abelian. No ad hoc stabilizing symmetry is required, as Dark Matter stability is an inherent consequence of the underlying flavour dynamics. As an explicit example, we construct a prototype model based on a $\text{SU}(2)_\text{F} \otimes \text{U}(1)_\text{F}$ Froggatt-Nielsen symmetry, which successfully reproduces the observed charged lepton mass spectrum and the PMNS mixing matrix, while identifying the lightest sterile neutrino as a stable Dark Matter candidate. Furthermore, we study the Dark Matter phenomenology within our model in the freeze-in regime, including production channels mediated by higher-dimensional operators suppressed by the cutoff scale $Λ$. Our analysis shows that continuous flavour symmetries can provide a unified, self-consistent framework addressing both the flavour puzzle and the origin, stability, and production of Dark Matter.

</details>


### [98] [Parton distribution functions and strong coupling constant: MSHT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16573)
*T. Cridge,L. A. Harland-Lang,M. Reader,R. S. Thorne*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了MSHT系列全局部分子分布函数（PDF）拟合工作，涵盖方法论、理论输入、数据集、高阶QCD和电弱修正的影响、参数化灵活性的闭合检验、实验数据误差的误差处理，以及强耦合常数的最新确定结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升全球PDF拟合的精度与可靠性，以支持高能物理实验和理论预测。

Method: 采用特定参数化形式，结合高阶QCD和电弱理论修正，拟合多组实验数据，并开展闭合检验及误差传播分析。

Result: 展示了高阶修正对PDF拟合的影响，改进了参数化灵活性评估与实验误差处理，并给出了强耦合常数α_s的最新PDF拟合结果。

Conclusion: MSHT PDF拟合持续受益于理论与方法学进步，显著提升了PDF精度及其在LHC等实验中的应用价值。

Abstract: We present an overview of the MSHT family of global PDF fits. We describe the underlying methodology, including parameterisation choice, theoretical inputs and datasets included in the fit. We describe the impact of recent theoretical advances in the inclusion of higher order QCD and EW corrections on the fit and their implications for phenomenology. We discuss recent methodological improvements and ongoing studies relating to closure tests of the parameterisation flexibility and the inclusion of errors on errors for experimental data. We in addition discuss recent results on the determination of the strong coupling from the MSHT fits.

</details>


### [99] [From Earth to Mars and Beyond: AI-Driven Cosmic-Ray Validation and Prediction with CorsikaAgent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16607)
*Zhuo Chen,Zhizheng Zhao,Jinhong Shen,Leyun Gao,Cheng-en Liu,Qite Li,Zijian Wang,Qiang Li*

TL;DR: 本文开发了CorsikaAgent系统，用于配置、执行和验证宇宙线空气簇射模拟，并预测未探索环境中的粒子通量；该系统基于大语言模型，具备可审计性，能自主重构模拟参数以适应新环境（如火星、金星），并成功复现地球海平面μ子通量及火星基准结果，首次预测了金星表面μ子通量。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升宇宙线模拟的可审计性、可复现性与跨环境泛化能力，尤其针对缺乏实测数据的行星环境（如金星）进行首例第一性原理预测。

Result: CorsikaAgent在地球上重现海平面μ子通量，与实验数据及解析参数化一致；在火星上复现CORSIKA 8基准；在金星上首次给出表面μ子通量预测；其独立采样结果与直接计算在当前精度下一致。

Conclusion: CorsikaAgent实现了从可审计复现到跨行星第一性原理预测的范式扩展，但受限于仅质子初级假设、高动量统计不足及系统误差处理不完善，当前物理精度仍有待提升。

Abstract: We calculate the sea-level cosmic-ray muon flux with CORSIKA and develop CorsikaAgent, an auditable large-language-model-based system for configuring, executing, and validating air-shower simulations, and for predicting particle fluxes in unexplored environments. The physics calculation uses proton primaries described by the solar-minimum spectrum of Papini et al., with UrQMD at low interaction energies and either Sibyll 2.3e or QGSJET-III at high energies. The predicted momentum spectra at approximately 0 and 75 degrees are compared with experimental measurements of the atmospheric muon flux and with commonly used analytic parametrizations. The two interaction-model configurations give similar spectra and describe the principal momentum and zenith-angle dependences of the data. The agent independently reconstructs the same observable through a different sampling strategy and keeps every steering card, executable, seed, log, and analysis script bound to the result it produced; its output is consistent with the direct calculation at the level resolved by the present comparison. Transferred to Mars, the workflow reproduces the CORSIKA 8 benchmark, and transferred to Venus it produces the first prediction of the surface muon flux, an environment for which no measurement exists. The agent thus extends the workflow from auditable reproduction to first-principles prediction: it autonomously reconfigures the simulation(atmosphere, magnetic field, electric field)for new environments, while the proton-only primary model, limited high-momentum statistics, and an incomplete systematic-uncertainty treatment bound the physical precision that can currently be claimed.

</details>


### [100] [Signature of GW from the Hidden Sector in light of the Recent LZ Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18234)
*Sujit Kumar Sahoo,Narendra Sahu,Vicky Singh Thounaojam*
