TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于U(1)_X隐藏扇区的理论模型，解释LZ实验观测到的单个核反冲事件，该模型通过标量场真空期望值实现对称性破缺，同时解释暗物质性质、引力波信号及热遗迹丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ合作组近期观测到的单个核反冲事件（248 keV，3.4σ显著性），并建立与暗物质候选者、早期宇宙一级相变产生的引力波以及热遗迹丰度之间的多信使联系。

Method: 构建一个带有自发破缺U(1)_X对称性的隐藏扇区模型，引入带电矢量样单态费米子作为暗物质候选者，并通过标量场VEV生成规范玻色子质量、微小马约拉纳质量分裂及一级相变引力波信号；结合直接探测、宇宙学约束与引力波探测前景进行参数空间扫描。

Result: 确定了能同时满足LZ事件解释、正确热暗物质遗迹丰度、未来μARES/THEIA可观测低频引力波信号，以及现有天体物理和宇宙学限制的可行参数区域。

Conclusion: U(1)_X隐藏扇区模型提供了一个自洽的多信使框架，将地下直接探测信号、早期宇宙动力学与引力波天文学统一起来，为暗物质本质研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: The LZ collaboration has recently reported a single nuclear-recoil event at $E_R=248\pm23_\text{stat}\pm23_\text{sys}$ keV, observed in a 2.84 tonne-year exposure with a local significance of $3.4σ$, pointing towards an inelastic dark matter (DM) interpretation. We explain this event within a gauged $U(1)_X$ hidden-sector model, spontaneously broken by a singlet scalar whose vacuum expectation value (VEV) gives rise to: \textit{(i)} gauge boson mass, \textit{(ii)} a small Majorana mass for the vector-like singlet fermion, the DM candidate, charged under the new symmetry, which splits the fermion into two pseudo-Dirac states (mass splitting proportional to the scalar VEV) thus resulting in a purely off-diagonal vector current, \textit{(iii)} the frequency and the amplitude of the GW signal arising from the hidden sector symmetry breaking via first-order phase transition (FOPT). The symmetry-breaking scale thus provides a multi-messenger probe connecting terrestrial direct-detection kinematics at the LZ and early-universe FOPT thermodynamics while obtaining thermal DM relic abundance. We delineate the viable parameter space compatible with the LZ event, correct relic density, and detectable GW signals in the low frequency regimes at future interferometers like $μ{\rm ARES}$ and THEIA while satisfying existing cosmological, astrophysical, and terrestrial bounds.

</details>


### [101] [Two-pion source analysis in small to large systems at SPS energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16786)
*Barnabás Pórfy,Zsombor Marcell Rácz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用UrQMD模型模拟了^7Be+^9Be和^{129}Xe+^{139}La中心碰撞中的双π介子源分布，并采用Lévy稳定分布拟合，系统研究了源参数随对横质量、碰撞能量和系统尺寸的变化规律，为NA61/SHINE实验提供纯强子参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为NA61/SHINE实验提供纯强子背景参考，并统一理解不同大小碰撞系统中pion发射的时空结构。

Method: 基于UrQMD模型（级联模式）模拟轻、中、重离子碰撞，施加与实验一致的动力学选择，对双π介子源进行一维Lévy拟合，分析源参数随横质量、能量和系统尺寸的依赖关系。

Result: 所有模拟系统中的源分布均能被Lévy分布良好描述；源参数呈现系统性演化趋势；结果与Ar+Sc中间系统数据一致。

Conclusion: Lévy稳定源函数可作为描述不同尺度强子碰撞中pion发射时空结构的统一唯象图像，本模拟结果构成NA61/SHINE实验重要的纯强子基准。

Abstract: Over the past decade, femtoscopic measurements using Lévy-stable source functions have been carried out in collision systems ranging from small to large at SPS, RHIC, and LHC energies. In recent years, these have been accompanied by a number of phenomenological studies of the same systems and energies. We contribute to this line of work by investigating the shape of two-pion pair-source distributions in central $^7$Be+$^9$Be and $^{129}$Xe+$^{139}$La collisions simulated with the UrQMD model in cascade mode. To make the comparison with measurements meaningful, kinematic selections similar to the experimental ones are applied to the simulated events and particles. One-dimensional Lévy fits are performed, and the resulting source parameters are studied as functions of pair transverse mass, collision energy, and system size. We find that the simulated sources are well described by Lévy distributions in all systems considered, which offers a common phenomenological picture of the space-time structure of pion emission. Finally, we compare our findings with results from an intermediate system, Ar+Sc. The results serve as a purely hadronic reference for past and upcoming femtoscopic measurements of the NA61/SHINE experiment.

</details>


### [102] [Cosmological Consequences of a Highly Decoupled Right-handed Neutrino](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18334)
*Stephen F. King,Rishav Roshan,Sudipta Show*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了最小类型I跷跷板模型中一个解耦的右手中微子（RHN）对宇宙学的影响，特别是在U(1)_{B-L}模型中其通过规范相互作用产生，并引发早期物质主导时期，稀释重子不对称性、延迟轻子味平衡，并在宇宙弦引力波谱中引入可探测的特征拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最小类型I跷跷板模型中，一个右手中微子可能因极弱耦合而解耦，但其长寿命可能导致它主导宇宙早期能量密度，进而影响重子生成、热历史和引力波信号——这一效应此前常被忽略。

Method: 基于最小U(1)_{B-L}扩展模型，分别分析轻est（N₁）和heaviest（N₃）RHN解耦情形下的热历史演化；计算RHN产额、衰变时间、早期物质主导时期持续时间及其对重子不对称性稀释和轻子味动力学的影响；并推导由此导致的宇宙弦引力波谱修正及特征频率。

Result: 解耦RHN引发早期物质主导时期：(1) 稀释重子不对称性；(2) 若N₃解耦，则延迟带电轻子Yukawa平衡，使更轻的N₁可在非味区分区产生不对称性；(3) U(1)_{B-L}破缺形成的宇宙弦引力波谱出现由解耦RHN质量和最轻中微子质量共同决定的拐点频率，处于SKA、LISA等未来探测器灵敏范围。

Conclusion: 解耦RHN虽不参与中微子质量生成，却是连接粒子物理与宇宙学观测（尤其是引力波）的关键桥梁；其质量与轻中微子质量共同设定了可观测引力波谱特征，为检验B-L对称性破缺提供新探针。

Abstract: In the minimal type-I seesaw, one right-handed neutrino (RHN) can decouple from neutrino mass generation. Such a state is usually neglected, yet its feeble couplings make it long-lived, and it can dominate the energy density of the Universe before decaying. We study this in a minimal $U(1)_{B-L}$ model, where the RHNs are produced through $B-L$ gauge interactions, for the cases of a decoupled lightest RHN $N_1$ and a decoupled heaviest RHN $N_3$. The resulting early matter-dominated era dilutes the baryon asymmetry, and for decoupled $N_3$ it also delays the equilibration of the charged-lepton Yukawa interactions, allowing a lighter $N_1$ to generate the asymmetry in the unflavoured regime. Breaking $U(1)_{B-L}$ forms cosmic strings whose gravitational-wave background acquires a spectral break from the early matter-dominated era, with a frequency set by the decoupled RHN mass and the lightest neutrino mass, within reach of future detectors such as SKA, THEIA, LISA, $μ$ARES, DECIGO and ET.

</details>


### [103] [Conserved-charge diffusion in hot and dense QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16829)
*Dushmanta Sahu,Kangkan Goswami,Raghunath Sahoo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了高温高密夸克物质中重子数、电荷和奇异数的耦合扩散，利用三味NJL模型计算了完整的扩散矩阵，并分析了其对温度和重子化学势的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解重子丰富QCD物质中守恒荷的耦合输运特性，需要在自洽生成夸克质量的框架下研究多组分扩散行为。

Method: 采用三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型，结合弛豫时间动理学方法，自洽计算包含对角与非对角元的完整扩散矩阵，并分析广义电荷扩散率与迁移率。

Result: 发现非对角输运系数具有明显的温度与重子化学势依赖性；在有限重子化学势下，重子与奇异数迁移率曲线发生交叉。

Conclusion: 该工作为重子丰富QCD物质中耦合守恒荷输运提供了模型基准。

Abstract: We study the coupled diffusion of baryon number, electric charge, and strangeness in hot and dense quark matter using a three-flavor Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model with self-consistently generated constituent quark masses. The complete diffusion matrix is evaluated within a relaxation-time kinetic approach, including its diagonal and off-diagonal components. We find that the off-diagonal transport coefficients exhibit distinct temperature and baryon-chemical-potential dependence associated with the flavor-dependent composition of the charge carriers. We further investigate generalized charge diffusivities and mobilities obtained from the corresponding susceptibilities. At finite baryon chemical potential, the baryon and strangeness mobility scales cross, reflecting the different density dependence of their transport coefficients and susceptibilities. The results provide model benchmarks for coupled conserved-charge transport in baryon-rich QCD matter.

</details>


### [104] [Event Generation for Neutrino and Muon DIS at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16836)
*Paulina Hernandez-Sainz,Felix Kling,Jelle Koorn,Juan Rojo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种蒙特卡洛事件生成器（Sherpa、Herwig7、POWHEG）与解析计算（YADISM）及中微子专用模拟工具（GENIE、NuWro、GiBUU）在LHC上中微子与缪子引发的深度非弹性散射（DIS）中的预测，评估了高阶QCD修正、核部分子分布函数（PDF）、强子化模型和QED辐射等关键因素的影响，并将结果应用于FASER实验的数据对比与物理分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FASER和SND@LHC实验在LHC前向区域探测中微子和缪子，开启TeV能区深度非弹性散射研究新窗口，但其实现依赖于高精度DIS模拟，而当前不同模拟工具间缺乏系统比对与不确定性量化。

Method: 系统比对NLO通用蒙特卡洛生成器（Sherpa、Herwig7、POWHEG）与解析结构函数计算（YADISM）及中微子专用模拟包（GENIE、NuWro、GiBUU）；量化QCD高阶修正、PDF（含核效应）、强子化、QED辐射等因素对包含观测量及FASER标定区域的影响；结合FASER最新数据开展验证与物理应用分析。

Result: 各MC生成器在统一设置下对包含和微分DIS观测量预测一致；但在强子末态敏感观测量上仍存在显著差异；基于比对结果，成功实现与FASER最新胶片/电子探测器数据的对比，提升了奇异PDF约束能力，并给出半包容DIS中带电强子产额的可靠预测。

Conclusion: 现代MC生成器已具备可靠模拟TeV能区中微子DIS的能力，但强子化建模仍是主要不确定性来源；本工作为FASER/SND@LHC中微子物理分析提供了基准模拟框架与不确定性评估方法，推动高能中微子相互作用与核子结构研究。

Abstract: Neutrinos and muons produced in the forward direction of LHC collisions and detected by the FASER and SND@LHC experiments enable a comprehensive deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) programme, covering both neutral and charged currents, at TeV energies. This programme aims to provide stringent constraints on proton and nuclear structure as well as on hadron fragmentation, and to offer novel insight into neutrino interactions at the highest energies achieved in a laboratory. Realising this potential requires accurate and precise simulations of neutral- and charged-current DIS. We systematically compare the predictions of NLO general-purpose Monte Carlo (MC) event generators for neutrino- and muon-initiated DIS at the LHC (Sherpa, Herwig7, and POWHEG) against analytical structure-function calculations (YADISM) and dedicated neutrino simulation packages (GENIE, NuWro, GiBUU). Within this framework, we quantify the role of higher-order QCD corrections, (nuclear) parton distribution functions (PDFs), parton shower and hadronisation modelling, and QED radiation both for inclusive observables and for the FASER fiducial selections. We also present representative applications to the FASER neutrino programme: comparisons with its most recent data from the emulsion and electronic sub-detectors, a study of strange PDF sensitivity in the dimuon final state, and predictions for charged-hadron production in semi-inclusive DIS. Our analysis highlights the agreement of modern event generators for inclusive and differential DIS observables once common settings are adopted, while differences remain for observables sensitive to the hadronic final state.

</details>


### [105] [Chiral partners of strange doubly heavy baryons: Mass relations and decay predictions after the 2026 LHCb discoveries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16841)
*Maciej A. Nowak,Ismail Zahed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于2026年LHCb对Ω_cc⁺、Ξ_cc⁺和B_s0*(5700)⁰的三项关键测量，构建了奇异双重重子（ccs、bbs及条件性bcs）的宇称伙伴多重态，并预测其质量、超精细劈裂与衰变特性；所有预测态均较窄，适合LHCb通过Ω_QQπ⁰/γ道进行高分辨质量与衰变模式研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用LHCb新观测的双重重强子（特别是含奇夸克的重子与新发现的B_s0*(5700)⁰）来实验约束并检验手征伙伴理论，尤其是Ma-Harada预言及重夸克-二夸克模型在奇异双重重子体系中的适用性。

Method: 结合LHCb质量测量、重夸克-二夸克超精细关系、手征加倍理论给出的手征能隙，以及介子到重子的手征宇称能隙迁移机制，构建ccs、bbs及条件性bcs宇称多重态；通过匹配已发表的介子宽度计算（Bardeen–Eichten–Hill与Harada–Rho–Sasaki）统一规范，估算各态绝对宽度与选择定则。

Result: 预测ccs负宇称态质量约为4.075 GeV与4.183 GeV；bbs态质量相对其基态由B_s手征能隙332 MeV确定；所有预测态均为窄宽（主导探测器分辨率）；提出Ω_QQπ⁰与Ω_QQγ作为LHCb关键搜寻道；发现上层ccs态虽高于K阈值，但在旁观者极限下受保护。

Conclusion: 该分析首次实现对手征伙伴双重重奇异重子的实验约束性预言，为LHCb后续精确谱学与衰变研究提供可验证框架，同时支持紧凑S波cc源图像及手征对称性在重强子物理中的延伸适用性。

Abstract: Three spectacular LHCb measurements in 2026---the observations of $Ω_{cc}^{+}$, $Ξ_{cc}^{+}$, and the beauty-strange structure $B_{s0}^{*}(5700)^0$---make possible an experimentally constrained analysis of chiral partners of strange doubly heavy baryons. The doubly charmed masses test the earlier Ma--Harada prediction and support a compact, internally S-wave $cc$ color source. We combine this comparison with the heavy-quark--diquark hyperfine relation and with chiral gaps from the theory of chiral doublers, to construct consistent $ccs$ and $bbs$ parity multiplets and a conditional extension to $bcs$. Under meson-to-baryon parity-gap transfer, the $349$ MeV input from the $D_s$ sector gives negative-parity $ccs$ targets near $4.075$ and $4.183$ GeV. The separate $332$ MeV input from the $B_s$ sector fixes the $bbs$ partner masses relative to their unknown ground-state mass, conditional on the new beauty-strange state's chiral-partner assignment. We relate the differences of hyperfine splittings in the two parity sectors of mesons and baryons, and identify equal reduced single-pion widths, an E1 reduced-width ratio $1:8:4:5$, and a kaon selection rule whose leading predictions contain no new transition parameters. Absolute widths are estimated by matching the published Bardeen--Eichten--Hill and Harada--Rho--Sasaki meson calculations to a common convention. In the reference calculation, all members of the predicted $ccs$ and $bbs$ multiplets are narrow. In the proposed $Ω_{QQ}π^0$ and $Ω_{QQ}γ$ searches at LHCb, these states are expected to produce peaks dominated by detector resolution, allowing their masses and decay patterns to be investigated even when their intrinsic widths remain unresolved. The upper charm state is above kaon threshold but protected in the spectator limit.

</details>


### [106] [Invisible Decays of the $Z$ in Portal Matter Models at Tera-Z](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16843)
*Thomas G. Rizzo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Z玻色子不可见宽度变化ΔΓ_inv对暗物质模型的约束，重点分析了矢量门户（vector portal）框架下门户物质（PM）场的圈图贡献，发现其在满足当前实验限制的同时可能被未来Tera-Z实验探测到；同时指出树级暗希格斯混合、Z与暗光子的质量/动能混合等机制产生的ΔΓ_inv太小而不可观测，而含非零弱同位旋分量的暗希格斯模型则已违反现有约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Z玻色子不可见宽度变化ΔΓ_inv来间接探测未知暗区新物理，并评估其对暗区模型（特别是矢量门户和门户物质模型）的约束能力。

Method: 在简化版E6类模型框架下，计算门户物质（矢量型费米子）圈图对ΔΓ_inv的贡献；对比分析树级暗希格斯混合、Z-暗光子混合及动能混合等机制的贡献；结合LHC对希格斯不可见分支比的限制和LEP对ΔΓ_inv的测量精度进行模型检验。

Result: 门户物质圈图可在满足当前ΔΓ_inv实验上限（~2 MeV）前提下产生可观测信号（Tera-Z灵敏度达~0.1 MeV）；树级暗希格斯混合及Z-暗光子相关机制贡献过小；含T3≠0暗希格斯成分的模型已超出当前ΔΓ_inv约束。

Conclusion: 未来高精度ΔΓ_inv测量（如Tera-Z）将成为检验暗区模型、尤其是门户物质圈图机制的重要间接探针，可有效区分不同暗区耦合机制并指导模型构建。

Abstract: Searches for new contributions to the invisible width of the $Z$, $ΔΓ_{inv}$, can be used to provide model-dependent constraints on new physics that may arise from an unknown dark sector. In the vector portal setup considered here it is shown that loops of portal matter (PM) fields, having both Standard Model (SM) and dark sector quantum numbers, can lead to new contributions to $ΔΓ_{inv}$ that satisfy current experimental constraints while being simultaneously accessible to measurements at the future Tera-Z. Such PM fields, which here take the form of vector-like fermions, are well known to be able to induce kinetic mixing (KM) between the SM and dark photons (DP) at low energies. This analysis is performed within a simplified version of a previously examined `$E_6-$like' setup that predicts a calculable value for the strength of KM in a phenomenologically interesting range. On the other hand, owing to the constraint on the invisible branching fraction of the Higgs, the tree-level mixing of the SM and a weak isosinglet dark Higgs fields yields a value of $ΔΓ_{inv}$ which is far too small to be observable. A similar result holds for the shift in the $Z$'s invisible width induced by the mass and/or KM of the $Z$ with the light DP which then subsequently decays to dark matter. On the other hand, PM models wherein the light dark Higgs carries a significant admixture of a $T_3\neq 0$ weak isospin component are shown to already violate existing $ΔΓ_{inv}$ constraints in the absence of parameter tuning. Future measurements of $ΔΓ_{inv}$ can thus play an important role in providing indirect constraints on dark sector interactions and PM model building, in particular, on PM loops not necessarily associated with the generation of KM of the DP with the corresponding SM field.

</details>


### [107] [Vortex structures in momentum distributions from multi-pair production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16976)
*Z. L. Li,R. Z. Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在任意偏振的时变均匀电场中，真空产生多对粒子-反粒子时动量分布中的涡旋结构，发现线性偏振下随场强增加出现涡旋-反涡旋对，圆偏振下始终存在中心涡旋，椭圆偏振下可共存离散与中心涡旋，且玻色子和费米子的涡旋拓扑荷规律不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强场QED中多对粒子-反粒子产生过程的涡旋拓扑结构及其潜在调控机制。

Method: 采用Bogoliubov变换方法分析空间均匀、时间变化的任意偏振电场下多对粒子-反粒子产生的动量分布涡旋结构。

Result: 线性偏振下出现涡旋-反涡旋对；圆偏振下始终存在中心涡旋，其拓扑荷对玻色子和费米子有不同整数倍关系；椭圆偏振下离散涡旋与中心涡旋可共存，但费米子中整数倍关系一般不成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为强场QED中多对产生过程的涡旋拓扑提供了理论基础，并揭示了其调控的可能性。

Abstract: The vortex structures in the momentum distributions of multiple particle-antiparticle pairs produced from the vacuum by a spatially uniform, time-varying electric field with arbitrary polarization are investigated using the Bogoliubov transformation. For linearly polarized fields, as the field strength increases, the nodal lines in the multi-pair momentum distribution break up into vortex-antivortex pairs in both the bosonic and fermionic cases. Each vortex carries a topological charge that is an integer multiple of the single-pair vortex topological charge. For circular polarization, a central vortex appears at all field strengths. For bosons, its topological charge is an integer multiple of the single-pair vortex topological charge; for fermions, it is an integer multiple of the vortex topological charge for single-pair production with zero total spin projection along the field propagation direction. For elliptical polarization with suitable ellipticity, discrete vortices coexist with a central vortex. In this case, however, the integer-multiple relation for fermions generally fails because the two-pair production amplitude is a determinant and spin-channel contributions are separated in momentum space. These results provide a theoretical basis for understanding the vortex topology of multi-pair production in strong-field QED and for its potential control.

</details>


### [108] [Cosmic Colliders in Binary Blazars Probing Ultra-High-Energy Particle Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17033)
*Giorgio Galanti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出双喷流耀变体可作为宇宙级对撞机，实现远超地面加速器的质心能量（达10^10 GeV），用于研究极高能QCD和新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破地面加速器和固定靶实验在质心能量（约10^5 GeV）上的限制，探索更高能标下的强相互作用与新物理现象。

Method: 利用亚秒差距尺度双喷流耀变体中因微小喷流偏角（~1°–6°）导致的共流质子束对撞，构建天然高能“宇宙对撞机”。

Result: 实现质心能量达~10^10 GeV的连续扫描；系统处于光学薄区（τ_pp ≪ 1），可检验渐近自由、胶子饱和与Froissart幺正性；产生PeV–EeV中微子和亚TeV伽马射线，可观测于LHAASO、CTAO、IceCube、GRAND等设备。

Conclusion: 双喷流耀变体是探索终极能量极限物理的独特天体实验室，为超越标准模型的新物理提供高灵敏度探测窗口。

Abstract: While terrestrial colliders and fixed-target cosmic-ray interactions are bounded at center-of-mass (CM) energies $\sqrt{s} \lesssim \mathcal{O}(10^5)\,\text{GeV}$, we demonstrate that bound sub-parsec binary blazars form broad-band, beam-beam hadronic ``Cosmic Colliders'', overcoming such limitation. Driven by modest jet misalignments ($θ_{\rm mis} \sim 1^\circ - 6^\circ$), co-streaming proton beams collide at CM energies reaching $\sqrt{s} \sim 10^{10}\,\text{GeV}$ - six orders of magnitude above the Large Hadron Collider. Operating in a strictly optically thin regime ($τ_{pp} \ll 1$), the system performs a continuous CM energy scan, probing asymptotic QCD scaling, gluon saturation, and Froissart unitarity, while opening a possible high-sensitivity window into physics beyond the Standard Model. Secondary PeV-EeV neutrinos and sub-TeV $γ$-rays fall directly within reach of current and planned observatories such as LHAASO, CTAO, IceCube, and GRAND, establishing binary blazars as laboratories for physics at the ultimate energy limit.

</details>


### [109] [Charged Lepton Flavor Violation: A Theory Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17134)
*P. S. Bhupal Dev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了带电轻子味违反（cLFV）作为探索超出标准模型新物理的最干净探针之一的重要意义，并总结了当前实验进展及不同测量手段对新物理机制的诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电轻子味违反是探测超出标准模型新物理的极敏感且本底干净的探针，理论和实验亟需系统性梳理以指导未来研究方向。

Method: 采用理论导向的综述方法，整合分析现有cLFV理论框架与各类实验测量结果，探讨其对新物理模型的区分能力。

Result: 厘清了cLFV在不同新物理场景（如SUSY、跷跷板机制、Leptoquarks等）中的特征信号，并指出关键实验通道（如μ→eγ、μ→3e、μ-e转换等）的诊断价值。

Conclusion: cLFV研究正处于黄金时期，理论与实验协同将极大提升对新物理能量尺度和耦合结构的约束能力。

Abstract: We present a theory-oriented overview of the significance of charged-lepton flavor violation -- one of the cleanest windows onto physics beyond the Standard Model. We also summarize the current experimental landscape and illustrate how different measurements can diagnose the underlying new physics.

</details>


### [110] [MadNIS, Ready for Production at LO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17177)
*Theo Heimel,Olivier Mattelaer,Daniele Massaro,Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了MadNIS中的神经多通道重要性采样方法，应用于完整的质子-质子对撞过程，并在多种LHC物理过程中实现了最高达250倍的事件生成加速，且无需针对具体过程调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升高能物理中蒙特卡洛事件生成的效率，尤其在复杂末态和高多重数情况下降低计算成本。

Method: 将神经多通道重要性采样（Neural Multi-Channel Importance Sampling）集成到MadGraph的生产级事件生成框架中，支持全pp过程的相空间积分与事件采样。

Result: 在多喷注、矢量玻色子、顶夸克及希格斯粒子产生等广泛LHC过程中，实现最高250倍的事件生成加速；量化了加速比与训练时间、末态多重数的关系，并确定了训练开销的摊销点。

Conclusion: MadNIS展现出强泛化能力与实用性，将成为MadGraph中领头阶相空间积分与事件生成的默认方法。

Abstract: We extend neural multi-channel importance sampling in MadNIS to full proton-proton processes in a production-ready event-generation setup. We benchmark a wide range of LHC processes, including multi-jet, vector-boson, top-quark, and Higgs production, and obtain event-generation speed-ups of up to a factor of 250 without process-specific hyperparameter tuning. We quantify how the speed-up scales with training time and final-state multiplicity, and when the training cost amortizes. MadNIS will become the default for phase-space integration and event generation at leading order in MadGraph.

</details>


### [111] [Implications for new physics solutions to the LSND and MiniBooNE anomalies after MicroBooNE results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17207)
*Waleed Abdallah,Raj Gandhi,Samiran Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了MicroBooNE实验对LSND和MiniBooNE中电子类事例超出的检验结果，评估了多种理论模型（如暗中介子、暗重轻子等）的可行性，提出一种满足当前约束的轻赝标量暗中介子模型，并探讨其与中微子狄拉克性质、CP破坏及紫外完备性的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LSND和MiniBooNE实验中长期存在的电子类事例超出，厘清其是否源于光子、电子或正负电子对，并检验新物理模型能否在MicroBooNE最新结果下依然成立。

Method: 系统分析MicroBooNE针对光子、电子和e+e−信号的多通道搜寻结果，结合标准模型背景与各类新物理模型（尤其是暗中介子与暗重轻子模型）的预言，进行理论兼容性评估，并构造一个满足所有现有约束的新模型。

Result: 多数原有新物理解释被MicroBooNE结果排除或严重受限；提出一个质量≤30 MeV、赝标耦合主导、衰变至e+e−的暗中性标量中介子模型可存活；该模型暗示中微子为狄拉克粒子，并预言O(100) MeV量级的暗重轻子；指出CP破坏与紫外完备性需进一步研究；建议在MicroBooNE/SBND中开展非相干散射道搜寻。

Conclusion: LSND/MiniBooNE异常尚未被完全排除，但参数空间大幅压缩；轻赝标暗中介子模型是当前数据下有希望的候选者；未来非相干散射测量对最终判别至关重要；该框架可能连接中微子质量起源与暗物质物理。

Abstract: Over the past couple of decades, the Liquid Scintillator Neutrino Detector (LSND) and MiniBooNE (MB) neutrino experiments have provided statistically significant evidence of persistent and as yet unexplained electron-like event excesses. The MicroBooNE experiment, with sensitive particle identification capabilities designed to shed light on these anomalous results, has conducted a number of analyses to test whether the excess is due to photons, electrons or $e^+e^-$ pair productions, all of which give almost identical signals in LSND and MB. Recent MicroBooNE searches, in addition to checking Standard Model (SM) processes, have focused on proposed new physics scenarios involving new mediators and dark portals beyond the SM. We study the consequences and effects of these analyses for the many theoretical proposals put forth to generate the three types of signals above in order to understand the observed excesses. We discuss which classes of solutions are allowed, disallowed or require further scrutiny involving theoretical and experimental efforts. We give an example of a new solution that survives the restrictions imposed by MicroBooNE results and other existing bounds. We point out that a light ($m\leq 30$ MeV) dark neutral spin zero mediator with dominantly pseudoscalar couplings, decaying to an $e^+e^-$ pair, can account for the observations. We indicate that if such a model is realised, it may point to the possibility that neutrinos are Dirac particles, and that there are dark Heavy Neutral Leptons (HNLs) of mass ${\cal O}(100)$ MeV. We discuss issues related to CP violation and ultra-violet completion of these models. Finally we stress that a search for the excess in incoherent scattering channels in MicroBooNE and SBND could help clarify the physics behind these persistent anomalies.

</details>


### [112] [Revisiting Critical Higgs Inflation: Electroweak Couplings, CMB Observables, and Perturbative Unitarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17211)
*Sarah R. Geller,Sol Gutierrez-Lara,David I. Kaiser,Thomas Steingasser*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了临界希格斯暴胀模型，系统评估其在电弱尺度测量、CMB观测符合性及紫外敏感性三方面的可行性，发现无法同时满足全部三个物理判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验临界希格斯暴胀模型在当前理论与观测约束下的整体自洽性，特别是其对未知紫外物理的敏感性问题。

Method: 系统分析希格斯势在暴胀能标下的参数行为，结合电弱精度测量、CMB观测量（如ns、r）约束，并评估度规与Palatini引力框架下微扰幺正性的破缺尺度。

Result: 虽可通过调节非最小耦合满足CMB观测，但不存在同时满足电弱测量、CMB数据和微扰幺正性（即紫外不敏感）的参数空间。

Conclusion: 临界希格斯暴胀模型无法在免紫外依赖的前提下自洽实现；其可观测量预测原则上依赖于紫外完备理论。

Abstract: We revisit the scenario of critical Higgs inflation, in which inflation is driven by the Standard Model Higgs boson with parameters chosen close to a critical point, giving rise to an inflection point in its potential. After reviewing this model, we perform a systematic study of its viability centered around three criteria: (1) consistency with electroweak-scale measurements, (2) ability to reproduce the cosmic microwave background (CMB) observables, and (3) sensitivity of the model predictions to unknown UV physics, based on a breakdown of perturbative unitarity. We first show that compliance with electroweak-scale measurements is controlled by the parameters in the Higgs potential at inflationary scales, also updating the literature values. We then demonstrate how for any choice of potential parameters, agreement with the CMB observables can be achieved through the choice of the nonminimal coupling, in both metric and Palatini formulations of gravity. However, we also find that there exist no regions of parameter space, in either the metric or Palatini formulations, in which compliance with all three physical criteria is possible. While this does not exclude the possibility of the Higgs field driving inflation, it means that predictions for any observable are in principle sensitive to the details of the model's UV completion.

</details>


### [113] [What Do Event Generators Compute? Flux Functionals from Detector Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17213)
*Jack Holguin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于探测器算符和渐近强子态的非微扰方法，直接构建强子通量的概率分布，并将其编码为事件生成器源泛函；该泛函自然包含强子化效应，且其限制到能量流时给出红外与共线安全的喷注源泛函，可用于精确评估部分子喷注精度；一阶计算揭示其对运动学反冲及喷注反冲方案的敏感性，并指出传统因子化无法完全重求和其多尺度对数；该算符框架还可推广至N=4超杨-米尔斯等非QCD理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件生成器以不可观的部分子末态（含模型化的强子化）为精度基础，而物理预测实际针对可观测的强子通量；因此需建立直接面向探测器测量、内建强子化效应的非微扰理论框架。

Method: 基于探测器算符作用于渐近强子态，构造强子通量的概率分布并定义为事件生成器源泛函；将其限制到能量流得到部分子喷注源泛函；在一阶微扰下计算其对数结构，分析其对反冲方案与低能标连续谱的依赖；探讨其在非QCD理论（如N=4 SYM）中的可推广性。

Result: 建立了首个直接面向探测器测量的非微扰事件生成器源泛函；导出红外/共线安全的喷注源泛函；发现其一阶对数结构显式依赖喷注反冲方案与方向相关的连续低能标；证明传统高能标驱动的因子化无法完全重求和全部对数；展示了向共形场论推广的可能性。

Conclusion: 事件生成器的精度基准应从部分子末态转向可观测的强子通量；所提出的算符泛函方法不仅为QCD喷注精度评估提供了新标准，也为在更广泛理论中探索非微扰事件生成开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Every physical prediction of an event generator is a statement about the hadronic fluxes that can be recorded by particle detectors. By contrast, the formal backbone of modern event-generator precision is provided by the unobservable partonic final states of the parton shower, with hadronisation included as a model. We construct the probability distribution of the hadronic fluxes non-perturbatively, directly from detector operators acting on asymptotic hadronic states, and encode it in an event generator source functional. This distribution contains every measurement a detector can make, and is therefore what a generator must compute with precision, including the effects of hadronisation. Restricting the functional to the energy flow yields the parton shower source functional, which is infrared and collinear safe and contains every calorimetric observable, making it a natural object against which to assess parton-shower accuracy. We compute its logarithmic structure at one loop in $e^+e^-$ annihilation, finding it directly sensitive to kinematic recoil and hence to the recoil scheme of a parton shower. The parton shower functional depends on a continuum of low scales, one for each resolved direction, and in a toy model its logarithms cannot be entirely resummed within a factorisation whose evolution derives only from the high-scale physics. Our operator-based formulation opens the door to the study of event-generator-like systems in theories beyond QCD, including conformal theories such as $N=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory, and thereby offers a new route to non-perturbative insight into event generation.

</details>


### [114] [Neutrino Tridents and $W$-boson production at the Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17263)
*Felix Kling,Toni Mäkelä,Sebastian Trojanowski,Bei Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了缪子对撞机作为高能中微子源的潜力，重点研究了中微子三重态（trident）和共振W玻色子产生过程的测量可行性，并预测在十年内可在百克级硅探测器中获得超百万量级事例，实现首次观测并提升新物理与核子结构约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用缪子对撞机产生的极高通量多TeV中微子，开展传统实验难以达到统计精度的稀有中微子过程研究，拓展中微子物理与强子结构探测前沿。

Method: 基于缪子对撞机中微子束流特性，结合硅基活性靶探测器模型，模拟并估算中微子三重态与阈值附近W玻色子产生事例率；分析背景抑制策略与关键探测器性能要求。

Result: 预测100 kg硅探测器在十年运行中可观测超10^6个中微子三重态事件和约10^5个W玻色子事件；该测量可超越DUNE对新型矢量/轴矢量相互作用的灵敏度，并以前所未有的精度约束高动量分数x区域的光子分布。

Conclusion: 缪子对撞机中微子实验为稀有中微子过程的首次高统计测量提供了独特平台，兼具新物理探针与核子结构精密检验双重价值，需发展低质量、高分辨率、强本底抑制能力的专用探测器。

Abstract: Muon colliders are exceptionally intense sources of multi-TeV neutrinos, allowing even relatively small detectors to record large rates of neutrino interactions. In addition to serving as neutrino-ion collider for precision measurements of neutrino deep inelastic scattering, this setup allow to study even rare neutrino processes with at high statistics. In this study, we investigate the feasibility of measuring neutrino trident and on-shell W-boson production, predicting over one million trident events and one hundred thousand W-boson events in a 100 kg silicon-based active target over ten years and allowing to detect these processes for the first time. We further show that these measurements could constrain new vector and axial-vector interactions beyond projected DUNE sensitivities and provide unprecedented constraints on the high-x photon distribution through W-boson production near threshold. We also discuss strategies for signal-background discrimination and the resulting detector requirements.

</details>


### [115] [Non-accumulation of background-enhanced neutrino forces in proto-neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17346)
*Himanshu Bora,Anupam Deka,Debajyoti Dutta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了原中子星（PNS）中中微子背景下的两中微子交换势对宏观哈特里（Hartree）效应的影响，发现尽管成对势在方向性背景下被显著增强，但由于角向和振荡结构的核函数抑制，其宏观哈特里贡献并不随系统尺寸增长，因此对PNS状态方程或脉冲星踢等现象无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在原中子星这种极高密度中微子环境中，真空两中微子交换势是否能产生可观测的宏观哈特里效应，从而影响天体物理过程如脉冲星踢或状态方程。

Method: 理论分析与平均场近似：对自旋无关、自旋依赖（宇称守恒与不守恒）项分别进行角度平均与背景投影，考察其在PNS典型条件下的宏观行为。

Result: 自旋无关部分因角度平均而投影至单极矩，哈特里贡献饱和而不随系统尺度增长；自旋依赖项在PNS平均场下亦不呈现宏观增长；整体效应被核函数的角向与振荡结构抑制，而非屏蔽机制。

Conclusion: 在标准模型框架下，该两中微子交换势虽局部增强，但无法产生显著宏观哈特里效应，因此对PNS物态方程及脉冲星踢等现象不重要。

Abstract: In a neutrino background, the vacuum two-neutrino exchange potential $V \propto G_F^2 /r^5$ acts as a much longer-ranged interaction, scaling as $V\sim1/r$ for a directional background. A proto-neutron star (PNS) hosts one of the densest astrophysical neutrino environments known, and naturally provides a setting in which to test whether this pairwise enhancement produces a macroscopic Hartree contribution. We show that this does not happen. In the spin-independent sector, averaging over pair orientations projects the background onto its angular monopole. The bulk Hartree contribution then saturates instead of growing with system size. We also find that the spin-dependent parity-conserving and parity-violating terms do not generate macroscopic growth under the PNS mean-field conditions considered here. Thus, although the pair potential can be strongly enhanced, the resulting bulk interaction remains very small. The suppression comes from the angular and oscillatory structure of the kernel, not from screening. Within the Standard Model mechanism considered here, the effect is not important for the PNS equation of state or for events like pulsar kicks.

</details>


### [116] [QGrAT: Quantum Grade-Aware Transformer for Jet Tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17351)
*Pallavi Bhardwaj,Michael Spannowsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合洛伦兹对称性和强子顶夸克衰变三叉结构的量子喷注标记器QGrAT，利用时空Clifford代数分级特征设计洛伦兹不变的成对注意力耦合，并通过辅助量子比特引入显式三粒子相互作用，在Top Tagging基准测试中展现出与经典基线相当且更优的背景抑制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用物理先验（洛伦兹对称性与顶夸克衰变的三叉结构）指导量子机器学习模型设计，提升量子喷注分类器的物理可解释性与性能。

Method: 提出QGrAT——一种基于时空Clifford代数Cℓ(1,3)分级结构的量子Transformer：将四动量构造的几何特征按代数grade组织；grade-specific内积定义洛伦兹不变的成对注意力耦合；引入辅助量子比特编码三粒子Minkowski内积乘积以建模高阶关联；采用可训练电路读出实现分类评分。

Result: 在10个组分/喷注的Top Tagging模拟任务中，QGrAT（12量子比特、144参数）达到AUC=0.944±0.002；在信号效率50%时背景抑制从19.9提升至35.7；加入三粒子辅助比特显著提升性能，而扩展至grade-4特征无一致增益。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了将时空几何嵌入量子注意力机制的可行框架，并证实显式三粒子关联是所测试架构中提升背景抑制能力的关键因素。

Abstract: Lorentz symmetry and the three-prong structure of hadronic top decays provide physical guidance for designing compact quantum jet taggers. We introduce QGrAT (Quantum Grade-Aware Transformer), a quantum transformer that incorporates these structures into its interactions between constituent qubits. Geometric features constructed from the constituent four-momenta are organised by the grades of the spacetime Clifford algebra $C\ell(1,3)$, and their grade-specific inner products determine the pairwise attention couplings. For fixed constituents and co-transforming reference vectors, these couplings are Lorentz invariant, although the component-wise input encoding makes the complete classifier frame dependent. An auxiliary qubit introduces an explicit three-particle interaction through products of pairwise Minkowski inner products, while a trainable in-circuit readout converts the resulting quantum state into a classification score. We evaluate the architecture in state-vector simulation on the Top Tagging benchmarks. With ten constituents per jet, the configuration using grades $0\text{-}3$ and the three-particle ancilla requires twelve qubits and $144$ trainable parameters, reaching AUC $0.944 \pm 0.002$ and background rejection $1/\varepsilon_B = 35.7 \pm 2.4\ (96.8 \pm 7.7)$ at signal efficiency $\varepsilon_S = 0.50\ (0.30)$. This performance is competitive with the L-GATr-Slim baseline at matched constituent and parameter budgets. Adding the three-particle ancilla raises background rejection at $50\%$ signal efficiency from $19.9$ to $35.7$, whereas extending the representation to the tested grade-$4$ feature gives no consistent improvement. The study establishes a framework for incorporating spacetime geometry into quantum attention and identifies explicit three-particle correlations as the principal source of improved background rejection among the architectural variations tested.

</details>


### [117] [Parton and nuclear dynamics at large $x$: the CJ26 global QCD analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17485)
*Alberto Accardi,Matteo Cerutti,Cynthia E. Keppel,Shujie Li,J. F. Owens,Sanghwa Park,Peter Risse*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: CJ26分析首次将JLab 6 GeV全部DIS数据和首批12 GeV数据纳入全球QCD拟合，同时提取质子部分子分布函数（PDF）与氘核核效应，并重点处理大-x区的高阶修正与离壳效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升大-x区域核效应与部分子分布的联合精度，解决低能质子/氘核数据中高阶效应与离壳修正的耦合问题，并量化实验相关系统误差以提高拟合可靠性。

Method: 全局QCD拟合方法，同时拟合质子PDF与氘核核效应；首次纳入JLab 6 GeV全部DIS及首批12 GeV数据；显式建模高阶扭度效应与离壳修正；严格处理实验相关系统误差。

Result: 获得NLO精度的PDF（LHAPDF格式公开），配套计算了含高阶扭度与离壳修正的结构函数；明确了高阶效应与离壳效应对夸克/胶子分布的影响；揭示了相关系统误差对拟合精度的关键作用。

Conclusion: CJ26为理解轻核中的核与部分子动力学提供了更可靠基准；强调未来JLab更高精度数据对解耦核效应与部分子结构的重要性；所发布PDF与结构函数可供广泛使用。

Abstract: We present the new CTEQ-JLab global QCD analysis, called CJ26, that simultaneously fits parton distribution functions of the proton and nuclear effects in deuteron targets. This work includes, for the first time in a global fit, the full set of JLab 6 GeV inclusive DIS measurements and the first published JLab 12 GeV data. The analysis focuses on the large-$x$ region, and carefully treats the interplay between higher-twist effects and off-shell corrections in low-energy proton and deuteron target data. We discuss their effect on quark and gluon distributions, and highlight the importance of quantifying the experimental correlated systematic uncertainty to achieve fits of the highest accuracy. We also outline how upcoming data may help unravel the nuclear and partonic dynamics in light nuclei. The extracted next-to-leading order parton distributions are available on the CJ web pages in LHAPDF format for general use, accompanied by leading- and higher-twist structure functions calculated with the simultaneously fitted PDFs, higher-twist corrections, and off-shell corrections. This write up clarifies several points that became clearer while finalizing the CJ26 paper and can be read as a companion to the latter.

</details>


### [118] [Kernel moments and thermodynamic stability in nonlocal Nambu--Jona-Lasinio models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17622)
*Xiaozhu Yu,Xinyang Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在零化学势下协变双味非局域Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型中的热力学一致性与稳定性，提出了一种基于欧几里得核矩的熵符号判据，并构造了一个满足全温度正熵与正热容的四次指数核实例，表明热正定性与静态状态方程斜率测试可分离；结果主要用于诊断平均场泛函，而非优选具体核形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验非局域NJL模型在热力学上的自洽性与稳定性，特别是熵和热容的正定性，以及其与静态物态方程斜率的关系。

Method: 采用齐次夸克平均场近似，在零化学势下开展协变双味非局域NJL模型分析；引入受控的大源展开，导出仅依赖欧几里得核矩的熵符号判据；通过轻源拟合π介子观测量与减去的凝聚态，对不同核形式进行数值拟合与热力学性质检验。

Result: 发现所有允许的正高斯混合核在足够大的源质量下均出现负总熵；但某一特定四次指数核在所有T>0下保持正熵与正热容，且dP/dε=s/C_V在某有限温区内大于1；数值重拟合显示该增强效应随凝聚态输入减弱，且存在反例排除所选匹配域内热正定性的普适成立。

Conclusion: 所得结果是对平均场泛函的诊断工具，而非支持某一特定核形式；热正定性不能由静态物态方程斜率唯一判定；忽略热介子效应可能影响低温斜率检验，且静态导数不决定实时激波传播速度。

Abstract: We study thermodynamic consistency and stability in a covariant two-flavor nonlocal Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model at zero chemical potential in the homogeneous quark mean-field approximation. A controlled large-source expansion gives a sufficient entropy-sign criterion directly from Euclidean kernel moments, without locating propagator poles. At fixed couplings and kernel, every admissible positive Gaussian mixture develops negative total entropy at sufficiently large source mass for each fixed positive temperature. In a separate light-source fit to pion observables and a subtracted condensate, one quartic-exponent kernel has positive entropy and heat capacity for all $T>0$, while $dP/dε=s/C_V$ exceeds unity on a finite interval. This constructive example separates thermal positivity from the static EOS slope test. Numerical refits show that the enhancement weakens with the condensate input, and a certified counterexample excludes uniform thermal positivity over the adopted matching domain. The results provide diagnostics of the mean-field functional, rather than a preferred phenomenological kernel. Omitted thermal mesons can change the low-temperature slope test, and the static derivative does not determine a real-time front velocity.

</details>


### [119] [Octupole parton structure of spin-3/2 targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17624)
*Brandon B. Le,Dustin Keller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了自旋-3/2靶中胶子横动量依赖分布（TMD）的完整领阶基，包含32个独立系数，其中14个描述胶子数、螺旋度和线性偏振的八极矩依赖；提出在重夸克对轻子产生过程中通过对比相反八极矩靶态来分离八极矩贡献，并推导了半包容深度非弹性散射与Drell-Yan过程中的夸克响应；验证了时间反演关系在对称性保持重整化与演化下的有效性，并将该框架联系到极化^7Li核的自旋-轨道关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自旋-3/2靶中，八极矩极化可揭示超出矢量极化与四极矩排列所能探测的夸克与胶子关联，亟需建立系统理论框架以提取并分离此类高阶极化效应。

Method: 构建领阶胶子横动量依赖分布（TMD）的完备基；结合重夸克对轻子产生、半包容深度非弹性散射与Drell-Yan等过程，利用靶八极矩翻转、束流螺旋度反转及联合角投影技术分离十四种胶子八极矩响应；分析时间反演对称性、因子化及核物理对应。

Result: 获得含32个独立系数的胶子TMD完备基，其中14个明确对应八极矩；给出各过程中八极矩响应的可观测量提取方案；证明时间反演关系在对称性保持的重整化与演化下仍成立；建立该框架与极化^7Li核中自旋-轨道关联的联系。

Conclusion: 胶子八极矩TMD为探索强子内部高阶自旋-动量关联提供了新探针；所构建的理论框架兼具普适性与可测性，为未来极化自旋-3/2靶实验（如电子-离子对撞机EIC）提供了关键理论支撑。

Abstract: In spin-$3/2$ targets, octupole polarization can reveal quark and gluon correlations beyond those probed by vector polarization and quadrupole alignment. Here, we construct the complete leading-twist gluon transverse-momentum-dependent distribution basis, with 32 independent coefficients for each specified Wilson-line and color structure. Fourteen of these describe octupole dependence in gluon number, helicity, and linear polarization. In heavy-quark-pair leptoproduction at small, nonzero pair imbalance, comparing target states with opposite octupoles and identical lower moments isolates the octupole contribution. Beam-helicity reversal and joint angular projections in target orientation and pair-imbalance azimuth can then separate all fourteen gluon octupole responses at leading-power Born order. We also derive the incoming-target quark responses in semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering and Drell-Yan production. Time-reversal relations between distributions remain valid under symmetry-preserving renormalization and evolution, while predictions across processes require factorization formulas connecting those distributions to scattering observables. Finally, we connect this framework to polarized $^{7}$Li through its collinear limits and nuclear spin-orbital correlations.

</details>


### [120] [A mechanism of the power-counting breaking in chiral perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17721)
*Ming-Ze Ma,Shao-Zhou Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了πK散射长度在手征微扰论中O(p^6)阶修正异常大的问题，发现其源于两圈图（尤其是sunset和顶点积分）对手征幂计数规则的显著违反，且πK系统低对称性及较大kaon质量加剧了该现象；相比之下，ππ散射因更高对称性而收敛更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决πK散射长度在手征展开中O(p^6)阶修正远大于O(p^4)阶、破坏手征幂计数收敛性的疑难问题。

Method: 详细分解πK散射振幅中树图、单圈图和两圈图的贡献，重点分析O(p^6)阶可约两圈积分、sunset积分和顶点积分对手征幂计数的偏离，并与ππ散射对比，考察对称性与kaon质量的影响。

Result: O(p^6)阶sunset和顶点积分显著违反幂计数规则，数值上虽部分抵消但仍残留大贡献；πK系统低对称性是主因，kaon质量影响较小；ππ散射虽也存在类似违反，但幅度小得多。

Conclusion: 手征展开在低对称性、重介子参与的过程中可能因多圈低对称图贡献显著而失效，未来需重视此类不可忽略的高阶图效应。

Abstract: Chiral perturbation theory is a power tool for studying the low-energy strong interactions. In the conventional chiral expansion, according to the power counting rule, the higher-order corrections for physical observables are expected to decrease order by order as the chiral dimension increases. However, most previous study of $πK$ scattering lengths by different references showed that the $\mathcal{O}(p^6)$ correction to the scattering lengths is anomalously large and even exceeds the $\mathcal{O}(p^4)$ one. It poses a serious challenge to the convergence of chiral expansion. In order to explore the underlying origin of this phenomenon, the contributions of two-loop diagrams, one-loop diagrams and tree diagrams for the $πK$ scattering amplitude are detailed analyzed. The reducible two-loop integrals, sunset integrals and vertex integrals from the $\mathcal{O}(p^6)$ order all violate the power counting rule. The sunset and vertex integrals can be largely canceled numerically, but not enough. Furthermore, $πK$ scattering is compared with $ππ$ scattering, which seems not violate the power counting rule. The analysis indicates that $ππ$ scattering contains a higher symmetry, although each $\mathcal{O}(p^6)$-order part also violates the power counting rule, but the violation is much smaller than the one from $πK$ scattering. Large kaon mass also makes a contribution to the power-counting breaking, but it is not a major origin. Hence, it needs to pay more attention to the many-loop low-symmetry diagrams in future work. These diagrams may give large contribution and cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [121] [Heavy flavour production in the presence of a weak magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17787)
*Sudeep Saha,Sandeep Chatterjee,Ujjal Kumar Dey,Snigdh Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在弱外磁场下，利用Schwinger固有时间方法计算了胶子融合产生重夸克对过程的截面至(eB)^2阶解析表达式，发现磁场导致截面各向异性并具有cos2θ和cos4θ角依赖，且不对称性在阈值附近最强、随质心能量增加而减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究相对论重离子碰撞初期强磁场对重味夸克产生过程的影响，寻找其在末态可观测量中的可计算印记。

Method: 采用Schwinger固有时间方法对费米子传播子做弱场展开，并在m_Q^2 ≫ eB尺度层次下忽略磁场对外线夸克自旋态的影响，计算gg→Q Q̄过程至(eB)^2阶的微分截面。

Result: 得到各向异性的微分截面，含cos2θ和cos4θ角依赖；截面在胶子沿磁场方向（θ=0,π）时最大、垂直时（θ=π/2）最小；产生的不对称性峰值位于产生阈值附近，随质心能量升高按(eB)^2/m_Q^4衰减。

Conclusion: 该结果为重离子碰撞中初态磁场提供了直接、可计算的理论信号，有望通过末态重味观测量加以验证。

Abstract: We calculate analytic expression for the cross section up to order $(eB)^2$ for the production of a heavy quark $(Q)$ of mass $m_Q$ through the gluon $(g)$ fusion process $g+g\to Q+\bar{Q}$ in the presence of a constant weak external magnetic field $(B)$ with strength $eB \ll m_Q^2$. This is done using the weak-field expansion of the fermion propagator calculated within Schwinger's proper-time formalism. Owing to the hierarchy of scales ($m_Q^2 \gg eB$), we neglect the effects of the magnetic field on the quark and antiquark spinors in the external lines. Since the magnetic field breaks rotational invariance, the cross section becomes anisotropic and depends on the relative orientation angle $(θ)$ between the magnetic field and the incoming gluons in the center-of-mass frame. The lowest-order magnetic correction to the vacuum cross section scales as $(eB)^2$ and is found to have $\cos 2θ$ and $\cos 4θ$ dependence, reflecting the breaking of rotational symmetry by the magnetic field. The cross section is maximized when the gluons propagate along the field direction ($θ= 0, π$) and minimized in the perpendicular configuration ($θ= π/2$). The resulting asymmetry peaks near the production threshold and falls with increasing center-of-mass energy, scaling as $(eB)^2/m_Q^4$ with the quark mass. These results provide direct, calculable signatures of the initial-stage magnetic field in relativistic heavy-ion collisions which can, in principle, be imprinted on heavy-flavour observables measured in the final state of heavy-ion collisions.

</details>


### [122] [One-loop contributing for $A \rightarrow Z Z γ$ in the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model with Vector-Like Quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17801)
*Dzung Tri Tran,Vo Quoc Phong,Khiem Hong Phan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次计算了双Higgs二重态模型及其含矢量样夸克扩展模型中A→ZZγ单圈衰变的偏衰变宽度和分支比，发现分支比可达10^-5至10^-4，矢量样夸克贡献约5%，未来高亮度多TeV缪子对撞机有望观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理信号，特别是双Higgs二重态模型及引入矢量样夸克后的A→ZZγ稀有衰变过程，以检验模型可检验性。

Method: 采用单圈费曼图计算方法，分别计算无极化和极化末态Z玻色子的偏衰变宽度与分支比，并系统分析矢量样夸克在圈图中的贡献。

Result: 在模型可行参数空间内多个基准点上，A→ZZγ分支比达10^-5–10^-4量级；矢量样夸克使总衰变率改变约5%；该效应在高亮度多TeV缪子对撞机上可能可观测。

Conclusion: A→ZZγ衰变是探查双Higgs二重态模型及矢量样夸克新物理的重要探针，其可观测性为未来缪子对撞机实验提供了明确物理目标。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the first results for the one-loop decay $A \rightarrow ZZγ$ in the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model and its extension with Vector-Like Quarks. We calculate the one-loop partial decay widths and branching ratios for both unpolarized and polarized final-state $Z$ bosons. The effects of Vector-Like Quarks propagating in the loop are also examined in our phenomenological analysis. We find that the branching ratios can reach $10^{-5}-10^{-4}$ for several benchmark points within the viable parameter space of the considered models. The contributions from Vector-Like Quarks can modify the total rates by approximately $5\%$. With the high luminosities expected at future multi-TeV muon colliders, these effects may be experimentally accessible.

</details>


### [123] [Covariant approach to allowed beta decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18062)
*Sergio Cruz-Alzaga,Adam Falkowski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种洛伦兹协变的核β衰变壳内形式理论，系统地以反冲动量为小参数展开核矩阵元，并用小群协变结构描述核自旋和极化依赖性；构建了标准与新物理贡献的一阶反冲精度下的最一般允许跃迁流，并导出由洛伦兹不变形状因子表征的微分衰变分布；该框架可自然推广至高阶反冲、辐射修正及禁戒跃迁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一个统一、洛伦兹协变且系统可扩展的核β衰变理论框架，弥合传统核物理、核子级与夸克级有效场论之间的表述差异，并为探索新物理提供严格基准。

Method: 基于洛伦兹协变的壳内（on-shell）有效场论方法，以核反冲动量为展开参数，引入小群（little group）协变结构刻画自旋与极化依赖；构造包含标准模型与可能的 exotic 项的一阶反冲精度下最一般允许β衰变流；推导洛伦兹不变的微分衰变率表达式。

Result: 获得一套完整的洛伦兹不变形状因子，精确描述允许β衰变的微分分布；在母核静止系中，将新形式论参数与传统β衰变文献及核子/夸克有效场论中的参数一一对应；证明该形式论天然兼容高阶修正与扩展。

Conclusion: 所提出的洛伦兹协变壳内形式论为核β衰变提供了系统、普适且可扩展的理论基础，不仅统一了不同尺度下的描述，也为高精度检验标准模型和探测新物理提供了坚实工具。

Abstract: We propose a Lorentz-covariant on-shell formalism for allowed nuclear beta decay. Nuclear matrix elements are organized in a systematic effective field theory expansion in the recoil momentum, with the dependence on nuclear spin and polarization encoded in little-group-covariant structures. In this language, we construct the most general standard and exotic currents for allowed transitions through first recoil order. We then derive the differential decay distribution encoded in a set of Lorentz-invariant form factors. Specializing to the rest frame of the parent nucleus, we relate our parameters to those used in the traditional beta-decay literature and in nucleon- and quark-level effective field theories. The formalism provides a natural framework for extensions to higher recoil orders, radiative corrections, and forbidden transitions.

</details>


### [124] [Color Lorentz force on a transversely polarized quark in the nucleon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18063)
*Ziqi Zhang,Chandan Mondal,Siqi Xu,Xingbo Zhao,James P. Vary*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了无极化核子中横向极化夸克所受横向色洛伦兹力的高阶扭度（twist-3）物理量 $e(x)$ 的 $x^2$ 矩，通过非微扰QCD方法构建其广义形状因子，并在横动量空间给出直观图像，揭示Boer-Mulders效应背后的末态相互作用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子色动力学（QCD）中夸克-胶子关联的非微扰机制，特别是与横向自旋相关的高扭度分布函数 $e(x)$ 所蕴含的横向色洛伦兹力物理意义。

Method: 研究 $e(x)$ 的 $x^2$ 矩对应的非正向（off-forward）矩阵元，推导其洛伦兹协变的广义形状因子分解；利用Basis Light-Front Quantization（BLFQ）框架下求解光前哈密顿量本征问题所得的光前波函数计算这些形状因子；再对其作傅里叶变换以获得横动量空间（impact-parameter space）中的力分布图像。

Result: 得到了描述横向色力的广义形状因子，尤其是 $\mathscr{F}_2$，其正向极限直接关联扭度-3矩阵元 $e_2$，并被诠释为Boer-Mulders力在横动量空间的表象，从而为空间上理解Boer-Mulders效应的末态相互作用提供了新视角。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了高扭度分布 $e(x)$ 与核子内部横向色力的空间图像之间的定量联系，拓展了广义部分子分布和横动量依赖部分子分布的物理内涵，为实验探测QCD非微扰机制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The $x^2$ moment of the genuine twist-3 component of the unpolarized scalar parton distribution function $e(x)$ is related to the average transverse color Lorentz force experienced by a transversely polarized quark in an unpolarized nucleon. This higher-twist quantity probes quark--gluon correlations and provides unique insight into the nonperturbative dynamics of quantum chromodynamics. For the first time, we study the corresponding off-forward matrix element and derive its Lorentz-covariant decomposition in terms of generalized form factors. We calculate these form factors from light-front wave functions obtained by solving the light-front Hamiltonian eigenvalue problem within the Basis Light-Front Quantization framework. Their Fourier transforms provide a spatial picture of the transverse color forces in impact-parameter space. In particular, $\mathscr{F}_2$, whose forward limit is directly related to the twist-3 matrix element $e_2$, can be interpreted as the impact-parameter-space representation of the Boer--Mulders force, providing an intuitive spatial picture of the final-state interaction mechanism underlying the Boer--Mulders effect.

</details>


### [125] [HEPTABench: A framework for portable and controlled agentic evaluations in high energy physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18454)
*Tony Menzo,Alexander Roman,George T. Fleming,Sergei Gleyzer,Konstantin Matchev,Stephen Mrenna*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了HEPTABench框架，用于高能物理（HEP）领域中对智能体系统进行可移植、受控的基准测试，支持独立配置任务、模型、工具等组件，并通过两轮基准实验验证了领域专用工具对提升数值精度、稳定性和成功率的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在高能物理领域对智能体系统进行可重复、受控的评估，以独立分析模型、工具和运行框架等各组件对性能的影响。

Method: 构建HEPTABench框架，集成HEP基准任务、科学评分标准、实验执行机制与版本化工具接口，强调完整记录实验配置、智能体行为轨迹及产出物，以支持可检验性、可复现性与事后评估。

Result: 在长寿命粒子探测范围计算任务中，引入HEP领域工具后，所有四个编码智能体的数值精度提高、试间离散度降低；在符号学双体衰变任务中，Claude Haiku 4.5的成功率从8%提升至79%，而GPT-5.5仅用内置工具即达100%成功率。

Conclusion: HEPTABench为系统性评估科学智能体提供了标准化框架，证实领域专用工具是提升其准确性与可靠性的重要因素。

Abstract: Quantifying the capability and reliability of agentic systems in high-energy physics (HEP) requires repeated evaluations under controlled configurations. Establishing whether changes to models, tools, or harnesses improve performance also requires these components to be varied independently. We present the High Energy Physics Tool-and-Agent Benchmarking (HEPTABench) framework for portable and controlled evaluations of agentic systems, with separately configurable tasks, scaffolding, models, providers, harnesses, and domain capabilities. The framework combines HEP benchmark tasks and scientific rubrics with experiment execution and versioned tool interfaces. To support inspection, reproducibility, and post-hoc grading, the framework prioritizes recording and reporting complete experiment specifications, agent trajectories, and experiment artifacts. We demonstrate the framework in two benchmark campaigns. The first campaign evaluates a forward long-lived-particle reach calculation across four frontier coding-agent systems, comparing their built-in coding tools with the same tools supplemented by HEP domain tools. We show that including these HEP domain tools improves numerical accuracy and reduces trial-to-trial dispersion for every tested system. In a complementary campaign covering eight symbolic two-body decay tasks, we show that adding symbolic domain tools increases the fraction of successful trials for Claude Haiku 4.5 from $8\%$ to $79\%$, while GPT-5.5 succeeds in every trial with built-in tools alone. Through systematic evaluation of agentic systems, HEPTABench enables the identification of factors that improve the accuracy and reliability of their scientific work.

</details>


### [126] [Deviations to cobimaximal mixing in a 3+1 neutrino scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18487)
*Diana C. Rivera-Agudelo,S. L. Tostado*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在3+1中微子框架下，引入一个惰性中微子如何破坏cobimaximal混合及μ-τ反射对称性，并分析其对中微子振荡参数、CP相位及无中微子双贝塔衰变有效质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cobimaximal混合仍与当前实验数据兼容，但需探究其在扩展中微子模型（如含惰性中微子）下的稳定性与对称性破缺机制。

Method: 基于小对称性破缺近似，在3+1中微子场景中分析惰性中微子对混合角、CP相位及Majorana相位的影响，并结合中微子振荡与无中微子双贝塔衰变实验约束进行参数限制。

Result: 惰性中微子导致cobimaximal混合和μ-τ反射对称性破缺；正常质量序仍可行并可被下一代0νββ实验探测，而倒置序在小破缺范围内被当前实验排除。

Conclusion: 在3+1模型中，cobimaximal混合的小破缺可自洽解释现有数据，但仅正常质量序与当前及未来实验兼容，倒置序被强烈限制。

Abstract: Cobimaximal mixing remains a viable approximate pattern since the predicted atmospheric angle ($π/4$) and Dirac CP phase ($-π/2$) are compatible with current experimental data at the $3σ$ level. In this work, we investigate how an additional sterile neutrino state breaks the cobimaximal mixing and the underlying $μ-τ$ reflection symmetry in a $3+1$ neutrino scenario. Given the strong constraints already imposed on active-sterile mixings, we examine the resulting restrictions on the model parameter space, assuming a small symmetry breaking induced by the sterile state. We derive predictions for the relevant model parameters, active-sterile mixing angles, and Dirac CP-violating phases, by considering small deviations from the symmetric limit. We also analyze the allowed combinations of Majorana phases through their impact on the effective Majorana mass governing neutrinoless double beta decay. Current experimental limits on this process impose severe constraints on the inverted ordering realization of the $3+1$ scenario, excluding the small breaking regime within the parameter ranges considered. In contrast, the normal ordering case remains viable and predicts values of the effective Majorana mass that lie within the sensitivity reach of upcoming neutrinoless double beta decay experiments.

</details>


### [127] [Multiboson production at multi-TeV lepton colliders as complementary probes to rare Higgs decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18703)
*Dario Buttazzo,Keshav Chaudhary,Roberto Franceschini,Arman Korajac*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了极高能轻子对撞机探测影响希格斯玻色子h→γγ和h→Zγ环诱导衰变的有效算符的能力，发现双玻色子和三玻色子末态过程可显著提升对有效场论参数空间的约束，尤其在超高能量下，三玻色子过程因新物理截面随能量增长而成为主导约束，甚至可能媲美FCC-hh。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为弥补大型强子对撞机（LHC）在希格斯耦合测量中的局限，探索极高能轻子对撞机在探测环诱导希格斯衰变相关新物理方面的互补潜力。

Method: 分析极高能轻子对撞机上含两个和三个规范玻色子的末态过程，评估其对影响h→γγ和h→Zγ的有效算符的联合与单独约束能力，并与HL-LHC、FCC-ee及FCC-hh的预期灵敏度进行比较。

Result: 多TeV轻子对撞机可通过双玻色子和特别是三玻色子过程，显著改善对相关有效场论参数的限制，其灵敏度优于HL-LHC和FCC-ee的投影，在极高能量下三玻色子过程的约束力甚至可与FCC-hh竞争。

Conclusion: 极高能轻子对撞机是探测希格斯环诱导衰变中新物理的重要平台，三玻色子过程因其能量依赖性成为超高能区最敏感探针，为未来对撞机设计和新物理搜索提供了关键理论依据。

Abstract: We study the potential of very high-energy lepton colliders to probe effective operators affecting the loop-induced Higgs boson decays $h\to γγ$ and $h\to Zγ$, providing constraints complementary to Higgs coupling measurements at the Large Hadron Collider. In particular, we consider processes with two and three bosons in the final state and analyze their individual and combined impact on the effective field theory parameter space. We demonstrate that these processes at a multi-TeV lepton collider can improve the bounds from rare Higgs decays and electroweak precision observables, surpassing the projections for High-Luminosity LHC and FCC-ee. This is especially true for triboson processes, whose sensitivity is enhanced by the growth with energy of the new physics cross-sections, making them the dominant constraint at very high energies, potentially competitive with FCC-hh.

</details>


### [128] [Virtual photon bremsstrahlung in a magnetized quark-gluon-plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18749)
*Aritra Bandyopadhyay,Krzysztof Redlich,Chihiro Sasaki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在热、磁化的夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）中通过虚夸克轫致辐射产生的双轻子，扩展了Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal（LPM）框架以考虑背景磁场和费米-狄拉克夸克统计；发现非平衡态修正项在全立体角积分后为零，不贡献于双轻子产额；而磁场的主导效应体现在平衡态部分，即通过朗道能级结构影响介质密度与德拜屏蔽质量，从而改变LPM抑制参数；首次确立了LPM机制下磁场对双轻子产额的非零印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强磁场环境下重离子碰撞中双轻子产额的物理机制，特别是LPM效应如何受磁场调制，以理解QGP的电磁性质及早期阶段磁场信号的可观测性。

Method: 扩展LPM框架，纳入外加电磁场下的夸克分布非平衡修正（由弛豫时间近似下的玻尔兹曼方程求解），并结合磁化QGP的朗道能级结构计算介质密度与德拜屏蔽质量；分析非平衡修正对双轻子产额的贡献，并提取磁场在平衡 sector 中的影响。

Result: 非平衡修正项（线性依赖电场）在全立体角积分后严格为零，不贡献于双轻子产额；磁场通过改变平衡态下的介质密度和德拜质量，调制LPM抑制参数，从而在双轻子产额中引入非零、可计算的磁场印记；首次在LPM机制下确立该磁场效应。

Conclusion: 在热磁化QGP中，双轻子产额的磁场信号并非来自非平衡输运效应，而是源于磁场对QGP平衡态性质（如朗道能级导致的密度与屏蔽效应）的调控；这为利用双轻子探针提取重离子碰撞早期强磁场信息提供了新理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate dilepton production via virtual quark bremsstrahlung in a hot, magnetized quark-gluon plasma (QGP), extending the Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal (LPM) framework to account for a background magnetic field and for Fermi-Dirac quark statistics. An external electromagnetic field drives the quark distribution out of equilibrium, and this off-equilibrium correction, obtained from the Boltzmann equation in the relaxation-time approximation, is in principle explicit in the electric and magnetic field strengths. We argue that this correction, being linear in the electric field, vanishes identically upon integration over the full dilepton solid angle and therefore does not contribute to the dilepton production rate (DPR). The leading magnetic-field signature in the rate instead enters entirely through the equilibrium sector: the medium density and Debye screening mass, computed from the Landau-level structure of the magnetized QGP, modifying the LPM suppression parameter. Working in the regime relevant to the hydrodynamic phase of heavy-ion collisions, we present the LPM-induced DPR for quark scatterers and establish, for the first time, a nonzero magnetic-field imprint on this observable through the LPM mechanism.

</details>
