Conclusion: EQG黑洞在等离子体环境中的阴影和弱透镜特征具有可区分性，为未来高精度观测验证量子引力模型提供了潜在途径。

Abstract: In this article, we present an investigation of observational signatures of a black hole (BH) in effective quantum gravity (EQG), particularly gravitational weak lensing and shadow analyses with the assumption that the BH is surrounded by uniform and non-uniform plasma. First, we review the spacetime by comparing it with well-known BH spacetimes such as Schwarzschild and Reissner--Nordström BHs. Subsequently, we explore the effect of the uniform and non-uniform plasma on the BH shadow. The results indicate that shadow radii decrease under the influence of the BH charge and both uniform and non-uniform plasma parameters. In addition, we obtain theoretical constraints on the spacetime parameters using observational data from the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) collaboration. Finally, we study gravitational weak lensing around the BH in EQG surrounded by uniform and non-uniform plasma. It was found that increasing the uniform plasma frequency leads to a rise in the deflection angle and vice versa for the non-uniform plasma.

</details>


### [145] [Analytic Brill wave initial data with trapped surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16773)
*Piotr Koc*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了第一个显式的、实解析的、非平凡的、渐近平坦的真空初始数据族，用于描述纯引力辐射，其共形因子由初等函数和双对数函数的闭式组合给出，并通过超几何方程展开为含Gamma函数系数的级数；该构造精确给出了此前需数值计算的物理量，确立了存在性临界值k_c，并分析了ADM质量与视界面形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自1959年Brill工作以来，有限振幅Brill波的共形因子仅能数值求解；本文旨在构造首个显式、解析、非平凡且满足物理合理性的真空初始数据，以突破长期依赖数值方法的局限。

Method: 利用Beig-Husa环面共形对称性与Natanzon型变量替换，将各模态方程约化为Gauss超几何方程；构造含双对数函数的闭式度规函数；通过变分原理与基态恒等式确定存在性阈值；严格分析级数收敛性、ADM质量表达式及视界面条件。

Result: 获得首个显式实解析Brill波初始数据族；精确得出共形因子级数（Gamma系数）、ADM质量（digamma级数）、临界参数k_c=0.8048...；证明强波下存在外陷闭曲面（显式极限函数）；数值发现最小球面在k≈0.7670处成对出现。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现Brill波初始数据的完全解析构造，消除了对数值模拟的依赖，为引力波初始数据理论、黑洞形成判据及非线性引力研究提供了严格基准与新工具。

Abstract: Since Brill's work of 1959, the conformal factor of finite-amplitude Brill waves, the axisymmetric and time-symmetric vacuum initial data on $\mathbb{R}^3$ that describe pure gravitational radiation, has been obtained only numerically, up to the most recent studies. Here an explicit one-parameter family of such data is constructed: the first explicit real-analytic, non-trivial, asymptotically flat vacuum initial data with trivial topology and a single end. The free metric function is a closed-form combination of elementary functions and a dilogarithm. The data have the toroidal conformal symmetry of Beig and Husa, and a change of variable of Natanzon type reduces every mode equation to the Gauss hypergeometric equation, so that the conformal factor is a series with explicit Gamma-function coefficients. The quantities that have so far required numerical work are thereby obtained exactly. A ground-state identity gives a sharp existence criterion: complete, asymptotically flat data exist precisely for $k<k_c=0.8048016772\ldots$, and the explicit series is identified with the unique variational solution. The ADM mass is an absolutely convergent digamma series, with $m/a=πk^2/10+O(k^3)$ for small amplitudes and an explicit residue at its critical divergence. Sufficiently strong waves contain trapped surfaces: it is proved that for $k$ close to $k_c$ the rescaled conformal factor converges to an explicit function and explicit tori and spheres become outer trapped, so that the data contain an apparent horizon. A numerical search, restricted to equatorially symmetric star-shaped surfaces, finds marginally trapped (minimal) spheres, which appear in a fold near $k=0.7670$. No thin-torus or weak-field approximation is used.

</details>


### [146] [Hamiltonian surface charges in General Parallel Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16799)
*Tomi Koivisto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出广义平行相对论，作为爱因斯坦引力的动力学等价表述，通过平行标架显式提供边界参考数据，并由此导出有限区域的能量-动量与视界熵作为不同的哈密顿面电荷；应用于RN黑洞与带电稀释子黑洞，验证了其热力学关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在有限区域内协变地定义引力能量-动量和黑洞熵，并统一处理物质贡献与边界电磁数据依赖性，需构建一种能显式引入边界参考结构的新引力表述。

Method: 基于平行标架建立广义平行相对论框架，将微分同胚沿规范标架方向生成准局域能量-动量（含物质面项），法平面洛伦兹提升给出视界熵；将该协变公式应用于Reissner-Nordström黑洞和稀释子带电黑洞。

Result: 在两个黑洞例子中，物质贡献及其勒让德变换将Misner-Sharp质量补全为适配所选电磁边界数据的哈密顿能量；所得视界荷满足由欧几里得边界作用量独立导出的热力学关系。

Conclusion: 广义平行相对论成功实现了有限区域能量-动量与视界熵的协变、几何化定义，且与热力学一致性得到具体黑洞解的验证，表明其为理解引力荷与边界数据关系的有效新框架。

Abstract: General Parallel Relativity is a dynamically equivalent formulation of Einstein gravity in which the parallel frame supplies explicit boundary reference data. In this formulation, finite-region energy-momentum and horizon entropy arise as distinct Hamiltonian surface charges. Diffeomorphisms along canonical frame directions define a quasi-local energy-momentum including a matter surface term, while normal-plane boosts at a smooth horizon give entropy. These covariant formulas are applied to the Reissner-Nordström black hole and to a dilatonic charged black hole. In both examples, the matter contribution and its Legendre transform complete the Misner-Sharp mass into the Hamiltonian energy appropriate to the chosen electromagnetic boundary datum. The resulting horizon charges satisfy the thermodynamic relation obtained independently from the Euclidean boundary action.

</details>


### [147] [Where is the conformal coupling in Palatini gravity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16954)
*Alexandros Karam,Sotirios Karamitsos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了无挠率Palatini理论中具有普通标量动能项的共形耦合问题，指出曲率耦合无法恢复Weyl不变性；通过消除联络，建立了曲率耦合与等效度规理论有效耦合之间的映射，并分析了标量传播、Weyl补偿子行为及耦合常数的一圈跑动性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Palatini框架下，普通标量动能项与曲率耦合能否恢复Weyl不变性尚不明确，且其量子行为（如重整化群流）与度规理论存在本质差异，需系统分析。

Method: 采用Palatini变分法，分析能量-动量张量迹、标量运动方程和联络消除后的等效度规映射；计算一阶圈图修正下的耦合常数β函数，并对比固定联络与联络响应两种方案下的Hessian与跑动行为。

Result: 1) 普通动能项下任意有限曲率耦合均不能恢复Weyl不变性；2) 标量即使在约化动能系数为零时仍传播；3) 仅在无穷大耦合极限下成为Weyl补偿子，对应四维Palatini R²引力；4) 两种联络处理方式下均存在趋近(ξ_eff, λ_eff)→(1/6,0)的红外极限，但正四次耦合下引力系数会在有限能标处归零。

Conclusion: Palatini标量-引力系统的共形结构与量子行为显著区别于度规理论，其Weyl不变性需特殊极限实现，且重整化流揭示了内在不稳定性（引力系数消失），对构建一致紫外完备模型构成关键约束。

Abstract: We study the conformal coupling in the torsion-free Palatini formulation with an ordinary scalar kinetic term. Tuning the curvature coupling cannot restore Weyl invariance in a Palatini theory with an ordinary scalar kinetic term because, for an independent and inert connection, the scalar-curvature term is separately Weyl-invariant, and no finite curvature coupling cancels the Weyl variation of the kinetic term. This can be demonstrated by evaluating the energy-momentum trace on the scalar equation of motion and noting that it is explicitly independent of the Palatini coupling. Eliminating the connection, we construct a map between the curvature coupling and the effective coupling for the equivalent metric theory, and demonstrate that the scalar continues to propagate even if the reduced kinetic coefficient vanishes. We also show that it becomes a Weyl compensator with a scale-invariant potential only in the rescaled infinite-coupling limit, which in four dimensions is directly related to Palatini ${\cal R}^2$ gravity. We calculate the running of the couplings at one loop, and we find that the conformal shift in the metric-curvature coupling's beta function generates a metric-curvature coupling on a fixed metric-affine background, whereas the independent-curvature coupling runs homogeneously. Furthermore, we follow an alternate prescription in which the connection is allowed to respond to the scalar fluctuations, which results in a different scalar Hessian and therefore different beta functions. Nonetheless, we find that both prescriptions admit the formal infrared limit $(ξ_{eff},λ_{eff})\to(1/6,0)$ through different limits of the original couplings in the case of positive perturbative quartics. Finally, we note that trajectories starting on the positive gravitational branch encounter a vanishing gravitational coefficient at a finite scale before this endpoint.

</details>


### [148] [Large mass-ratio ($3< q \leq 10$) inspiral-merger-ringdown model for eccentric binary black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16958)
*Pratul Manna,Tamal RoyChowdhury,Chandra Kant Mishra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为EccHybIMR22的完全解析模型，用于描述具有偏心轨道的双星系统在22模态下的引力波信号，涵盖旋进、并合与铃荡全过程；该模型通过拼接偏心旋进波形与准圆并合-铃荡波形构建，并在质量比q≤10的偏心数值相对论（NR）模拟上训练，特别关注高质比（q>3）情形；利用扩展的SXS NR数据集构造91个混合波形，训练后对测试波形实现约65%–80%的高精度恢复（匹配误差<3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力波模板多基于准圆轨道假设，难以准确建模高偏心率或高质比双星系统的信号；尤其在LIGO等探测器低频段（如20Hz），缺乏足够高质比、高偏心率的数值相对论波形，亟需一个兼顾物理准确性与计算效率的全解析偏心IMR模型。

Method: 提出EccHybIMR22模型：将解析的偏心旋进波形（基于后牛顿理论）与准圆并合-铃荡波形（基于EOB或经验拟合）平滑拼接；使用SXS新发布的偏心NR模拟构造91个混合波形（PN+NR hybrid），其中约30个用于训练，其余用于验证；模型显式建模偏心率e_{20Hz}和质量比q依赖性，特别优化q>3区域的拟合精度。

Result: 在总质量40M⊙下，对保留的混合波形实现65%样本的匹配误差<3%（e_{20Hz}≤0.33当q≤3；e_{20Hz}≤0.12当q>3）；在175个独立SXS NR模拟上，约80%样本达到类似精度（e^{NR}_{20Hz}∈[0,0.3]）；模型可覆盖低至M_low=20M⊙、f=20Hz的探测敏感区，填补了高q偏心波形空白。

Conclusion: EccHybIMR22是首个面向高质比、中等偏心率（e_{20Hz}≲0.3）双星系统的全解析、混合型IMR引力波模板，显著拓展了当前偏心波形建模能力，为LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA数据分析中偏心源的搜寻与参数估计提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We present $\texttt{EccHybIMR22}$, a fully analytical $\textbf{Ecc}$entric-$\textbf{Hyb}$rid-$\textbf{I}$nspiral-$\textbf{M}$erger-$\textbf{R}$ingdown model for the quadrupole ($\ell$=$2$, $|m|$=$2$ or simply the $\textbf{22}$) mode. The model is obtained by smoothly attaching an eccentric-inspiral to a quasi-circular-merger-ringdown prescription and is trained on eccentric numerical relativity (NR) simulations with mass ratios $q\leq 10$. In particular, we focus on obtaining a model that is sensitive to large mass ratios ($q>3$). Leveraging the recent expansion of SXS catalog of NR simulations, we construct a set of 91 hybrids (obtained by combining waveforms from post-Newtonian (PN) theory with NR simulations) with a target low total mass limit ($M_{\rm low}$) of $20M_{\odot}$ at an averaged 22 mode frequency of 20Hz, inaccessible with existing NR simulations alone. Employing a little less than a third of these hybrids as targets, we construct $\texttt{EccHybIMR22}$ and recover $65\%$ of the hybrids from the remaining set with mismatches smaller than $\sim3\%$ for a total mass of $40M_\odot$ (chosen arbitrarily) and orbital eccentricity $e_{\rm 20Hz}\lesssim0.33$ ($e_{\rm 20Hz}\lesssim 0.12$) for mass ratios $q\leq3$ ($q>3$). Furthermore, when tested against 175 SXS simulations, the model is able to recover nearly $80\%$ of the simulations with similar mismatches for systems with $0\lesssim e^{\rm NR}_{\rm 20Hz}\lesssim 0.3$.

</details>


### [149] [Quasibound states of charged Dirac fields around Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17034)
*Nodirbek Anarboev,Javlonbek Khasanov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Bobomurat Ahmedov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡（EEH）黑洞背景下带质量、带电狄拉克场的准束缚态，发现EEH量子电动力学修正对准束缚态频率影响微弱且呈线性，而对衰减率的影响则依赖于模式分支，并揭示了库仑屏蔽增强泄漏、度规修正调控角动量相关效应的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子真空极化效应对黑洞周围场的束缚态行为的影响，特别是EEH理论作为RN黑洞的QED修正模型中准束缚态的稳定性与频谱特性。

Method: 推导一阶EEH背景度规与势；分离径向方程并建立排除实频准束缚态的恒等式；采用双侧打靶法结合Muller根求解器数值计算复频率；分别开启度规与势修正并辅以WKB分析；时域特征演化结合Richardson外推矩阵 pencil 方法验证频域结果。

Result: EEH修正下无实频准束缚态（即无超辐射不稳定性）；能级结构与RN黑洞一致，无精细结构分裂；实部频率偏移为近区主导、线性且随角动量强抑制；虚部（衰减率）变化模式依赖：ℓ=0基态寿命略增，而特定高j、ℓ态衰减加快达80%，近捕获阈值处衰减率最多增加60%；库仑屏蔽总增强泄漏，度规修正决定ℓ相关效应的符号与大小。

Conclusion: EEH量子真空修正不改变RN黑洞准束缚态的基本稳定性与氢原子类能谱，但通过不同机制（势与度规）选择性调制各模式的衰减行为，表明低能有效QED修正对黑洞量子场动力学具有可分辨但受限的影响。

Abstract: We study quasibound states of a massive charged Dirac field on the Einstein--Euler--Heisenberg (EEH) black hole, the extension of Reissner--Nordström (RN) by the leading weak-field vacuum-polarization term of QED. We derive the background to first order in the Euler--Heisenberg coupling $a$. We show that it admits a nondegenerate horizon at $Q=M$ and an extremal charge $Q_{\rm ext}=M[1+a/(40M^2)+\mathcal{O}(a^2)]>M$, with horizon data that depend on $\sqrt a$ near extremality. From the separated radial equations, we derive an identity that excludes real-frequency quasibound states on any background of this class, so damped branches cannot become unstable. Since the EEH corrections first enter at $\mathcal{O}(r^{-5})$ in the electrostatic potential, the trapping condition $Mμ^2-qQω_R>0$ and the hydrogenic spectrum coincide with RN, and no fine-structure-order splitting arises. Two-sided shooting with a Muller root finder shows that the real frequencies shift only by near-zone amounts that are linear in $a$ and strongly suppressed with angular momentum. The damping rates respond in a branch-dependent way. At $Q/M=0.99$ the $\ell=0$ ground state becomes slightly longer-lived, while the $(n_r,j,\ell,λ)=(0,\tfrac32,1,-2)$ state decays up to $80\%$ faster for $a/M^2\le1$, and near the trapping threshold the damping rate increases by up to $60\%$. Switching on the metric and potential corrections separately, together with a WKB analysis of the inner barrier, identifies the mechanisms: Coulomb screening always increases leakage, while the metric correction controls the sign and size of the $\ell$-dependent effect. Characteristic time-domain evolutions with Richardson-extrapolated matrix-pencil extraction reproduce the frequency-domain shifts.

</details>


### [150] [Bartnik's splitting conjecture and the cosmological wall: a Cauchy Busemann function approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17092)
*E. Minguzzi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Bartnik猜想，证明了任何反例都具有特殊的全局因果结构，包括未来和过去宇宙发展区域、宇宙墙以及宇宙视界，并利用Cauchy Busemann函数分析其正则性和动力学性质，进而得到若干刚性与分裂结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Bartnik猜想——满足类时收敛条件的类时测地完备宇宙时空是否必为静态乘积结构，并探索其反例可能具有的几何与因果特征。

Method: 引入Cauchy Busemann函数b±刻画宇宙发展区域；通过M×ℝ上的乘积技巧将b+视为某Cauchy视界的图函数；结合半凹性、Lipschitz连续性、标定曲线（类时射线）等分析工具；运用因果性、凸性及平均曲率条件进行刚性推导。

Result: 发现反例必含宇宙墙与C⁰类光超曲面构成的宇宙视界，且视界成分含类光直线；b+在其有限域上连续、局部Lipschitz与局部半凹，其标定曲线为类时射线；若两宇宙发展区域相交（如b+在有限域上C¹），则存在类时直线从而时空分裂；获得Hawking奇点定理的局域刚性版本：若某紧致Cauchy超曲面在未来发展上平均曲率非正，则时空分裂；若宇宙墙内部为空，则其等于两视界并被类光直线所铺满，在零完备性下各分支为光滑全测地类光超曲面。

Conclusion: Bartnik猜想尚未完全解决，但本文系统揭示了潜在反例的刚性结构约束，表明其必须同时满足精细的因果、正则与动力学条件，从而为最终证明或构造反例提供了关键几何框架与必要条件。

Abstract: Bartnik's conjecture states that a timelike geodesically complete cosmological spacetime satisfying the timelike convergence condition splits as a static product. We show that any counterexample exhibits a peculiar global causal structure: two disjoint open regions, the future and the past cosmological developments, separated by a non-empty closed causally convex set: the cosmological wall. The developments are bounded by $C^0$ null hypersurfaces, the cosmological horizons, and a component of each horizon contains a lightlike line. These objects are natural and hence carry physical significance, as they do not depend on the choice of Cauchy hypersurface. They can be defined through the Cauchy Busemann functions $b^\pm$ of a compact Cauchy hypersurface $Σ$, the developments being their finiteness domains. Using a product trick we show that $b^+$ is the graph function of a Cauchy horizon in $M\times \mathbb{R}$, and so inherits the regularity and dynamical properties of horizons: $b^+$ is continuous, locally Lipschitz and locally semi-concave on its finiteness domain, and its calibrated curves are timelike rays. We prove that if the two cosmological developments intersect, which happens whenever $b^+$ is $C^1$ on its finiteness domain, then the spacetime contains a timelike line and hence splits, a result which recovers several positive results of the literature. We also obtain a localized rigidity version of Hawking's singularity theorem: if some compact Cauchy hypersurface has non-positive mean curvature on the future development, the spacetime splits. Hence, in a counterexample, every compact Cauchy hypersurface expands somewhere in the future development and contracts somewhere in the past one. If the wall has empty interior, it coincides with both horizons and is ruled by lightlike lines, and under null completeness its components are smooth totally geodesic null hypersurfaces.

</details>


### [151] [Vanishing Love Numbers of Kerr-Newman Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17179)
*Sumanta Chakraborty,Takuya Katagiri,Paolo Pani,Jun-Xi Shi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过利用引力与电磁扰动可分离性的最新突破，首次解析确定了四维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中最一般稳态克尔-纽曼黑洞的静态潮汐响应，发现其所有保守型Love数（包括混合引力-电磁耦合项）均严格为零，并计算了由参考系拖曳引起的非零静态耗散响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克尔-纽曼黑洞的潮汐响应因引力与电磁扰动内在耦合而长期未解，而潮汐Love数是探测致密天体本质的关键保守量。

Method: 利用近期发现的引力与电磁扰动可分离性，对任意多极阶、电荷和自转的克尔-纽曼黑洞进行解析分析，计算其静态保守与耗散响应。

Result: 证明任意多极阶、电荷和自转下，克尔-纽曼黑洞的所有保守型Love数（含混合项）严格为零；同时计算出由参考系拖曳导致的非零静态耗散响应。

Conclusion: 最一般的四维稳态黑洞在保守层面上无潮汐形变，支持‘黑洞无毛’在潮汐响应层面的延拓；但存在由转动引发的耗散效应，揭示了黑洞响应的更丰富结构。

Abstract: Tidal Love numbers characterize the conservative response of compact objects to external perturbations and provide a powerful probe of their nature. While the static Love numbers of Schwarzschild, Kerr, and Reissner--Nordström black holes remarkably vanish, the tidal response of a generic Kerr--Newman black hole has remained unexplored due to the intrinsic coupling between gravitational and electromagnetic perturbations. Exploiting a recent breakthrough in the separability of these perturbations, we analytically determine the static tidal response of the most general stationary black hole in four-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell theory. For arbitrary multipole order, charge, and spin, we show that the entire matrix of conservative Love numbers vanishes, including the mixed gravito-electromagnetic couplings. We also compute the nonvanishing static dissipative response induced by frame dragging.

</details>


### [152] [Turning Selection Bias into a New Statistic: Asymmetry and Fringes of Lensed Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18768)
*Juno C. L. Chan,Elwin K. Y. Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了引力波在传播过程中因引力透镜效应导致的波光学畸变对现有匹配滤波搜寻方法的影响，发现这种畸变会显著降低探测效率；提出了一种不扩展模板库即可识别被降级的透镜化引力波信号的新统计量，首次建立了透镜可观测量与探测之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前匹配滤波搜寻未纳入引力透镜引起的波光学畸变，可能导致大量透镜化引力波事件被遗漏，亟需评估其对信号一致性检验的影响并发展新探测策略。

Method: 采用双像干涉模型，结合时域和频域的典型信号一致性统计量，系统分析引力透镜对SNR分布的影响；基于时间域SNR残差的反射对称性破缺，构建新型统计量以区分透镜化信号与噪声及非透镜信号。

Result: 发现重质量信号在频域出现SNR残差极小值；所提统计量能有效分离被降级的透镜化注入信号与背景噪声及成对非透镜信号，且无需扩展模板库。

Conclusion: 当前仅依赖匹配滤波检测后筛选透镜信号的策略存在根本局限；本文提出的不对称性统计方法为首次直接探测透镜化引力波开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) propagating across the Universe can be distorted by intervening lenses, producing characteristic wave-optics signatures. These effects are not incorporated into current matched-filtering searches and can therefore substantially reduce detection efficiency. The underlying mechanism is the signal-consistency test, which measures deviations between the observed and expected signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) distributions. We report a systematic study of the impact of gravitational lensing on signal-consistency tests, using a two-image interference model and representative signal-consistency statistics in both the time and frequency domains. We find that overlapping images produce sharply localized SNR residuals in the time domain, breaking the reflection symmetry of the autocorrelation about the merger time. Although interference fringes produce broadband SNR residuals in the frequency domain, both domains exhibit a minimum for high-mass signals when the fringe structure becomes comparable to the signal bandwidth. This highlights a limitation of current strategies that search for distorted lensed signals only among GW events detected by matched-filtering searches. Exploiting this asymmetry, we develop a statistic that separates selected down-ranked lensed injections from noise-background triggers and paired nonlensed injections, without extending the template bank. Our results establish the first connection between lensing observables and detection, paving the way for the discovery of lensed GWs.

</details>


### [153] [Null-Energy Bounds on Photon-Orbit Instability in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17273)
*Sarandas A.,C. Fairoos,C. L. Ahmed Rizwan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了高维爱因斯坦-高斯-博内引力中不稳定类光圆轨道的坐标时间李雅普诺夫指数的局域上界，给出了其与物质密度、Misner-Sharp质量及光学曲率等几何量的关系，并讨论了其在强引力透镜和试探场阻尼中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维修正引力理论中光子球稳定性与混沌动力学的关系，特别是高斯-博内项对类光轨道不稳定性的影响，以及该不稳定性如何受物质分布约束。

Method: 基于静态球对称渐近平直时空，利用爱因斯坦-高斯-博内场方程与类光测地线变分分析，结合切向零能量条件、Misner-Sharp质量定义与光学几何（高斯曲率、零汇）进行解析推导。

Result: 得到坐标时间李雅普诺夫指数的局域上界，其系数偏离广义相对论值(n−3)的程度由局域密度与广义Misner-Sharp质量密度之差决定；真空解达边界，带电麦克斯韦场产生严格正间隙；存在不依赖密度的推论。

Conclusion: 该上界将引力理论修正、物质内容与光学混沌行为统一于一个几何不等式中，为检验高维引力模型提供了新的可观测量和约束条件。

Abstract: We derive a local upper bound on the coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent of unstable circular null orbits in static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity in $n\geq5$ dimensions with nonnegative Gauss--Bonnet coupling. On the regular Einstein branch, the tangential null energy condition suffices; no additional density-pressure inequality is required for the principal result. The bound retains the local matter-density contribution dictated by the field equations. Using the spherical mass relation, we express the departure of its dimensionless coefficient from the Einstein value $n-3$ in terms of the difference between the local density and the enclosed generalized Misner--Sharp mass per areal volume. This gives a sufficient density-contrast criterion for the Einstein optical bound to remain valid at nonzero coupling. Optical Gaussian curvature and tangential null convergence provide geometrical interpretations that distinguish radial tidal instability from the focusing of a null congruence. Vacuum solutions saturate the bound, while a Maxwell field with nonzero charge produces a strictly positive gap. The smooth Einstein limit and explicit vacuum and charged solutions provide consistency checks. A separate matter condition yields a density-free corollary. We also discuss consequences for strong-deflection lensing and test-field eikonal damping within their respective regimes of validity.

</details>


### [154] [Einstein Telescope: the triangle in a network and the impact of the relative orientation of detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17446)
*Giacomo Bruno,Antonin Oswald,Federico De Lillo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）不同几何构型（三角形、双L形及混合构型）在独立运行及与其它引力波探测器联合运行时的科学性能，发现混合构型在独立模式下表现最优，而联合探测可缩小构型间差异；同时探讨了共址噪声影响及探测器相对朝向对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ET的几何构型是其关键设计选择之一，需评估不同构型在多种科学目标（如致密双星并合、随机引力波背景）下的性能差异，并考虑其与现有或未来探测器网络协同工作的效果。

Method: 通过数值模拟与灵敏度分析，比较三种ET构型（单三角形、双L形、三角形+远距离L形）在独立运行及与Cosmic Explorer或LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA网络联合运行下的定位精度、信噪比、背景谱估计能力等指标；同时分析共址噪声影响及相对朝向（如倾斜角）的敏感性。

Result: 混合构型（三角形+远距离L形）在独立运行时显著优于其他两种构型；联合探测削弱了构型间差异；共址相关噪声可通过增加探测器缓解；双L形ET的最佳倾斜角约为30°，而含三角形的构型对朝向不敏感。

Conclusion: ET应优先考虑混合构型以最大化独立科学产出；在多探测器网络背景下，构型选择可更灵活；设计中需兼顾朝向优化与噪声抑制策略。

Abstract: The geometrical configuration of the Einstein Telescope (ET) gravitational-wave observatory is one the most important design choices to be made for this future scientific facility. Two baseline configurations are currently being considered, a single triangle or 2 distant L-shaped detectors tilted by about $45^{\circ}$. In this document we present a study of the scientific performance of the ET baseline configurations in both stand-alone mode and in the presence of either an L-shaped Cosmic Explorer detector with 40-km long arms located in the United States or the existing LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA network. We also include in the study an alternative ET configuration, which is currently not being considered and consists of a triangle plus a distant L-shaped detector. We find that in stand-alone mode this alternative configuration performs significantly better than the other two in both the compact binary coalescence and stochastic gravitational wave background scientific cases. The differences in performance between the ET configurations are reduced in the presence of the considered, additional non-ET detectors. We also show that the impact of possible correlated noise in co-located detectors on the stochastic gravitational-wave background performance can be addressed with the addition of extra detectors. Finally, we study the impact on the ET scientific performance of the relative orientation of the future detectors in the network. We find that, while the configurations that include a triangular ET detector are basically insensitive to this orientation, a balanced compromise for the ET configuration consisting of two L-shaped detectors could be represented by a tilt angle between the two detectors of about $30^{\circ}$.

</details>


### [155] [Mathematical framework for simulation of non-perturbative cavity optomechanics with plate-like dielectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17475)
*Andrew Laeuger,Nancy Aggarwal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了真空双光子形式主义，以处理有限厚度平面电介质，系统地建立了包含Fresnel反射、内部传播、光学力和机械反作用的腔光力学模型，为含腔内电介质的实验（如引力波探测器）提供了探测器响应和噪声预算的计算起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腔光力学模型无法自洽地确定空间分辨的腔场、光学力、机械平衡及反作用，尤其当有限厚度介电板完全拦截光学模式并重构腔内场时。

Method: 扩展真空双光子形式主义至有限厚度平面电介质，将Fresnel反射、内部传播、光学力和机械反作用直接纳入场方程，并推导载波与边带传递函数。

Result: 得到了描述耦合光-机系统的载波和边带传递函数，涵盖光力学冷却及对外部扰动的响应，为含腔内电介质的实验提供系统化的建模起点。

Conclusion: 该模型为引力波探测器等使用悬浮传感器的腔实验提供了更精确的探测器响应与噪声预算分析框架。

Abstract: Cavity optomechanical models typically treat a sensor either as a perturbative polarizable object in a pre-existing optical field, or solve the field at a fixed equilibrium and linearize in a fixed optical-mode basis. Neither self-consistently determines the spatially resolved cavity field, the optical force, the mechanical equilibrium, and the back-action when a finite-thickness dielectric plate intercepts the entire optical mode and restructures the intracavity field. In this companion paper to [1], we develop that treatment by extending the vacuum two-photon formalism to finite-thickness planar dielectrics, incorporating their Fresnel reflection, internal propagation, optical forces, and mechanical back-action directly into the field equations. We derive the carrier and sideband transfer functions needed to describe the coupled optical-mechanical system, including optomechanical cooling and the response to external perturbations. The result is a systematic starting point for calculating detector response and noise budgets for cavity experiments with intracavity dielectrics, including gravitational wave detectors deploying levitated sensors.

</details>


### [156] [Hamiltonian analysis of disformally coupled fermions in scalar-tensor theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17501)
*Alexander Ganz,Apostolos Tsampodimos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了N个标量场与狄拉克费米子耦合下的逆变变换，证明了即使引入高阶导数，也不会产生Ostrogradsky幽灵，并通过哈密顿分析确认了自由度数目等于玻色理论与费米子自由度之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多标量场与费米子耦合下逆变变换所导致的高阶导数问题及其是否引发Ostrogradsky幽灵。

Method: 采用逆变变换方法，结合哈密顿分析与Grassmann代数展开，分析约束矩阵结构与场方程性质。

Result: 证明高阶导数项不贡献于约束矩阵主体，场方程在每阶Grassmann展开下均为线性；自由度数目保持为原始玻色理论加费米子自由度。

Conclusion: 逆变变换在单方向作用时可避免有害高阶导数；费米子耦合不额外限制多场DHOST理论中逆变函数的形式，且不引入Ostrogradsky不稳定性。

Abstract: We study disformal transformations of $\mathcal{N}$ scalar fields coupled to a Dirac fermion. The resulting axial coupling generically introduces higher derivatives in the scalar field equations of motion; for field-dependent free functions, these are absent if and only if the disformal transformation acts along a single field-space direction. We show that these higher derivatives do not signal an Ostrogradsky ghost. After the transformation, the fermion registers the derivatives of the matter-frame tetrad only through the axial current, so that every coupling to the derivatives of the scalar fields multiplies a fermion bilinear, which is Grassmann even and nilpotent. Such terms do not contribute to the body of the constraint matrix, and at each level of the Grassmann expansion the field equations are linear, with the principal part of the theory without fermions. Through a Hamiltonian analysis, we show that the number of degrees of freedom is that of the bosonic theory plus that of the fermion, both in the Einstein frame, for free functions depending on the scalar fields and their kinetic matrix, and for minimally coupled fermions in multi-field DHOST theories. The same mechanism applies to disformal transformations with higher derivatives of a single scalar field, for which fermions impose no condition beyond those required by bosonic matter.

</details>


### [157] [Black holes with dark matter halos and a positive cosmological constant: Energy conditions and signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17628)
*Long-Yue Li,Li-Ming Cao,Yungui Gong,Xia-Yuan Liu,Liang-Bi Wu,Wenting Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在正宇宙学常数存在下，被暗物质晕包围的静态球对称黑洞，分析了能量条件对暗物质分布的约束，并构建了Hernquist型暗物质分布模型，进而研究了黑洞阴影图像和标量拟正则模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗物质晕与黑洞及宇宙学常数共同作用下的时空结构及其可观测效应（如黑洞图像和拟正则模）

Method: 基于零径向压强假设，推导各类能量条件对暗物质晕的约束；构造Hernquist型暗物质密度分布；计算黑洞阴影图像与标量场拟正则模

Result: 暗物质晕被限制在两个视界之间的有限径向区间内，总质量不超过Nariai极限；正宇宙学常数显著改变黑洞图像；当暗物质质量较小时，某选定拟正则模的相对偏差近似正比于暗物质晕的紧致度（M_DM / a），比例系数依赖于宇宙学常数

Conclusion: 暗物质晕的分布受严格能量条件约束，其与宇宙学常数共同显著影响黑洞的光学与动力学性质，为联合探测量子引力、暗物质与宇宙加速膨胀提供新途径

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric black holes surrounded by dark matter halos in the presence of a positive cosmological constant. Assuming vanishing radial pressure, we derive the constraints imposed by the null, weak, strong, and dominant energy conditions on the halo matter. The halo is confined to a finite radial interval separated from both horizons, and the total mass is bounded above by the Nariai value. We construct a Hernquist-type profile and study black hole images, showing that a positive cosmological constant can substantially change the images. We also compute scalar quasinormal modes of the Hernquist-type spacetime. When the dark matter mass is sufficiently small, the relative difference of a selected mode is approximately linear in the compactness of the matter halo, i.e., the ratio of mass parameter $M_{\text{DM}}$ and scale parameter $a$, with a coefficient that depends on the cosmological constant.

</details>


### [158] [Exact radial stability threshold for a Minkowski-Schwarzschild thin shell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17629)
*Anuar Idrissov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了分隔平直内部与史瓦西外部的普通物质薄壳的稳定性，这是亚光速曲速驱动的最简单模型。研究发现，对于由因果物质构成的壳，存在一个精确的临界半径，低于该半径壳在径向扰动下无法保持稳定；当壳内放置黑洞时，该临界半径随黑洞质量增大而增大，表明平直内部是最有利情形。由于此类壳已知对非径向扰动不稳定，该结果标定了任何稳定机制需起作用的区域边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建亚光速曲速驱动的物理可行性模型，特别是分析其核心结构——分隔不同时空区域的普通物质薄壳的稳定性。

Method: 基于广义相对论的膜世界方法和Israel拼接条件，分析薄壳在径向扰动下的稳定性，并推广至壳内含黑洞的情形。

Result: 推导出因果物质薄壳稳定的精确径向临界半径；发现该半径随壳内黑洞质量增加而增大；确认平直内部情形最有利于稳定性；指出非径向不稳定性意味着该临界半径定义了稳定机制必须起效的边界。

Conclusion: 亚光速曲速驱动所需的普通物质薄壳存在固有稳定性极限，其径向稳定性的临界尺度受内部时空结构（尤其是是否存在黑洞）显著影响；任何实际实现方案必须依赖能克服非径向不稳定的额外稳定机制。

Abstract: We study the stability of a thin shell of ordinary matter that separates a flat interior from a Schwarzschild exterior, which is the simplest model of a subluminal warp drive. The matter content of the shell is fixed by the junction conditions and satisfies all the standard energy conditions except very close to the horizon. For a shell made of causal matter we find an exact radius below which it cannot be stable under radial perturbations. We then place a black hole inside the shell and show that this radius grows with the black hole mass, so that the flat interior is the most favourable case. Since such shells are known to be unstable to non-radial perturbations, our result marks the boundary of the region in which any stabilising mechanism would have to operate.

</details>


### [159] [Classical and quantum scattering of massive Klein--Gordon fields in asymptotically flat spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17819)
*Mikhail Molodyk*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用微局部分析工具，在非捕获渐近平坦时空（包括闵可夫斯基空间的辐射扰动）中定义了大质量Klein-Gordon场量子理论的入射与出射真空态；证明了这些态满足Hadamard条件，且对应的粒子表象单位等价，即量子S矩阵良定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非捕获渐近平坦时空中，为大质量Klein-Gordon场建立严格的量子散射理论，尤其避免依赖经典波的显式渐近展开。

Method: 采用微局部分析方法，刻画经典Klein-Gordon方程正、负频率解的渐近区分，进而定义满足Hadamard条件的入射与出射真空态，并分析其量子表象的单位等价性。

Result: 成功定义了满足Hadamard条件的入射与出射真空态，并证明其对应粒子表象单位等价，从而确立了量子S矩阵的良好定义性。

Conclusion: 微局部分析方法可在不依赖经典波显式渐近形式的前提下，严格构建渐近平坦时空中的量子散射理论，为弯曲时空量子场论提供新框架。

Abstract: We use microlocal tools to define distinguished incoming and outgoing vacuum states of the quantum theory of massive Klein--Gordon fields in non-trapping asymptotically flat spacetimes, including radiative perturbations of Minkowski space. The asymptotic vacuum states satisfy the Hadamard condition, and the incoming and outgoing particle representations are unitarily equivalent, or in other words the quantum S-matrix is well-defined. These results are based on a description of scattering of classical fields which keeps track of the asymptotic distinction between positive- and negative-frequency solutions of the Klein--Gordon equation. Our emphasis is on developing the theory without recourse to explicit asymptotics for classical waves.

</details>


### [160] [Quasiperiodic oscillations as probes of rotating black holes in exponential dark matter environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18044)
*Madina Alimkulova,Kuantay Boshkayev,Sholpan Gumarova,Aliya Taukenova,Ainur Urazalina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了具有指数球型暗物质晕的旋转黑洞模型，并研究其对测试粒子运动和准周期振荡（QPOs）的影响；结果表明暗物质晕中心密度起效如等效宇宙常数，且对QPO数据拟合限制了暗物质密度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质晕对黑洞时空结构及周围粒子动力学的影响，特别是其在天体物理观测（如QPO）中的可探测印记。

Method: 采用Azreg-Aïnou形式的Newman-Janis算法构造带指数球型暗物质晕的旋转黑洞度规；推导适用于任意Kerr-like度规的轨道与竖直/径向epicyclic频率解析表达式；利用相对论进动模型结合MCMC方法拟合GRO J1655−40和XTE J1859+226的QPO三重峰数据。

Result: 暗物质晕中心密度ρ₀以等效宇宙常数Λ_eff=8πρ₀形式一阶影响频率；使最内稳定圆轨道外移并引入稳定轨道外边界；QPO拟合给出ρ₀M² < 2.9×10⁻⁵ 和 3.0×10⁻⁵（95%置信度），对应物理密度上限ρ₀ ≳ 10¹¹ g/cm³。

Conclusion: 常规暗物质晕对Kerr黑洞QPO特征影响微弱，当前QPO观测尚无法区分Kerr黑洞与含典型暗晕的旋转黑洞；仅极高密度暗核可被排除。

Abstract: We construct the rotating counterpart of a black hole embedded in a dark-matter halo with the exponential-sphere profile $ρ=ρ_0e^{-r/r_0}$, using the Newman--Janis algorithm in the Azreg-Aïnou form, and study its imprints on test-particle motion and quasiperiodic oscillations (QPOs). We derive closed-form orbital and epicyclic frequencies valid for any Kerr-like metric with a mass function and show that, to first order, the halo enters them only through its central density, acting as an effective cosmological constant $Λ_{eff}=8πρ_0$; the corrections are therefore universal for all cored profiles. The halo moves the innermost stable circular orbit outward and produces an outer boundary of stable orbits, while the sign of the frequency shift depends on the assumed equation of state of the halo. Fitting the relativistic precession model to the simultaneous QPO triplets of GRO~J1655$-$40 and XTE~J1859+226 with Markov-chain Monte Carlo sampling yields spins and emission radii consistent with Kerr and $ρ_0M^2<2.9\times10^{-5}$ and $3.0\times10^{-5}$ (95\%), respectively. In physical units these limits exclude only ultra-dense dark cores, $ρ_0\gtrsim10^{11}$~g\,cm$^{-3}$.

</details>


### [161] [Pseudo-spectral methods in gravitational lensing: Spin precession in loop quantum black hole spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18064)
*Xiankai Pang,Xiao-Kan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将引力透镜效应视为两点边值问题的新视角，通过指定偏转角来反求轨道，并利用切比雪夫伪谱法高效求解；同时统一处理了两种圈量子引力黑洞时空中的粒子自旋进动，发现量子修正对慢速粒子影响显著，而超相对论极限下仍恢复普适自旋翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上引力透镜被视为初值问题，本文旨在从逆向思维出发，将偏转角作为输入、冲击参数作为非线性本征值，构建更自然且数值稳定的两点边值问题框架。

Method: 采用基于切比雪夫配置点的伪谱法求解两点边值问题；将自旋平行输运方程转化为同一网格上的线性系统；所有方程以一般静态球对称度规（含三个自由函数）形式写出，从而统一处理两种圈量子引力黑洞模型。

Result: 成功计算了两种圈量子黑洞（协变有效度规与自对偶圈量子黑洞）中后向散射粒子的自旋进动；发现量子修正对低速粒子影响显著，而v→1时总趋于普适自旋翻转；cosξ对速度v在v=1处的导数是强场可观测量，明确依赖于两种时空的量子参数。

Conclusion: 该边值问题框架不仅适用于所研究的两种量子黑洞模型，还具有广泛适用性，为强场引力透镜与自旋动力学提供了稳健、统一且高效的数值分析工具。

Abstract: Gravitational lensing is usually treated as an initial value problem: for a given impact parameter of an unbound orbit, one computes the resulting deflection angle. In this paper we advocate the inverse point of view. For a prescribed deflection angle, the orbit is selected by two boundary conditions at spatial infinity, so that the lensing problem becomes a well-defined two-point boundary value problem, with the impact parameter playing the role of a nonlinear eigenvalue. We show that pseudo-spectral methods based on Chebyshev collocation solve this boundary value problem accurately at modest computational cost, and that the parallel transport equations governing the spin precession of the particle reduce to a single linear system on the same grid. As a concrete application, we compute the spin precession of massive particles in backward scattering off two black hole spacetimes from loop quantum gravity: the covariant effective metric and the self-dual loop quantum black hole. All equations are written for a general static, spherically symmetric line element with three free functions, so that both spacetimes are handled on the same footing. We find that the quantum corrections are most pronounced for slowly moving particles, while in the ultra-relativistic limit the universal spin flip is always recovered. The flip itself is universal, but the rate at which it is approached is not: the derivative of $\cosξ$ with respect to the particle speed at $v=1$ is a strong-field quantity in every backward channel and depends clearly on the quantum parameters of both spacetimes. The framework is not restricted to the models studied

</details>


### [162] [Kantowski Sachs Cosmological Model with Radiation and Generalized Chaplygin Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18112)
*G. Oliveira-Neto,R. Hincho Laucata*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了与辐射和广义Chaplygin气体耦合的各向异性Kantowski-Sachs宇宙学模型的演化，旨在验证其是否会演化为描述当前宇宙的标准各向同性FRW模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Kantowski-Sachs各向异性模型是否能演化为当前被广泛接受的各向同性FRW宇宙模型，从而探讨其作为宇宙早期阶段的可能性。

Method: 理论分析Kantowski-Sachs模型在辐射与广义Chaplygin气体耦合作用下的动力学演化行为。

Result: 未明确给出具体数值或定性结果，但研究聚焦于判断该模型能否趋近于FRW模型。

Conclusion: 若该模型确实可演化至FRW形式，则暗示Kantowski-Sachs模型可能是宇宙早期的合理候选模型。

Abstract: In the present paper we study the evolution of a homogeneous and anisotropic Kantowski Sachs cosmological model coupled with radiation and a generalized Chaplygin gas. Our main objective is verifying if this model evolves to a homogeneous and isotropic Friedmann Robertson Walker cosmological model, which is the more accepted model to describe our present Universe. If this is the case, we may imagine that the Kantowski Sachs cosmological model could had been an early stage of our Universe.

</details>


### [163] [Adiabatic particle production in a time-dependent accelerated frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18241)
*Victor M Villalba*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在一种特殊的时变加速参考系中，一个大质量标量场的量子行为。该参考系中固定坐标观测者在t=0时刻具有最大固有加速度，并随时间渐近趋于惯性，且不形成视界。作者求解了Klein-Gordon方程，发现其时间部分满足修正Mathieu方程，空间部分对应一个具有离散谱的束缚势薛定谔问题。通过在最大加速度时刻设定绝热真空初态，并计算未来惯性区域中的粒子数，发现粒子谱呈代数衰减（而非热谱），且高阶绝热近似可逐项消除端点展开中的发散项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非惯性系下量子场论的基本结构，特别是无视界但存在瞬时强加速的情形下粒子产生的机制与谱形特征，补充传统Unruh效应（依赖于视界和热谱）之外的物理图景。

Method: 将标量场置于一类特殊时间依赖的加速坐标系中，分离Klein-Gordon方程得到时间（修正Mathieu方程）和空间（束缚态薛定谔问题）两部分；采用t=0时刻的绝热真空作为初态；通过解析延拓空间本征函数构造out模，并严格计算粒子数；分析绝热展开的渐近行为。

Result: 粒子数谱随初始模式频率呈1/ω⁸衰减（零阶绝热）；高阶绝热近似可系统抵消端点展开项；粒子产生完全源于t=0时刻初态与out真空之间的失配；最终谱为代数型，非Planckian。

Conclusion: 在无视界的瞬时强加速过程中，仍可观测到粒子产生，但其谱由动力学失配主导，呈现代数衰减特征，揭示了Unruh类效应更广泛的适用范围与非热本质。

Abstract: We study a massive scalar field in flat spacetime written in a time-dependent accelerated frame whose fixed-coordinate observers have maximal proper acceleration at $t=0$ and become asymptotically inertial without forming a horizon. The Klein-Gordon equation separates into a modified Mathieu equation in time and a confining Schrödinger problem in space with a discrete spectrum. We impose an adiabatic vacuum at the instant of maximal acceleration and compute the particle number in the inertial future. For the zeroth-order adiabatic state it falls as the inverse eighth power of the initial mode frequency, and raising the adiabatic order cancels successive terms of the endpoint expansion, in analogy with the switching transients of finite-time detectors. Analytic continuation of the spatial eigenfunctions gives the out modes in closed form and an exact expression for the particle number, which is entirely the mismatch at $t=0$ between the initial state and the out vacuum. The spectrum is algebraic rather than Planckian.

</details>


### [164] [Dirac stars in modified $F(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18273)
*Zhuldyz Alibekova,Vladimir Dzhunushaliev,Vladimir Folomeev,Sergei Odintsov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在R²修正引力理论中，由线性和非线性旋量场共同激发的狄拉克迷你星，分析了其质量分布、自旋场与度规函数、有效能量密度等性质随旋量非线性参数变化的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究修正引力（特别是R²引力）对狄拉克迷你星结构的影响，以及旋量场非线性效应对星体物理性质的调控作用。

Method: 构建广义相对论与R²修正引力下的二维（依赖旋量频率）和三维（依赖中心旋量场值）迷你星质量分布图；数值求解含非线性旋量场的引力-物质耦合方程组，分析各物理量随非线性参数的变化。

Result: R²引力修正显著改变迷你星的结构特性；增大旋量非线性参数会明显改变旋量场与度规函数形态、有效能量密度分布及总质量与振荡频率的关系；效应在大非线性参数下最为显著。

Conclusion: 旋量场非线性与R²引力修正具有协同效应，可显著重塑狄拉克迷你星的物理图像，为区分引力理论及致密天体物态提供新线索。

Abstract: We study Dirac mini-stars in modified $R^2$ gravity, where gravity is sourced by both linear and nonlinear spinor fields. Two-dimensional (2D) and three-dimensional (3D) profiles of the mini-star mass are constructed as functions of the spinor frequency (for the 2D profiles) and the central value of the spinor field (for the 3D profiles) in general relativity and modified gravity for various values of the spinor nonlinearity parameter. We show that the modification of gravity alters the properties of these configurations, while increasing the nonlinearity parameter within the framework of $R^2$ gravity significantly changes the profiles of the spinor and metric functions, the effective energy density, and the dependence of the total mass on the oscillation frequency. The most pronounced changes occur at large values of the nonlinearity parameter.

</details>


### [165] [Stable manifolds of heteroclinic chains in Hořava-Lifshitz cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18283)
*Hauke Sprink,Phillipo Lappicy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hořava-Lifshitz引力中真空各向同性模型的动力学行为，重点分析其对广义相对论（GR）的扰动（参数v≈1/2），证明了Bianchi II型异宿链在小扰动下具有鲁棒性，并在超临界情形（v∈(1/2,1)）中证明了每个异宿链存在稳定流形（定义于Cantor集上），特别讨论了周期为三和四的异宿链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Hořava-Lifshitz引力理论中时空奇点的一般行为，尤其是其与广义相对论的偏离如何影响经典宇宙学模型（如Bianchi模型）的动力学结构。

Method: 采用动力系统方法，分析Hořava-Lifshitz引力中空间均匀真空模型的相空间结构；利用扰动理论证明异宿链的鲁棒性；结合不变流形理论与Cantor集构造，证明超临界情形下稳定流形的存在性。

Result: 1) 所有Bianchi II型周期异宿链在v≈1/2时保持鲁棒；2) 在超临界情形v∈(1/2,1)下，每条异宿链均存在一个定义在Cantor集上的稳定流形；3) 对周期三和四的异宿链进行了详细刻画。

Conclusion: Hořava-Lifshitz引力在接近广义相对论极限时继承了其部分混沌奇点结构，但在超临界区域展现出新的、具有分形特征的稳定动力学行为，暗示该理论中奇点可能具有更丰富的几何与动力学性质。

Abstract: We consider vacuum spatially homogeneous models in Hořava-Lifshitz gravity, which are expected to describe generic singularities for these modified gravity theories. These models perturb the well-known Bianchi models in general relativity (GR) by a parameter $v\in (0,1)$, in which GR occurs at $v=1/2$. We show that all periodic heteroclinic chains of Bianchi type II orbits are robust for small perturbations of GR, i.e. they exist for any $v\approx 1/2$. Moreover, we prove the existence of a stable manifold for each heteroclinic chain within a Cantor set for the supercritical case, $v\in (1/2,1)$. In particular, we discuss the heteroclinic chains of periods three and four in detail.

</details>


### [166] [On the Mathematical Foundations of Gravitational Decoupling in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18380)
*Andrea Giusti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在一类全局静态、球对称时空中的引力解耦的代数与几何结构，揭示了几何变形构成群胚，并建立了其与逆形变变换的联系，证明最小几何变形总可表示为以面积半径为辅助标量的逆形变形式，而扩展变形则存在全局障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解引力解耦在静态球对称时空中的内在代数与几何结构，尤其是几何变形与更广义的时空变换（如逆形变）之间的关系。

Method: 基于固定类全局静态、球对称时空（且面积半径微分非零），分析最小与扩展几何变形的代数结构（群胚性质），并系统建立其与逆形变变换的对应关系，辅以微分几何与张量分析工具。

Result: 1) 最小与扩展几何变形构成偏变换群胚；2) 所有允许的最小几何变形均可由面积半径作为辅助标量实现逆形变表示；3) 扩展变形存在一个必要条件及在保持面积半径和静态Killing方向（至常数归一化）的微分同胚下仍存在的全局障碍；4) 逆形变构造能复现标准最小几何变形的源方程与分离守恒律（含各向异性种子解）。

Conclusion: 引力解耦中的几何变形具有深刻的代数（群胚）与几何（逆形变可表征性）结构；最小变形具有良好可表征性，而扩展变形受本质几何障碍限制，表明其物理适用性存在固有约束。

Abstract: We study the algebraic and geometric structures of gravitational decoupling within a fixed class of globally static, spherically symmetric spacetimes with non-vanishing areal-radius differential. We show that (minimal and extended) geometric deformations of the seed metric define a groupoid of partial transformations. We also draw a connection between the geometric deformation procedures and disformal transformations. It is shown that every admissible minimal geometric deformation admits a disformal representation using the areal radius as an auxiliary scalar. For extended deformations, we derive a necessary condition and exhibit a global obstruction that persists under diffeomorphisms preserving the areal radius and the static Killing direction up to constant normalization. Finally, we verify that the disformal construction reproduces the standard minimal geometric deformation source equations and separate conservation law, including anisotropic seeds.

</details>


### [167] [Tidal Excitation of Relativistic Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18475)
*Zhiqiang Miao,Junquan Su,Neev Khera,Huan Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个因果相对论理论，统一处理了相对论恒星对时变潮汐响应的各个组成部分，包括直接响应、准正规模和晚期尾部响应，并通过 retarded Green 函数的谱分解和复双线性形式实现了完整的模态-非模态分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论中，潮汐场具有规范依赖性、辐射模是非厄米且不完备的，且直接响应与晚期响应本质上是非模态的，因此缺乏牛顿力学中正常模展开那样的第一性原理对应理论。

Method: 发展了匹配恒星内部–史瓦西外部的 retarded Green 函数的谱分解，定义了时域中的四个区域；引入复双线性形式于全度规–流体相空间，将非旋转完美流体恒星的线性响应分解为直接、准正规模和晚期尾部三部分；每部分均满足显式的首阶驱动演化方程。

Result: 实现了时空与流体扰动的完整模态–非模态分解，物理上可描述为离散频率与连续频率两组振子的动力学；数值验证了该分解在一般时变潮汐驱动下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该理论首次在相对论框架下统一、因果地刻画了恒星潮汐响应的全部成分，为黑洞微扰等应用提供了新工具和新视角。

Abstract: How a relativistic star responds to a time-dependent tide has lacked a first-principles counterpart of the Newtonian normal-mode expansion. In general relativity, the tidal field is gauge sensitive, radiative modes are non-Hermitian and incomplete, and the direct and late-time responses are intrinsically nonmodal. In this work we present, to our knowledge, the first causal relativistic theory that treats all of these components on equal footing. We first develop the spectral decomposition of the retarded Green function of the matched stellar interior--Schwarzschild exterior, which classifies four separate regimes in time domain: acausal region, prompt wave, surface-reflected wave, and mode+tail. The prompt signal is identical to that of a Schwarzschild black hole with the same mass, which is combined with the surface-reflected wave to be referred as the "direct part". With the Green function and a complex bilinear form on the full metric--fluid phase space, we decompose the linear response of a nonrotating perfect-fluid star into direct, quasinormal-mode, and late-time-tail sectors. Crucially, every sector admits an explicit first-order driven evolution equation: discrete bilinear projections determine the dynamically evolving quasinormal-mode amplitudes, while continuum projections of the same tidal source onto branch-cut states determine the direct and tail amplitudes. The result is a complete modal--nonmodal decomposition of the spacetime and fluid perturbations, which is physically described by the dynamics of a discrete and a continuous-frequency set of oscillators. We verify the decomposition numerically under generic time-dependent tidal driving and discuss applications to black hole perturbations.

</details>


### [168] [Linear Response to Black Hole Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18521)
*Stefan Hohenegger*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性响应理论的通用形式主义，用于计算球对称静态黑洞周围可观测量（如黑洞阴影、最内稳定圆轨道半径、标量拟正则模谱等）相对于史瓦西解的主阶修正，修正阶数由爱因斯坦张量的一个特征值ℰ标定；该ℰ可类比为激发几何变形的外源能动量张量，并与有效新物理模型相联系，从而实现观测约束与理论模型之间的双向桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论之外的引力修正对黑洞物理影响微妙且模型依赖性强；现有有效几何描述虽高效但常缺乏通往有效新物理的清晰路径。

Method: 受线性响应理论启发，引入爱因斯坦张量的特征值ℰ作为小参数，将偏离史瓦西度规的几何变形视为由等效外源能动量张量ℰ所激发；在此框架下系统计算各类黑洞邻域可观测量的主阶修正。

Result: 成功推导出黑洞阴影半径、最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）半径及标量场拟正则模频率等关键可观测量的一阶修正表达式，并指出这些观测结果可反向约束ℰ，进而限制有效物理模型。

Conclusion: 该形式主义为连接黑洞精细观测与基础引力新物理提供了通用、模型无关的桥梁，既便于理论预测，也利于从观测中提取有效物理信息。

Abstract: Modifications of gravity beyond (vacuum) General Relativity have a subtle impact on the physics around black holes. Describing these effects across different observables is, however, a priori strongly model dependent. While effective descriptions of the geometry allow to efficiently capture deviations from benchmark predictions, they sometimes lack a clear passage towards (effective) new physics. Inspired by linear response theory, in this work I therefore propose a formalism that allows to calculate leading corrections of observables relative to the Schwarzschild black hole anywhere in space-time (outside of the horizon): the order of corrections is fixed through an eigenvalue $\mathcal{E}$ of the Einstein tensor, which is identified with an external energy momentum tensor that generates the deformation of the geometry. In this ways, $\mathcal{E}$ can be linked to effective physics models. Focusing on spherically symmetric and static black holes, I illustrate the approach at the hand of a number of observables in the vicinity of the horizon, such as characteristic quantities of particles moving around the black hole (e.g. the black hole shadow or the radius of the innermost stable circular orbit) or the spectrum of scalar quasi-normal modes. Reversing the logic, I argue that the knowledge of such observables provides non-trivial constraints on $\mathcal{E}$, which in turn grant insights into the effective physics in the vicinity of the black hole.

</details>


### [169] [Dynamical stability and observational constraints on a late-time dark-matter particle-creation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18531)
*Anirban Chatterjee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含辐射、重子、暗物质（DM）和独立守恒暗能量的平直FLRW宇宙中，引力粒子产生对宇宙动力学的影响；将DM建模为具有内压和粒子产生负压的有效开放热力学流体，并通过变量u=H₀/(H₀+H)构建四维自治系统，分析其临界点、稳定性与物理可容性；结合多组天文观测数据约束模型，发现观测偏好一个稳定、物理可容、暗能量主导的加速吸引子，排除了非平凡的DM主导类德西特分支；演化序列重现了辐射-物质-加速三阶段，过渡红移z_tr≈0.71–0.73，约92%–97%样本支持该吸引子；远期渐近行为趋近于类精质幂律膨胀（H→0），而非德西特态；成果建立了观测约束、引力粒子产生与宇宙远期全局动力学之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力粒子产生如何影响宇宙整体演化，特别是暗物质作为开放热力学流体的动力学行为，及其与当前加速膨胀和远期宇宙命运的关联。

Method: 引入紧凑变量u=H₀/(H₀+H)，构建描述辐射、重子、暗物质和暗能量共存宇宙的四维自治动力学系统；系统分析临界点、线性稳定性及物理可容性；利用PPS+BAO+OHD、Union3+BAO+OHD和DES-DOV+BAO+OHD三组观测数据对模型参数进行贝叶斯约束与后验分析。

Result: 观测数据排除了非平凡的暗物质主导类德西特解；确认存在一个稳定、物理可容、暗能量主导的加速吸引子；宇宙演化成功再现辐射–物质–加速三阶段，过渡红移z_tr≈0.71–0.73；92%–97%的后验样本支持该吸引子；远期渐近行为为H→0的类精质幂律膨胀，而非德西特态。

Conclusion: 引力粒子产生机制可自然导出符合观测的加速膨胀吸引子，且其远期宇宙命运由幂律衰减主导，这为理解暗物质本质、加速机制及宇宙终极演化提供了新视角，并建立起观测、粒子产生与全局动力学之间的紧密联系。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological dynamics of gravitational particle creation in a spatially flat FLRW Universe comprising radiation, baryons, dark matter (DM), and a separately conserved dark-energy component. The DM sector is described as an effective open thermodynamic fluid incorporating intrinsic pressure and particle-creation-induced negative pressure. Introducing the compact variable $u=H_0/(H_0+H)$, we construct a four-dimensional autonomous system and systematically investigate its critical points, dynamical stability, and physical admissibility. The model is constrained using three observational combinations: PPS+BAO+OHD, Union3+BAO+OHD, and DES-DOV+BAO+OHD. The observationally preferred parameter regions exclude the nontrivial DM-dominated de Sitter-like branches from the physical phase space, while identifying a stable, physically admissible, dark-energy-dominated accelerating attractor. The reconstructed cosmological evolution consistently reproduces the radiation--matter--acceleration sequence, with a transition redshift $z_{\rm tr}\simeq0.71$--$0.73$. Posterior-level analysis reveals that approximately $92\%$--$97\%$ of the samples support this accelerating attractor. The asymptotic evolution approaches a quintessence-like power-law expansion with $H\rightarrow0$, rather than a de Sitter state. These findings establish a connection between observational constraints, gravitational particle creation, and the global dynamical structure governing the distant cosmic future.

</details>


### [170] [Mini-Superspace Hamiltonian Dynamics and Canonical Quantization of Bumblebee Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18542)
*Pranjal Sarmah*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Bumblebee引力理论下，均匀各向同性宇宙的经典与量子哈密顿系统，通过最小超空间拉格朗日量推导出含Lorentz破缺的哈密顿约束，并分析其动力学稳定性与相空间行为；进一步构造Wheeler-DeWitt方程并求得深量子和近真空区域的近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lorentz对称性自发破缺（由bumblebee矢量场真空期望值引发）对宇宙学演化的影响，统一构建经典哈密顿框架与量子宇宙学描述。

Method: 从四维协变作用量出发，推导平直FLRW背景下的1维mini-superspace拉格朗日密度（仅含时间分量bumblebee场）；通过勒让德变换构建经典哈密顿系统，识别lapse函数为拉格朗日乘子以施加哈密顿约束；应用Liouville定理、临界点分析与稳定性判据检验系统；导出修正Friedmann方程并数值分析相空间；最后通过正则量子化得到Wheeler-DeWitt方程并求其渐近解。

Result: 获得含Lorentz破缺的修正Friedmann方程及有效引力耦合的时间演化；确定并分类相空间临界点，验证Lorentz破缺真空的稳定性；构造Wheeler-DeWitt方程，并在a→0（深量子）和近真空区域得到其近似解析解。

Conclusion: Bumblebee引力在经典与量子层面均可自洽地描述宇宙演化，Lorentz破缺对早期宇宙动力学与量子行为具有可观测影响，该哈密顿框架为检验量子引力效应提供了新途径。

Abstract: The work presents a canonical Hamiltonian system in a classical scenario and further extend the analysis to a quantum mechanical framework for a homogeneous and isotropic universe governed by Bumblebee gravity, in which spontaneous Lorentz symmetry breaking is triggered by a vector field acquiring a vacuum expectation value (VEV). Starting from the full four-dimensional covariant action, the derivation has been carried out for the one-dimensional mini-superspace Lagrangian density for a flat Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) background with a purely temporal bumblebee field configuration. Further, the work focuses on to construct the classical Hamiltonian system through a canonical Legendre transformation, identifying the lapse function $N(t)$ as a Lagrange multiplier enforcing a primary Hamiltonian constraint. Apart form that, the conservation of phase-space volume via Liouville's theorem, locate and classify the critical points of the resulting dynamical system, and examine the stability of the Lorentz-violating vacuum has been carried to test the classical Hamiltonian system in the work. Furthermore, the modified first and second Friedmann equations and discuss the resulting time-dependence of the effective gravitational coupling, illustrating the results with numerical phase-space has been discussed in this work. Finally, the work extended to construct the Wheeler-DeWitt equation through the canonical quantization and obtain the approximate solutions in the deep-quantum ($a\to0$) and near-vacuum regimes for it.

</details>


### [171] [Noncommutative BTZ Gravastar in Gauss-Bonnet Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18752)
*M. A. Anacleto,A. T. N. Silva,L. Casarini,B. R. Carvalho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个(2+1)维的gravastar模型，内部采用含高斯-博内修正的非对易几何描述，外部则由高斯-博内修正的BTZ解描述，并通过广义拼接形式将两者在薄壳处连接，进而研究其能量条件与热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在低维时空下结合非对易几何与高斯-博内引力修正的gravastar模型，以检验其物理自洽性及热力学行为。

Method: 采用广义拼接形式连接内外两种几何，在薄壳处导出物态方程；分析壳层上的能量条件（零能、弱能、强能条件）；计算非对易内部区域的温度、熵和热容等热力学量。

Result: 得到了满足各类能量条件的参数区域；获得了非对易内部区域的热力学量表达式，揭示了其热稳定性特征。

Conclusion: 该(2+1)维gravastar模型在特定参数范围内可同时满足经典能量条件并具有合理的热力学行为，为理解量子引力效应下的致密天体提供了新视角。

Abstract: We constructed a (2+1)-dimensional gravastar whose interior region was characterized by a noncommutative geometry with Gauss-Bonnet corrections, while the exterior region was described by a Gauss-Bonnet-modified BTZ solution. The two geometries were joined across a thin shell using the generalized junction formalism, from which we derived the surface equations of state. We investigated the energy conditions at the shell, determining the parameter regimes under which the null, weak, and strong energy conditions were satisfied. We also analyzed the thermodynamic properties of the noncommutative interior region, including the temperature, entropy, and heat capacity.

</details>


### [172] [Exact two-loop gravitational tail integrals and their ultraviolet-infrared structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18757)
*Gabriel Luz Almeida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了多极有效场论中引力尾-尾修正所涉及的一类非可分解标量积分的精确闭式解，并在一般维度下给出其费曼、推迟和超前传播子的解析延拓形式；同时通过动量区域分析，独立确定了紫外（UV）和红外（IR）发散的留数，验证了IR极点结构与单圈引力尾的指数化一致，并复现了多极反常维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力尾-尾修正是辐射在源质量单极场中两次散射所致，在多极有效场论中，其中一类两圈图具有真正耦合的圈动量，无法通过嵌套单圈积分求解，导致出现随多极阶数增长而分子幂次无界的标量积分族，亟需系统解析方法。

Method: 采用张量约化处理耦合圈图，推导适用于任意整数分子幂、通用维度d的标量积分精确闭式解；通过一致解析延拓涵盖费曼、推迟和超前传播子；在d=3+ε附近展开至O(ε)精度；结合动量区域分析（硬区、软区、迟滞区等）区分UV与IR发散来源，并应用于任意电/磁多极的尾-尾辐射图。

Result: 获得整个相关整数分子幂域的精确闭式积分表达式；给出d=3+ε下含O(ε)项的显式洛朗展开；明确区分并计算了尾-尾图的UV与IR留数；验证IR双极点与单极点结构完全符合单圈引力尾的指数化预言；UV留数结合其余图贡献复现出已知的多极反常维数。

Conclusion: 本文为高阶引力辐射计算提供了关键积分工具，首次系统解决了非可分解两圈尾-尾积分的解析问题，并通过动量区域分析严格厘清了UV/IR起源，不仅验证了红外指数化结构的普适性，也为后续更高阶后牛顿与后闵可夫斯基计算奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Gravitational tail-of-tail corrections arise when radiation scatters twice from the mass-monopole field of its source. In the multipolar effective field theory, one of the two-loop topologies contributing to this process involves genuinely coupled loop momenta, preventing its evaluation through nested one-loop integrations. After tensor reduction, this nonfactorizing structure leads to a particularly challenging family of scalar integrals with unbounded numerator powers as the multipole rank increases. We derive exact closed-form expressions for the entire relevant integer numerator domain, valid in generic dimension and accommodating Feynman, retarded, and advanced prescriptions through consistent analytic continuation. Near $d=3+ε$, we also obtain explicit Laurent expansions through $O(ε)$, retaining the subleading information needed in higher-order radiative calculations. While these expansions determine the dimensional poles, identifying their ultraviolet (UV) or infrared (IR) origin requires a separate analysis of momentum regions. We carry out this analysis throughout the integral domain and apply it directly to the coupled tail-of-tail emission diagram for arbitrary electric and magnetic multipoles. In this way, we independently determine the UV and IR residues, and explicitly verify that the complete infrared pole structure, including both double and simple poles, agrees with the exponentiation of the one-loop gravitational tail. The UV residues, calculated directly from hard-momentum regions, reproduce the established multipole anomalous dimensions when combined with the known contributions of the remaining diagrams.

</details>


### [173] [Testing compact binary nature in eccentric orbits with third-generation detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18833)
*Syed U. Naqvi,Kaushik Paul,Chandra Kant Mishra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了自旋诱导四极矩（SIQM）的对称性检验至偏心、共线自旋双星系统，并利用第三代引力波探测器（如Cosmic Explorer和Einstein Telescope）进行可测性预测；结果表明偏心率对SIQM测量精度的影响依赖于双星参数与探测器配置，且Einstein Telescope因低频偏心周期更多而将SIQM不确定性减半，同时指出忽略偏心会导致模板匹配失效，强调在3G探测中纳入偏心效应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力波对自旋诱导四极矩（SIQM）的检验多基于近圆轨道假设，但动力学形成通道可能产生在第三代探测器敏感频段内仍可观测的偏心率，因此需拓展SIQM检验至偏心双星系统以更严格检验Kerr假说。

Method: 采用后牛顿自旋-偏心相位模型，将对称SIQM检验推广至偏心、共线自旋双星，并结合Fisher矩阵单事件预报与截断偏心展开的种群研究，在Cosmic Explorer和Einstein Telescope背景下评估SIQM参数Δκ_s的测量精度；同时分析准圆模板库对中等偏心信号的有效性损失。

Result: 单事件Fisher预报显示偏心对SIQM测量精度的影响非单调劣化，而种群分析表明Einstein Telescope相较Cosmic Explorer可将Δκ_s不确定性中位数由0.20–0.23降至0.11–0.12；准圆模板库在中等偏心下显著失效，证实需构建偏心-自旋联合模板族。

Conclusion: 在第三代引力波探测中，纳入轨道偏心效应对精确测量SIQM、避免搜寻系统误差、强化致密天体本质检验均至关重要。

Abstract: The spin-induced quadrupole moment (SIQM) of a compact object provides a direct null test of the Kerr hypothesis. Most existing gravitational-wave tests of SIQM assume quasi-circular binaries, although dynamical formation channels can produce measurable eccentricity that may persist in the sensitive band of third-generation (3G) detectors. We extend the symmetric SIQM test to eccentric, aligned-spin binaries using a post-Newtonian spinning-eccentric phasing model, and forecast its measurability with Cosmic Explorer (CE) and Einstein Telescope (ET). Single-event Fisher-matrix forecasts show that the impact of eccentricity on SIQM measurement precision depends on the binary parameters and detector configuration, rather than uniformly degrading the measurement. A population study with a truncated eccentric expansion indicates that ET's additional low-frequency eccentric cycles tighten $Δκ_s$ by roughly a factor of two relative to CE (median $0.11$ to $0.12$ versus $0.20$ to $0.23$); the absolute values are indicative only. We further show that quasi-circular template banks can lose significant effectualness for moderately eccentric signals, motivating the development of eccentric-spin template families. Accounting for eccentricity is therefore important both for SIQM measurements and for avoiding systematic losses in searches, and it strengthens tests of the nature of compact objects with 3G gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [174] [Black hole sensitivity in Einstein--aether theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00503)
*Morgan Kidnew,Andrew Kovachik,Sergey Sibiryakov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文数值求解了爱因斯坦-以太理论中缓慢运动的史瓦西黑洞解，并基于点粒子有效理论计算了黑洞与以太场耦合的敏感性参数，同时给出了该参数对以太理论参数依赖关系的简单解析近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究爱因斯坦-以太理论中黑洞的性质，特别是其与以太场的耦合行为，以检验该理论在强引力场下的可行性及可观测效应。

Method: 采用数值方法求解缓慢运动的 Schwarzschild 黑洞在 Einstein--aether 理论中的精确解，并在点粒子有效理论框架下提取黑洞的敏感性参数；进一步推导其对理论参数的解析依赖关系。

Result: 获得了缓慢运动黑洞的数值解，计算出敏感性参数，并给出其关于 Einstein--aether 参数的简单解析近似表达式。

Conclusion: 敏感性参数可定量刻画黑洞在 Einstein--aether 理论中的独特响应，其解析近似有助于后续引力波与脉冲星计时等观测约束。

Abstract: We numerically find the solutions for slowly moving Schwarzschild black holes in Einstein--aether theory. We use these solutions to compute the sensitivity parameter which describes the coupling of the black hole to the aether field within point-particle effective theory. A simple analytic approximation for the dependence of the sensitivity on the Einstein--aether parameters is derived.

</details>

### [175] [Perturbative Renormalization of Noether Currents in SYM with Improved Discretizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14242)
*Marios Costa,Elias Antoniou,Salomi Dimou,Constantinos Costa,Panos K. Chrysanthis,Gregoris Spanoudes,Haralambos Panagopoulos*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了在格点正则化下，N=1超对称杨-米尔斯理论中手征对称性和超对称性对应的Noether流的微扰重整化，计算了一圈阶的手征流和超流的重整化因子与混合系数，并探讨了改进的胶子/胶微子作用量及格点参数对手征/超对称恢复的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在格点模拟中恢复连续极限下的手征对称性和超对称性，需精确理解格点上Noether流（如手征流、超流）的重整化行为及算符混合效应，尤其在使用改进离散方案时。

Method: 采用一圈微扰计算方法，在多种改进格点作用量（含stout涂抹Wilson费米子、clover项、Symanzik改进胶子作用量）下，计算手征流与超流的重整化因子及与其它算符（包括规范不变与非不变算符）的混合系数。

Result: 得到了一阶微扰下超流与多个算符的混合系数及重整化因子；发现改进的胶子/胶微子作用量及适当选取clover/stout系数可有效压制一类格点误差；结果可用于Ward恒等式实施与格点-连续匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作为格点超对称理论中对称性恢复提供了关键微扰输入，表明通过精细调控格点离散方案可显著改善超对称性的实现。

Abstract: We investigate the perturbative renormalization of Noether currents associated with chiral symmetry and supersymmetry transformations in $\mathcal{N}=1$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, regularized on the lattice using improved discretizations. In particular, we study the chiral current and the supercurrent operator for a broad class of lattice actions: Wilson fermions with one-step stout-smeared links and a clover term, together with a family of Symanzik-improved gluon actions. Since the supercurrent mixes with both gauge-invariant and gauge-noninvariant operators, we compute the corresponding renormalization factors and mixing coefficients at one-loop order. We analyze the impact of improved gluon and gluino actions on this mixing pattern and investigate whether suitable choices of lattice parameters, such as the clover and stout coefficients, can reduce unwanted lattice artifacts at one loop. Our results provide perturbative input for the implementation of Ward identities and for matching lattice observables to their continuum counterparts, while also offering guidance for simulations aimed at restoring supersymmetry in the continuum limit.

</details>


### [176] [Impact of Window Observables on the Transversity Quark Parton Distribution Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16950)
*Daniel Adamiak,Christopher Monahan*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了格点QCD窗口观测量对核子等矢量横自旋部分子分布函数（PDFs）的约束能力，利用ePump Hessian更新框架，评估现有及未来格点计算对PDF全局拟合的改进潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验测得的横自旋PDF不确定性高达50–100%，而当前格点QCD计算已达到相当甚至更优精度，亟需量化其对PDF全局分析的实际约束力。

Method: 采用ePump Hessian-updating框架，系统评估格点QCD计算的横自旋窗口矩（window moments）和高斯窗口（Gaussian windows）对当前现象学PDF拟合的约束能力，并估算不同精度下PDF不确定性的降低幅度。

Result: 明确了哪些窗口观测量对PDF全局分析最具约束力，给出了当前及近未来格点精度下PDF不确定性的定量缩减估计。

Conclusion: 格点QCD横自旋窗口观测量有望显著提升PDF提取精度，本工作为后续格点与实验协同优化PDF提供了明确指导。

Abstract: We study the potential impact of lattice QCD window observables on nucleon isovector quark transversity parton distribution functions (PDFs) using the ePump Hessian-updating framework. Window observables, recently proposed in arXiv:2505.22795 as benchmark quantities enabling direct quantitative comparison between lattice QCD and phenomenological PDF extractions, are weighted integrals of PDFs over a range of Bjorken-$x$ for which both approaches are reliable. Experimental determinations of transversity PDFs carry uncertainties of 50--100%, while current lattice calculations have achieved comparable or better precision, creating an opportunity for lattice inputs to significantly constrain a global analysis. We evaluate the constraining power of existing lattice QCD computations of transversity window moments and Gaussian windows against current phenomenological PDF. We additionally examine prospective tensor charges in the transversity channel and estimate the PDF uncertainty reduction achievable at current and near-future lattice precision. Our results provide quantitative guidance on which window observables will most constrain global PDF analyses.

</details>


### [177] [Time-Domain Observables for Resonant Scattering in Quantum Simulations of Lattice Gauge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17470)
*Praveen Balaji,Patrick Draper,Enrico Rinaldi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了纯SU(3)格点规范理论中胶球散射问题，利用经典精确对角化方法刻画其散射行为，并提出适用于量子模拟的时间域可观测量；通过构建共享谱特征的玩具模型，在Quantinuum H2-2硬件上实现了含共振碰撞的近似模拟，验证了近期硬件上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为评估当前及近期量子硬件能力、测试新算法与可观测量，需构建日益复杂的基准问题；量子计算有望助力粒子物理中非平衡量子动力学（如散射过程）的研究。

Method: 采用经典精确对角化方法分析胶球散射；结合实时模拟与近似解析方法识别连续谱共振结构；提出时间域可观测量以从量子模拟中提取共振性质；构建具有相同谱特征的玩具模型并进行量子电路实现与优化。

Result: 成功刻画了线状plaquette格点上最低能胶球的散射与共振结构；提出了可行的时间域可观测量；在Quantinuum H2-2（28量子比特）上模拟了玩具模型的共振碰撞，优化后Trotter电路每步约需25n个纠缠门。

Conclusion: 该工作为在近期量子硬件上模拟格点规范理论中的非平凡动力学提供了切实可行的路径，验证了时间域可观测量与玩具模型策略的有效性，推动了量子模拟在高能物理中的应用。

Abstract: Quantum computing may allow unprecedented access to nonequilibrium quantum dynamics, including problems of interest in particle physics, like the real-time development of scattering processes. Although fault-tolerant simulations at scale are still in the future, recent advancements toward early fault-tolerant simulations motivate building increasingly elaborate benchmark problems in order to assess state-of-the-art capabilities and test new algorithms and observables. In this work, we study glueball scattering on a line-of-plaquettes lattice in pure $SU(3)$ lattice gauge theory. We characterize scattering of the lowest-energy glueball states using classical exact diagonalization methods, identifying the continuum resonance structure using real-time simulations and approximate analytic methods, and we propose time-domain observables for extracting properties of the resonance from quantum simulations. We furthermore construct a toy model that shares the spectral features of the lattice gauge theory and demonstrate extraction of the observables. Feasibility on near-term hardware is demonstrated through simulation of a resonant collision of this toy model on Quantinuum System Model $\texttt{H2-2}$, with a 14-plaquette chain encoded in 28 qubits. The Trotterized circuit after optimization and compression requires $\sim 25n$ entangling gates for $n$ Trotter steps.

</details>


### [178] [Data Free Training of Diffusion Models for Lattice Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18863)
*Dennis Grinwald,Ankur Singha,Khaled Kahouli,Karl Jansen,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参考采样数据（如HMC生成的样本）即可训练扩散模型的方法，利用目标分布的未归一化密度和目标分数恒等式，通过重要性加权或自洽不动点方式实现数据免费训练，在2D φ⁴理论中验证了其在各相态下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  lattice field theory中平衡场构型采样计算成本高，传统扩散模型训练依赖目标分布的真实样本，又回到了原始采样难题，因此需摆脱对参考样本的依赖。

Method: 利用目标分数恒等式（target score identity），结合未归一化概率密度，设计重要性加权和无权重（自洽不动点）两种训练方案，使扩散模型可仅用自身生成样本进行训练。

Result: 在二维φ⁴理论的对称相、临界相和破缺相中成功验证，所提数据免费训练方法性能媲美基于HMC参考数据的训练方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型无需真实样本即可高效训练，为格点场论提供了一种可行的、计算友好的生成采样新范式。

Abstract: Sampling equilibrium field configurations remains a major computational bottleneck in lattice field theory, motivating the development of generative samplers that can produce independent configurations. Diffusion models are promising for this purpose, but their standard denoising score matching objective requires samples from the target distribution, seemingly reintroducing the original sampling problem. Here, we show that diffusion models can instead be trained from their own generated samples using the target score identity, provided access to the unnormalized probability density. Importance-weighted formulations of this identity yield a consistent target throughout training, while unweighted variants define a self-consistent fixed-point training scheme. We benchmark the different approaches in two-dimensional \(φ^4\) theory and identify effective choices that enable efficient training and sampling across the symmetric, critical, and broken phases. Ultimately, our data-free training approach achieves comparable performance to training on HMC reference data.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

### [179] [Semiclassical state on algebra of observables for de Sitter space and time reversal symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14175)
*Muxin Han,Yichen Hu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文解决了de Sitter静态补丁全息中由动力学时钟耦合导致的冯·诺依曼代数迹态无法复现半经典体关联函数的问题，通过引入‘重时钟态’并推广观测者哈密顿量（含正反向时钟），构建了满足半经典体关联的II₁型代数结构，并从布朗–库查尔尘埃场出发为该模型提供了场论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在de Sitter静态补丁全息中，将体量子场耦合到观测者的动力学时钟所导出的II₁型冯·诺依曼代数M₊上的唯一迹态无法复现半经典体关联函数，这构成了理论自洽性的关键障碍。

Method: 引入重时钟态（表征大惯性参考系），严格证明其下n点规范不变‘ dressed ’可观测量关联函数收敛于de Sitter体时空中的量子场关联；推广观测者哈密顿量为H̑_obs = |π̂|，导出扩展代数M ≅ M₊ ⊗ M₂(ℂ)，并构造时间反演不变子代数F；最后通过将爱因斯坦引力与布朗–库查尔尘埃场耦合，在G→0极限下沿观测者世界线做微扰约化，导出该哈密顿量。

Result: 1) 重时钟极限下，规范不变dressed可观测量的n点关联函数精确复现半经典体关联；2) 推广哈密顿量给出II₁因子M ≅ M₊ ⊗ M₂(ℂ)，时间反演实现为交换两个时钟取向的反幺正算符，其不变代数F亦为II₁型；3) 重时钟态可自然延拓至M和F，仍保持关联函数匹配；4) 从含尘埃场的引力理论中导出了H̑_obs = |π̂|，为其提供了场论基础。

Conclusion: 重时钟框架成功弥合了静态补丁全息中代数结构与半经典物理之间的鸿沟，统一处理正向/反向时间演化，并通过尘埃场模型将观测者动力学嵌入第一性原理引力理论，为de Sitter全息提供了更坚实、对称且可观测的代数表述。

Abstract: In de Sitter static patch holography, coupling bulk quantum fields to an observer's dynamical clock yields a type II$_1$ von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{M}_+$ of gauge-invariant observables. However, the unique tracial state on this algebra fails to reproduce semiclassical bulk correlation functions. We resolve this issue by introducing a family of heavy-clock states, representing an observer reference frame with heavy inertia. We prove that in the heavy-clock limit, $n$-point correlation functions of gauge-invariant dressed observables reproduce the bulk quantum field correlation functions on de Sitter spacetime. Furthermore, we investigate a generalized observer Hamiltonian $\widehat{H}_{\rm obs}=|\widehatπ|$ that incorporates both forward-running ($π\geq 0$) and backward-running ($π<0$) clocks. The resulting von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{M}$ of gauge-invariant observables remains a type~II$_1$ factor and is isomorphic to $\mathcal{M}_+\otimes M_2(\mathbb{C})$, where $M_2(\mathbb{C})$ is the algebra of $2\times2$ complex matrices transforming between the two clock orientations. Time-reversal symmetry is implemented in this setting as an anti-unitary operator that exchanges the two clock orientations, yielding a type~II$_1$ von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{F}$ of gauge-invariant, time-reversal-invariant observables. We generalize the heavy-clock state construction to both $\mathcal{M}$ and $\mathcal{F}$, showing that $n$-point correlation functions calculated in heavy-clock states match the corresponding semiclassical bulk correlation functions. Finally, by coupling Einstein gravity to the Brown--Kuchař dust field, we derive the observer Hamiltonian $\widehat{H}_{\rm obs}=|\widehatπ|$ from a perturbative reduction along the observer's worldline in the $G \to 0$ limit. This provides a field-theoretic foundation for the observer model developed in this work.

</details>


### [180] [Defect conformal field theories with free-in-the-bulk fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14176)
*Christopher P. Herzog,Daniel Lloyd-Jones*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维自由费米子体理论中二维共形缺陷的性质，通过体狄拉克方程严格约束缺陷CFT数据，揭示了双扭关系及其在单极缺陷低模中的例外情形，并以双线性流和Gross-Neveu型四次耦合为例展示了该结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自由费米子体理论中低维共形缺陷的约束结构及其对算符维度与三点函数的影响。

Method: 利用体狄拉克方程分析缺陷共形场论数据，结合单极缺陷、双线性流和Gross-Neveu型四次耦合等模型进行解析与微扰计算。

Result: 确定了体-缺陷OPE中算符维度；发现多数情况下满足双扭关系，但在最低单极模式中双影伙伴均可为幺正，其三点函数由影变换关联；在混合不动点处找到违反双扭条件但相关函数仍正则的相互作用例子。

Conclusion: 体狄拉克方程对二维共形缺陷施加强约束，双扭关系普遍成立，但单极缺陷构成关键例外；该框架为构造和理解缺陷CFT提供了新路径。

Abstract: We study codimension-two conformal defects for massless fermions that are free in a four-dimensional bulk, but interact on a two-dimensional defect. The bulk Dirac equation strongly constrains defect conformal field theory data: it fixes the dimensions of operators in the bulk-defect operator product expansion and, for bulk-defect-defect three-point functions, enforces a double-twist relation when only one member of a shadow pair is allowed by unitarity. The lowest modes of monodromy defects provide an important exception, since both shadow partners can be unitary and their three-point functions are instead related by a shadow transform.
  We illustrate these structures with defect interactions, including an exactly tractable bilinear flow and Gross-Neveu-type quartic couplings. At a perturbative mixed fixed point, we find an explicit interacting example in which the double-twist condition is violated and the two shadow channels combine to give a regular bulk-defect-defect correlation function.

</details>


### [181] [The dimensional reduction of the spin-3 and spin-4 partially broken Fronsdal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14284)
*D. Dalmazi,A. L. R. dos Santos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Kaluza-Klein紧致化，将高维部分破缺的Fronsdal自旋3和4模型降维，得到不含额外自由度的有质量高自旋场模型；其结构与Singh-Hagen模型不同但壳上等价，并展现出非平凡的质量为零极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建无鬼、协变且不含多余自由度的有质量高自旋（s=3,4）场理论模型，并理解其与低维质量less模型及Singh-Hagen模型的关系。

Method: 对D+1维部分破缺的Fronsdal自旋3和4模型实施Kaluza-Klein维度约化；构造协变的规范不变场组合以实现高导数描述；分析质量极限下的粒子谱与场混合特性。

Result: 得到D维有质量自旋3和4模型，无额外传播自由度；模型含壳上消失的二次项，区别于Singh-Hagen模型；质量为零极限下螺旋度态非平庸耦合，无法通过局域场重定义对角化。

Conclusion: 高自旋（s≥3）有质量模型在协变框架下天然呈现高导数结构，且其质量为零极限具有内在非局域性，揭示了高自旋场与低自旋（s≤2）本质差异。

Abstract: Here we perform a Kaluza-Klein dimensional reduction from $D+1$ to $D$ dimensions of a partially broken Fronsdal model for spin-3 and spin-4. The reduced models describe massive spin-3 and spin-4 particles without extra propagating modes. They differ from Singh-Hagen models by quadratic terms which vanish on shell, similarly to their higher dimensional massless parents. A covariant description of the reduced models in terms of gauge invariant field combinations naturally leads to higher derivative models, contrary to lower spin cases ($s\le 2$).
  For both $s=3$ and $s=4$ we have a rather peculiar massless limit. Although their particle content corresponds to the $\pm j$ helicities with $j=0,1, \cdots s$, as expected, they appear intertwined in a non trivial way preventing diagonalization through any local field redefinition.

</details>


### [182] [Three-dimensional vacua of $G_2$ flux compactifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14313)
*Aravind Aikot,Julian Choi,Timm Wrase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维N=1超对称有效理论中来自IIA、IIB/I型及杂化弦论在G2-结构空间紧致化下的超对称真空，推导出AdS3真空中的标量质量与二维共形场论中对应算符维度Δ的关系，并给出多种情形下Δ的上下界及精确值，同时指出Minkowski真空中存在体积-稀释子平直方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三维超对称真空结构及其在全息对偶中的意义，特别是AdS3/CFT2框架下标量谱与算符维度的联系，并探索不同弦论紧致化方案中共有的真空性质与限制。

Method: 利用超势齐次性与物理标量度规导出普适真空关系和轴子稳定化约束；将AdS3标量质量表示为对偶CFT2中算符的标度维数Δ；结合不同类型弦论（IIA、IIB/I、杂化）的紧致化机制与G2-结构几何特性进行分析。

Result: 得到IIA中AdS3真空的Δ_max下界≈7.5及B2轴子精确维度Δ_b=6；IIB/I与杂化AdS3真空中存在普适体积-稀释子模Δ_φs=4；IIB单态扭转支上该模Δ_φs=20/7且Δ_b=16/7；所有经典闭弦Minkowski真空均含精确体积-稀释子平直方向；识别出各向同性尺度分离的扭转障碍。

Conclusion: 不同弦论框架下三维超对称真空展现出深刻的普适性，尤其体现在轴子稳定、标量谱与对偶CFT维度的严格关联上；Minkowski真空的平直方向提示其对量子修正敏感；尺度分离受几何扭转显著制约。

Abstract: We study supersymmetric vacua of three-dimensional $\mathcal N=1$ effective theories from type IIA, type IIB/type I, and heterotic compactifications on $G_2$-structure spaces. Superpotential homogeneity and the physical scalar metric yield universal vacuum relations and axion stabilization constraints without specifying the full moduli-space metric. We express AdS$_3$ scalar masses through $Δ$, the scaling dimension of the corresponding operator in the dual two-dimensional conformal field theory. In massive type IIA AdS$_3$ vacua, we derive the bound $Δ_{\max}\geq(20+4\sqrt{67})/7\approx 7.5$ and an exact dimension $Δ_b=6$ for $B_2$ axions on the torsion-free branch. Type IIB type I and heterotic AdS$_3$ vacua contain a universal volume-dilaton mode with $Δ_{φs}=4$. On the singlet-torsion type IIB branch, we also obtain the volume-dilaton mode with $Δ_{φs}=20/7$ and $Δ_b=16/7$. Every supersymmetric Minkowski vacuum in the retained classical closed-string theories possesses an exact volume-dilaton flat direction. We characterize physical large-flux limits after tadpole constraints and axionic identifications, and identify torsion obstructions to isotropic scale separation.

</details>


### [183] [One-loop QED effective action at finite temperature and density beyond HTL limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14345)
*Farahmand Hasanov,Nikita Kolganov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算有限温、有限化学势下标量和旋量QED中非局域单圈有效作用量的框架，基于热核展开，得到含多对数函数及其阶导数的闭式表达式，并推导了光子极化算子及其静态极限下的德拜屏蔽质量和电磁磁化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一个能处理有限温度和化学势下非局域量子电动力学效应的通用计算框架，克服软动量近似等限制。

Method: 基于热核展开至背景场二次项，将有效作用量形式因子归约为一个基本核；通过对外动量展开进行重求和，获得无质量情形下以多对数函数及其阶导数表示的闭式解，并推广至小质量展开任意阶。

Result: 得到了保留完整外欧氏动量、温度与化学势依赖性的闭式有效作用量表达式；导出了光子极化算子，并在静态极限下计算出德拜屏蔽质量和电磁磁化率。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究有限温密QED系统的非局域量子效应提供了系统、精确且普适的工具，适用于任意偶数维时空。

Abstract: We develop a framework for computing the nonlocal one-loop effective action in scalar and spinor QED at finite temperature and chemical potential in arbitrary even spacetime dimensions. Based on the heat kernel expansion to quadratic order in background fields, the framework reduces the effective action form factors to a common fundamental kernel. By resumming the external-momentum expansion, we obtain explicit closed-form expressions for the massless form factors in terms of polylogarithms and their derivatives with respect to the order. The same method determines the coefficients of the small-mass expansion to arbitrary order. The resulting expressions retain the full dependence on external Euclidean momentum, temperature, and chemical potential, without invoking the soft-momentum approximation. We also derive the corresponding photon polarization operators and evaluate their static limits, obtaining the Debye screening masses and electromagnetic susceptibilities.

</details>


### [184] [Characteristic Modes of Non-minimally Coupled Thick Branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14430)
*Jing-Xin Gong,Heng Guo,Yong-Tao Lu,Xin-Yi Pan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非最小耦合标量场厚膜模型中的特征模，分析了张量扰动方程，考察了引力子零模的局域化及共振KK模和准正规模（QNMs）的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
