Motivation: 特征模能揭示引力系统的内在属性和动力学响应，本文旨在探究非最小耦合标量场厚膜模型中特征模的特性，特别是零模局域化、KK共振模及准正规模行为。

Method: 通过分析张量扰动方程，研究两类厚膜模型（单扭结与变形扭结）中引力子零模的局域化；计算并对比频域与时域下的准正规模；分析不同非最小耦合参数ξ和形变参数k对共振KK模的影响。

Result: 两类模型中引力子零模均局域在膜上；单扭结模型在ξ=1/7时无明显KK共振模；变形扭结模型在ξ=0.99时，增大形变参数k可增加共振模数量并延长首阶共振寿命；频域与时域所得QNFs一致，且KK共振模质量与衰减率分别对应QNFs实部与虚部绝对值。

Conclusion: 厚膜结构与非最小耦合参数显著影响特征模谱，尤其是KK共振与准正规模特性，为理解高维引力模型中的模稳定性与可观测效应提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Characteristic modes provide important information about the intrinsic properties and dynamical response of gravitational systems. In this work, we investigate characteristic modes of thick branes with a non-minimally coupled bulk scalar field. Two representative thick brane solutions are considered, corresponding to a single-kink scalar configuration and a deformed kink configuration with internal structure, respectively. By analyzing the tensor perturbation equations, we first examine the localization of the graviton zero mode in the two brane models. For both brane models, the graviton zero mode is localized on the brane. No pronounced resonant Kaluza--Klein (KK) modes are found for the single-kink brane at $ξ=1/7$. For the deformed brane, we investigate the resonant KK modes at $ξ=0.99$, finding that increasing the deformation parameter $k$ leads to more resonant modes and a longer-lived first resonance.
  We then calculate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of both brane models independently in the frequency and time domains. The quasinormal frequencies (QNFs) obtained by the independent methods agree well for the low overtones. The masses and decay rates of the resonant KK modes agree closely with the real parts and the magnitudes of the imaginary parts of the corresponding QNFs, respectively.

</details>


### [185] [Haagerup conformal field theory at central charge eight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14441)
*Tzu-Chen Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个中心荷为8的左手代数U，并证明其强有理性和完全幺正性，同时揭示了其表示范畴与Haagerup融合范畴H3的Drinfeld中心等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索顶点算子代数与融合范畴之间的深层联系，特别是通过构造特定左手代数来理解Haagerup范畴的物理实现。

Method: 在格顶点算子代数V_{E_6⊕A_2}中构造左手代数U，利用两个交换的仿射sl_2电流代数、应力张量及一个由三个格指数生成的权为2场；结合表示论与范畴论分析其性质。

Result: 证明U是强有理且完全幺正的；Rep(U)作为幺正辫子范畴等价于Haagerup融合范畴H3的Drinfeld中心Z(H3)；其特征标为Evans--Gannon向量γ=0；由此导出对角共形场论和受H3拓扑缺陷作用的全纯E8扩张。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了左手代数U与Haagerup范畴Z(H3)之间的精确对应，为融合范畴的共形场论实现提供了新范例，并拓展了对E8结构与拓扑缺陷的理解。

Abstract: We construct a chiral algebra $U$ of central charge eight inside the lattice vertex operator algebra $V_{E_6\oplus A_2}$. It is generated by two commuting affine $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ current algebras at levels $1$ and $39$, the stress tensor of the lattice theory, and a single weight-two field built from three lattice exponentials. We announce that $U$ is strongly rational and completely unitary, and that $Rep(U)$ is equivalent, as a unitary ribbon category, to the Drinfeld center $Z(\mathcal{H}_3)$ of the Haagerup fusion category $\mathcal{H}_3$. Its characters are the $γ=0$ Evans--Gannon vector. From $U$ we obtain a diagonal full CFT, and also a holomorphic $E_8$ extension on which $\mathcal{H}_3$ acts by topological defects.

</details>


### [186] [PINNs for N=2* holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14461)
*Alex Buchel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了能否仅从热力学状态方程重构全息理论，利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）在Pilch-Warner模型的玻色质量扇区中进行研究，发现高精度高温态方程可有效约束体标量势的非线性相互作用，结合体粘滞度数据可显著提升重构精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究是否能仅从热力学状态方程唯一确定全息对偶中的体理论（特别是标量势），即热力学信息对体物理的约束能力。

Method: 采用物理信息神经网络（PINN），结合热力学、紫外渐近行为、Ward恒等式和视界正则性等物理约束，重建非线性黑膜几何与标量势，并通过前向求解验证重构精度。

Result: 高精度高温态方程可显著约束标量势的三次耦合；固定该耦合至PW精确值使标量自相互作用重构误差降低约15倍；加入体粘滞度数据后误差再降约13倍，且前向计算复现体粘滞度误差仅0.09%。

Conclusion: 热力学数据（尤其是高温展开和输运系数）对全息体理论具有强约束力，但仅拟合热力学不足以保证标量势的准确重构，需融合多源物理约束。

Abstract: Can a holographic theory be reconstructed from its equation of state? We investigate this question using physics-informed neural networks in the bosonic-mass sector of the Pilch--Warner model. We first validate the PINN approach against an analytic solution encoding the leading high-temperature corrections to the conformal equation of state. We then reconstruct nonlinear black-brane geometries and the scalar potential using thermodynamics, ultraviolet asymptotics, Ward identities, and horizon regularity. Independent solutions of the bulk Einstein-scalar equations demonstrate that accurately reproducing thermodynamics with a trained PINN does not guarantee an accurate reconstruction of the scalar potential. High-precision knowledge of the high-temperature equation of state constrains nonlinear interactions in the bulk scalar potential. Fixing the cubic coupling to its exact PW value, motivated by this high-temperature extraction, reduces the maximum sampled error in the reconstructed scalar self-interactions by a factor of $\sim 15$ compared with constraining only the bulk scalar mass. Adding knowledge of the bulk viscosity of the gauge theory plasma further reduces this error by an additional factor of $\sim 13$, with independent forward solutions reproducing the bulk viscosity within $0.09\%$.

</details>


### [187] [Bondi Four-Momentum Balance in Quantum NSF Classical matching, DKL supermomentum, and quantum energy redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14484)
*Carlos Kozameh,Gerardo DEpaola*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在零面公式（NSF）的辐射相空间中推导了过去与未来零无穷之间的Bondi四动量守恒关系，结合Weyl匹配与端点News为零条件，得到Bondi质量角动量的对跖连续性；其平移分量构成Lorentz四矢量，并在特定辐射扇区中实现经典与量子层面的四动量守恒；进一步计算了出射能量期望值，在入射能量本征态下，二阶连通能量缺陷因轴向旋转协变性而消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解引力辐射过程中动量守恒的深层结构，尤其在经典与量子引力交界处如何协调Bondi动量演化的经典平衡与量子可观测量之间的关系。

Method: 基于零面公式（NSF）的辐射相空间分析，结合Weyl匹配于空间无穷远、端点News为零条件、Dominguez–Kozameh–Ludvigsen辐射扇区设定、Fock空间上的幺正共轭传输、以及经典动量平衡作为量子附加条件；辅以规范收缩、生成元代数与角动量投影技术。

Result: 1) Bondi质量角动量具有对跖连续性，其平移分量构成Lorentz四矢量；2) 在特定辐射扇区中，经典News通量相等，且可提升为量子四动量守恒；3) 对入射能量本征态，出射能量期望值的二阶连通能量缺陷在轴对称条件下严格为零。

Conclusion: 经典Bondi四动量平衡可自然延拓至量子辐射过程，且在合理物理前提（如有限螺旋度投影、轴向对称准备态）下，低阶能量缺陷消失，支持引力辐射中动量守恒的普适性；更高阶效应与含旁观者项的完整期望值仍待研究。

Abstract: We derive Bondi four-momentum balance between past and future null infinity in the radiative phase space of the null-surface formulation (NSF). Weyl matching at spatial infinity, together with vanishing endpoint News, gives antipodal continuity of the Bondi mass aspect. Its translation projection is independent of the infrared shear term and transforms as a Lorentz four-vector. Bondi evolution relates the incoming and outgoing News fluxes to the timelike endpoint momenta; in the Dominguez--Kozameh--Ludvigsen radiative sector these momenta vanish and the fluxes are equal. On a common radiative Fock domain, unitary conjugation transports the flux observables. Adopting their classical balance as an additional quantum condition yields exclusive four-momentum conservation. We also calculate the outgoing energy expectation without imposing that condition. For an incoming energy eigenstate, the second-generator commutator vanishes diagonally, leaving a second-order coefficient determined by the cubic generator. Direct canonical contractions isolate the contribution connected to all four external legs. For the equal-energy center-of-mass preparation, axial rotational covariance forces the angularly integrated exchange coefficient to vanish under the finite helicity-projection prescription of Part~I. The connected energy defect therefore vanishes through second order. The full expectation (including spectators) and higher-order balance remain open.

</details>


### [188] [A critical coupling for the magnetic instability of holographic SU(2) plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14675)
*Migael Strydom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有SU(2)全局对称性的全息等离子体中线性磁不稳定性如何被引力消除。在探针极限下，带电矢量玻色子在临界磁场以上会变得不稳定并凝聚成涡旋晶格；而在Einstein-SU(2)杨-米尔斯理论中，通过精确包含场的反作用（仅对凝聚态做展开），发现临界磁场在耦合参数α趋近于2/3时发散，此时正常相在任意磁场和温度下均无线性带电不稳定性。零温下该不稳定性对应于AdS₃×ℝ²喉部Breitenlohner-Freedman界限的破坏，而相变起始温度在α⋆处以BKT型本征奇异性消失。此外，给出了不同维度下正常相存在带电不稳定的C_J/C_T判据，并指出当α > 0.372时涡旋间引力导致均匀晶格失稳、相变为一级，B_c仅具自旋odal意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解引力如何影响全息等离子体中的磁不稳定性，特别是SU(2)对称性下的带电矢量场在强磁场下的行为及其相变机制。

Method: 在AdS₅中构建Einstein-SU(2)杨-米尔斯理论，精确包含磁场与规范场的背反应，围绕D'Hoker-Kraus磁brane对凝聚态做微扰展开；结合Landau能级分析、BF界判断、数值计算及BKT型相变理论分析临界行为。

Result: 发现临界磁场随耦合α→2/3而发散；α>2/3时正常相无任何线性带电不稳定性；零温下不稳定性源于AdS₃×ℝ²喉部BF界违反；起始温度在α⋆处以BKT型本征奇异性消失；给出d=4,5,6维中C_J/C_T判据；α>0.372时相变变为一级，B_c成为自旋odal点。

Conclusion: 引力可完全抑制全息等离子体的线性磁不稳定性，其效应由耦合常数α调控：当α超过临界值α⋆=2/3时，系统不再发生该类不稳定性；而当α进一步增大至α_L=0.372以上，凝聚相结构亦发生质变，暗示引力不仅稳定正常相，还深刻改变有序相的性质。

Abstract: We show that gravity can remove the linear magnetic instability of a holographic plasma with an $SU(2)$ global symmetry. In the probe limit, charged vector bosons become tachyonic in their lowest Landau level above a critical field and condense into a vortex lattice. In Einstein-$SU(2)$ Yang-Mills theory in AdS$_5$ we include the backreaction of the field exactly, expanding only in the condensate around the D'Hoker-Kraus magnetic brane. The critical field diverges as the coupling $α= κ^2/\hat g^2$ approaches $α_\star = 2/3$, above which the normal phase has no linear charged instability at any field or temperature: we show this at zero temperature and establish it numerically at finite temperature. At zero temperature the field cancels from the mass of the lowest Landau level in the brane's AdS$_3 \times \mathbb{R}^2$ throat, and the instability is a violation of the throat's Breitenlohner-Freedman bound. The onset temperature vanishes at $α_\star$ with an essential singularity of Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless type. For two-derivative Einstein-Yang-Mills duals in $d = 4$, 5 and 6 dimensions the normal phase has a charged instability if and only if $C_J/C_T < 8/(d^2(d+1))$, one tenth in $d = 4$. Above $α_L = 0.372$ the gravitational attraction between vortices makes the uniform lattice unstable and the transition first order, so $B_c$ is only a spinodal; whether a condensed phase survives above $α_\star$ is left open.

</details>


### [189] [Charged Deformation of a Hairy Black Brane: Perturbative Solution and Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14929)
*Sangheon Yun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维Einstein-Maxwell-标量理论中带电黑洞膜的性质，重点分析其非渐近AdS几何、热力学行为、输运性质及极端极限下的结构特征，并探讨了不同边界条件（共形与非共形）下系统的稳定性与相变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在缺乏紫外AdS固定点、标量场具对数发散行为的非共形引力背景中，带电黑膜的全息对偶性质、热力学稳定性及量子临界行为。

Method: 采用二阶微扰方法处理电荷展开；结合独立的微扰计算与非线性数值解验证；应用多迹边界条件与Dirichlet标量源条件对比分析；计算热力学量、输运系数（如不可相干电导率、四阶累积比）、准正规模及稳定性判据（如Buchel界、局部热力学稳定性）。

Result: 获得闭式表达的电磁场、磁化率、热力学量、凝聚态、不可相干电导率及负定的四阶累积比T²χ₄/χ₂=6c(α)<0；验证微扰与非线性结果高度一致；发现无有限标量值的正则极值视界，极值极限为AdS₂×ℝ²喉部且标量呈ln ln(1/u)增长；ρ/s严格小于√21/(2π)；引入Dirichlet标量源后系统呈现温度上限T_max(μ_Q)，并在该边界发生扩散不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了非共形全息系统中强关联系统输运与临界行为的可控范例；揭示了标量场红外动力学对热力学、输运和稳定性起决定性作用；极值极限与边界不稳定性共同刻画了量子临界相图的核心特征。

Abstract: We study electrically charged black branes in four-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell-scalar theory with Maxwell coupling $Z(φ)=e^{3φ/\sqrt7}$, whose potential has an AdS$_4$ fixed point only in the infrared. For nonzero hair parameter $α$ the scalar grows logarithmically at large radius and the geometry is not asymptotically AdS, so the holographic dictionary is a working assumption. With the scale-invariant multi-trace boundary condition of the neutral branes, giving conformal thermodynamics, we work to second order in the charge, obtaining the Maxwell field and susceptibility in closed form, plus the thermodynamics, condensate, incoherent conductivity and cumulant ratio $T^2χ_4/χ_2=6c(α)<0$ for all $α$ considered. Independent perturbative computations and first-law closed forms agree within $4\times10^{-6}$; nonlinear solutions reproduce second-order results to $1.2\times10^{-5}$ and $c$ to $6\times10^{-4}$. They are locally thermodynamically stable where constructed, and the pressure is analytic in $(μ_Q/T)^2$ near zero density. No regular extremal horizon exists at finite scalar value, and $ρ/s<\sqrt{21}/(2π)$ exactly. The extremal limit is an AdS$_2\times\mathbb R^2$ throat where the scalar grows as $\ln\ln(1/u)$ in the AdS$_2$ radial coordinate $u$; the bound is saturated there, and extremal values are approached as powers of $1/\ln(μ_Q/T)$. With a Dirichlet condition on the scalar source, the same solutions describe a non-conformal theory with scale $Λ$, whose charged branes exist only below $T_{\max}(μ_Q)$, which falls from $0.194Λ$ at zero density to zero at $μ_Q=0.506Λ$. This boundary is a diffusive instability where the charge susceptibility diverges at nonzero density, and the bulk viscosity violates Buchel's bound at all densities. Quasinormal modes with the normalizable large-radius condition reproduce its hydrodynamics.

</details>


### [190] [Holographic theory of non-invertible gravitational anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15358)
*Xiao-Gang Wen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用多体物理语言描述引力反常的全息理论：将反常视为无法在同维局域玻色晶格系统中实现低能数据的障碍；反常可由高一维的拓扑有序体态（可逆或非可逆）来分类和刻画，并揭示了对称性、引力反常与高一维拓扑序之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反常理论多基于场论连续极限，缺乏普适、离散、多体视角下的统一理解；本文旨在建立一个不依赖于连续极限、适用于一般局域量子系统的、基于晶格多体物理的引力反常全息描述框架。

Method: 采用多体局域哈密顿量框架，将反常定义为低能边界理论无法嵌入同维局域玻色晶格系统的 obstruction；通过构造高一维的 gapped 玻色体态（含可逆与非可逆拓扑序）作为‘全息体’，建立边界-体对应；引入域壁与可逆边界连接构造理论间 morphism 的 sandwich 图像；结合具体例子（手征玻色/费米子、Laughlin 边、ℤ₂ 高阶规范理论）分析反常信息在输运系数、超选择扇区、凝聚数据中的体现。

Result: 建立了引力反常的多体全息分类：可逆体对应传统反常流入，非可逆体对应新型非可逆反常；证明了对称性、引力反常与高一维拓扑序三者等价；给出了理论间 morphism 的物理实现（域壁+可逆边界），并揭示费米统计本身可视为一种特定非可逆引力反常。

Conclusion: 引力反常本质上是高一维玻色拓扑序的指纹；该全息观点统一了对称性保护、反常与高维拓扑序，为理解量子物质中的非可逆现象（如费米化、非幺正演生）提供了新范式。

Abstract: We review a many-body formulation of gravitational anomalies as obstructions to realizing the complete low-energy data by a local {\it bosonic} lattice quantum system of the same dimension. In this formulation, an anomalous theory can be realized at the boundary of a gapped {\it bosonic} lattice bulk, whose topological order gives a one-to-one classification of the anomaly. An invertible bulk reproduces conventional anomaly inflow; a bulk with non-trivial topological excitations yields a non-invertible generalization. To describe the holographic theory of anomalies, we explain the distinction between uniquely defined topological order and many versions of topological quantum field theory and discuss the boundary-to-bulk map. As an application of the holographic theory of anomalies, we describe morphisms between local quantum theories (such as quantum field theories). These morphisms are built from a bulk domain wall and an invertible boundary junction, which give rise to a sandwich picture. Chiral boson/fermion theory, Laughlin edges, and boundaries of $\mathbb{Z}_2$ higher gauge theory with bosonic or fermionic point-like excitations illustrate the different kinds of information about non-invertible anomalies contained in transport coefficients, superselection sectors, and condensation data. In particular, a fermionic theory can be viewed as a theory with a particular non-invertible gravitational anomaly. Those examples also reveal that {\it symmetry $\sim$ gravitational anomaly $\sim$ topological order in one higher dimension}.

</details>


### [191] [Rank polarization and dynamical autonomy in a projective rotor model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15361)
*Shannon Wang,David Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究高阶经典集体序参量如何选择主导的投影本征线，并在p=2实现中支持动力学自主的低能指向矢（director）子系统；提出一种旋转不变的锁定相互作用构造，证明谱选择与红外横模抑制是独立的物理现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投影序参量仅驱动张量有效秩趋近于1，但无法保证动力学自主性；作者旨在厘清秩极化与横模动力学自主性之间的逻辑独立性，并构建能独立调控二者的方法。

Method: 基于全连接投影转子模型，引入二谐波模型揭示相同序张量T可对应不同横模二次型；构造旋转不变的锁定相互作用，在保持有序张量精确固定的同时，全局可控地增强所有非戈德斯通恢复系数；结合惯性/过阻尼动力学分析低频响应。

Result: 证明了谱选择（即选定主导本征线）与红外横模响应抑制（O(λ⁻¹)）是两个独立可控的物理数据；对p=2情形获得最低横模频率按Θ(λ^{1/2})增长；p>2时指向矢流形维数为(p−1)，但动力学构造依赖p=2的1维戈德斯通模式。

Conclusion: 秩极化本身不足以保证动力学自主性；需额外结构（如锁定相互作用、度规选择、或后续的‘焊接’与可积性结构）才能实现物理可观测的低能指向矢自由度；谱性质与低能响应属不同层次的涌现现象。

Abstract: We study how a higher-rank classical collective order parameter can select one dominant projective eigenline and, in a $p=2$ realization, support a dynamically autonomous low-energy director sector. In a fully connected projective-rotor model, the collective second moment is a positive semidefinite tensor $T$ of fixed trace. Conventional projective ordering drives its effective rank toward one, selecting a single dominant eigenline. Rank polarization alone, however, does not determine dynamical autonomy: extrinsic-curvature criteria depend on the chosen state-space metric, and the equilibrium tensor does not determine the transverse free-energy Hessian. A two-harmonic model makes this logical independence explicit by producing families with identical $T$ and $r_{\rm eff}$ but different transverse quadratic forms. We then construct rotationally invariant locking interactions that leave a prescribed ordered tensor exactly fixed while increasing every non-Goldstone restoring coefficient as $Θ(λ)$ within a globally controlled stable regime. For a phenomenological inertial realization with a finite positive kinetic matrix, the lowest transverse normal-mode frequency grows as $Θ(λ^{1/2})$; more generally, the low-frequency transverse susceptibility is suppressed as $O(λ^{-1})$, including in overdamped dynamics. The construction therefore proves that spectral selection and infrared transverse suppression are independent data. For $p>2$ the selected eigenline has a $(p-1)$-dimensional director manifold; the one-dimensional Goldstone sector used in the dynamical construction is specific to $p=2$. Promoting the selected internal eigenline to a physical clock requires additional soldering and integrability structure.

</details>


### [192] [Phase transitions and quasinormal modes of Euler-Heisenberg-dilaton AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15508)
*Yi-Qi Chen,De-Cheng Zou,Ming Zhang,Xufen Zhang,Bo Liu,Rui-Hong Yue*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁荷Euler-Heisenberg-稀释AdS黑洞在扩展相空间中的相变和标量拟正则模，发现负耦合下存在类似范德瓦尔斯的小/大黑洞相变，并通过改进的渐近迭代法观察到准正则模轨迹在临界压强下的合并现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Euler-Heisenberg-稀释AdS黑洞在扩展相空间中的热力学相变行为及其拟正则模特性，特别是不同EH耦合符号对相结构和动力学稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用热力学分析方法分类黑洞视界结构，推导含压强与耦合参数变化的扩展第一定律；利用改进的渐近迭代法（Improved Asymptotic Iteration Method）计算标量场的拟正则模频谱。

Result: 负EH耦合下出现有限临界点及Van der Waals型小/大黑洞相变；QNM轨迹在临界压强以下分离、在临界点合并；GHS极限与正耦合情形无有限临界点。

Conclusion: EH耦合符号显著影响黑洞热力学相结构与扰动谱行为，仅负耦合支持类液-气相变及对应的临界现象，为非线性电磁理论中黑洞相变提供了新例证。

Abstract: In this paper, we study phase transitions and scalar quasinormal modes(QNMs) of magnetically charged Euler--Heisenberg-dilaton AdS black holes in the extended phase space. We classify the horizon structures for all three signs of the EH coupling and derive the extended first law when the pressure and coupling are varied. For negative coupling, the physical branch exhibits a Van der Waals-like small/large black-hole transition. Using the improved asymptotic iteration method, we find two separated QNM trajectories below the critical pressure, whereas they merge into one continuous curve at the critical point. The GHS limit and the positive couplings considered here do not produce a finite-radius critical point.

</details>


### [193] [Aspects of $\mathcal{N}=2$ SUSY gauge theories and applications to Quantum Field Theory and Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15619)
*Andrea Cipriani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了超对称规范理论技术在量子场论与引力中的应用，重点研究Seiberg-Witten（SW）曲线及其量子版本；在量子场论中分析Argyres-Douglas理论及瞬子修正，在引力中研究拓扑星稳定性、电荷超辐射及Schwarzschild背景下的引力波尾部贡献，并提出基于哈密顿神经网络的机器学习方法模拟fuzzball几何中的测地线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将超对称规范理论中发展的技术（尤其是Seiberg-Witten曲线）拓展应用于更广泛的物理问题，包括非超对称量子场论中的奇异共形理论和引力中黑洞/ fuzzball等强耦合时空结构的动力学问题。

Method: 结合理论推导（如SW曲线分析、微分方程求解、共形场论计算）、稳定性分析、特殊函数（合流Heun函数）的新型超几何表示，以及基于哈密顿神经网络的机器学习建模方法。

Result: 1) 在Argyres-Douglas理论中验证了极值关联函数的瞬子修正与有效场论预测一致；2) 证明拓扑星对带质量带电标量扰动是线性稳定的；3) 给出合流Heun函数的新超几何表示，导出Schwarzschild背景下引力波尾部的高精度解析表达式；4) 提出的哈密顿神经网络在关键测地线模拟中优于传统数值积分器。

Conclusion: SW形式主义不仅深刻刻画了超对称理论的结构，还可作为强大工具桥接量子场论与量子引力问题；结合现代数学方法与机器学习，能有效处理传统解析或数值手段难以应对的强耦合、非微扰物理问题。

Abstract: This thesis investigates the application of techniques developed in supersymmetric gauge theories to physical problems in Quantum Field Theory and Gravity, focusing on the Seiberg-Witten (SW) curve and its quantum version. After reviewing the essential concepts of Supersymmetry and the derivation of the SW curve, we explore its applications in both fields. In Quantum Field Theory, we investigate the emergence of Argyres-Douglas theories in the moduli space of supersymmetric models, focusing on extremal correlators. We analyze a conformal model to compute their instanton corrections, finding agreement with predictions from Effective Field Theory, and discuss the implications for analogous observables in Argyres-Douglas theories. In Gravity, we exploit the quantum SW curve and its relation to differential equations describing physical systems. We establish the linear stability of Topological Stars, arising in the fuzzball proposal, under massive charged scalar perturbations. We also investigate charge superradiance in configurations with a horizon and derive a novel hypergeometric representation of the confluent Heun function, relevant to gravitational-wave propagation in the Schwarzschild background. This result yields a new resummed expression for waveform tail contributions, in excellent agreement with numerical data. Finally, beyond the SW formalism, we develop a Machine Learning algorithm based on Hamiltonian Neural Networks to reproduce the geodesics of a neutral particle in a fuzzball geometry. For critical trajectories, our method outperforms standard numerical integrators in both efficiency and accuracy.

</details>


### [194] [New topological phase transitions in $κ$-deformed $φ^4$ lattice models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15644)
*Adith A,A. Bhagwat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了κ-变形φ⁴格点模型在动量空间中的拓扑相变，发现双阱结构导致高能下动量态简并，并通过数值与基于奇点理论的解析方法确定相变能量满足E ∼ 1/α⁴；还揭示了一个非对易系统特有的非微扰效应：简并动量态粒子占比在对易极限下仍非零，可作为非对易时空存在的可检验预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非对易时空背景下（以κ-变形为模型）动量空间中可能存在的新型拓扑相变，并寻找其可观测的非微扰物理效应，以检验非对易时空的存在性。

Method: 采用平均场近似处理κ-变形φ⁴格点模型；结合数值计算与基于A₃奇点理论的解析方法，分析动量空间等能面的拓扑变化；将相变能量标度行为与非对易参数α关联。

Result: 发现动量空间双阱结构引发高能简并及相应拓扑相变；数值和解析均得出相变能量满足E ∼ 1/α⁴；首次指出简并态粒子分数在α→0（即对易极限）下仍趋于非零值，该效应是非微扰的。

Conclusion: κ-变形模型中存在由动量空间拓扑变化驱动的相变，其非微扰特征（简并态分数的非零对易极限）为实验探测非对易时空提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper explores topological phase transitions in the momentum space of the $κ$-deformed $φ^4$ lattice model within the mean-field approximation. We show that the double-well structure of the momentum space gives rise to degenerate momentum states at high energies and that this change is marked by a phase transition. We investigate this transition using both numerical and analytical methods, with the analytical approach based primarily on singularity theory. Our numerical analysis shows that the energy at which the topological phase transition occurs is related to the noncommutative parameter by the simple power law $E \sim 1/α^{4}$. By mapping the constant-energy-level surface to the $A_{3}$ singularity, we obtain the same result analytically. We also present results unique to noncommutative systems, showing that the fraction of particles in degenerate momentum states has a nonzero commutative limit. As a nonperturbative effect, this result provides a testable prediction for the existence of noncommutative spacetime.

</details>


### [195] [Higher Dressing and Shift-Invariance in 4D Topological BF Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15737)
*J. François,L. Ravera*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过引入高阶dressing field，实现了四维BF理论中非阿贝尔平移对称性的精确约化，揭示了其无局域传播自由度的拓扑特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示拓扑场论中无局域传播自由度这一特征，并为高阶形式Dressing Field方法提供首个显式实例。

Method: 识别一种能补偿平移的1-形式高阶dressing field，构造平移不变的复合场B̅ = B − λ⁻¹F，并以此重写理论动力学。

Result: 动力学变为代数式并直接给出B̅ = 0，从而显式暴露理论无局域传播自由度；首次实现高阶形式Dressing Field方法。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅使BF理论的拓扑性质更清晰，也为处理其他具有高阶对称性的场论提供了新工具。

Abstract: A characteristic feature of topological field theories is the absence of local propagating degrees of freedom. In four-dimensional BF theory with cosmological term, we make this feature manifest by exactly reducing the finite non-Abelian shift symmetry. We do so by identifying a higher dressing field, a 1-form compensating the shift, allowing to build from first principles the composite shift-invariant field $\boldsymbol{B}=B-λ^{-1}F$. In terms of this dressed field, the dynamics becomes algebraic and sets $\boldsymbol{B}=0$, directly exposing the absence of local propagating degrees of freedom. This provides the first explicit instance of the higher-form Dressing Field Method.

</details>


### [196] [Area metric gravity à la Plebanski: connection, curvature and dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15838)
*Bianca Dittrich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了非线性面积度量动力学，发展了几何工具以不依赖微扰理论地研究一般非线性面积度量作用量，并将面积度量作用量重新表述为庞加莱规范理论；结果表明其在平直背景上描述一个引力子和一个具有宇称破缺耦合的重质量自旋二场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为检验线性化层面发现的面积度量作用量避免传播子额外极点这一优良性质是否在完整非线性理论中保持，需构建非线性面积度量动力学。

Method: 基于修正的Plebanski框架构造非线性面积度量动力学；发展提取长度度量的算法、由面积度量唯一确定的二形式联络及其曲率不变量等几何工具；推导一阶与二阶版本的面积度量作用量；将其重写为庞加莱规范理论形式。

Result: 建立了不依赖微扰论的面积度量几何框架；导出非线性面积度量作用量；证明其可等价表述为庞加莱规范理论；在平直背景下有效长度度量作用量包含引力子与质量自旋二场，其宇称破缺耦合由Barbero-Immirzi参数决定；讨论了宇宙学背景与物质耦合。

Conclusion: 非线性面积度量理论不仅延续了线性层面的良好传播性质，还提供了通向量子引力具体物理应用（如宇宙学）的新路径，且与庞加莱规范理论存在深刻联系。

Abstract: Area metrics arise as effective description at intermediate scales in a number of approaches to quantum gravity, including string theory, holography and loop quantum gravity. Recently, it has been shown that area metric actions can be derived from the modified Plebanski framework on the linearized level. Surprisingly these actions avoid extra poles in the propagator for the effective length dynamics, and hence constitute interesting deformations of linearized Einstein gravity. To be able to study whether this property persist for the full theory, we construct here non-linear area metric dynamics from modified Plebanski actions. We provide the geometric tools for area metrics that do not rely on perturbation theory and allow a systematic construction and study of general non-linear area metric actions: an algorithm that extracts a length metric from an area metric and a connection on two-forms that is uniquely determined by the area metric, together with its curvature and curvature invariants. We derive area metric actions from modified Plebanski actions in first and second order versions. We also find that area metric actions can be reformulated as Poincare gauge theories. We determine the effective length metric action, which on a flat background describes a graviton and a massive spin-two field with parity-violating couplings set by the Barbero--Immirzi parameter. Additionally we discuss cosmological backgrounds and the role of the matter coupling, which together open the way to cosmological applications.

</details>


### [197] [From Lyapunov Growth to Weak Quantum Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15880)
*Sašo Grozdanov,Tomaž Prosen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了局域非时序关联子（OTOCs）在量子多体系统中的空间传播行为，提出了三个相关可观测量，并证明了在满足Lieb-Robinson局域性和指数衰减关联的吉布斯态下，其时间增长受t^d限制；并通过两个具体模型验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子混沌中信息的空间传播与热化过程，特别是局域算符对易子随时间演化的空间扩展行为。

Method: 基于Lieb-Robinson定理和指数衰减关联假设，理论推导了局部平方对易子C_r(t)、其空间和ar C(t)以及对称正则化连通dOTOC D(t)的时间增长上界；并结合布朗耦合团簇模型与有限温度SYK链进行具体验证。

Result: 证明了在均匀指数簇集的吉布斯态下，D(t) ≤ t^d，改进了先前t^{3d}的次优界；并在两个模型中通过无序对称性将D(t)与ar C(t)关联，揭示了后热化阶段的幂律增长行为。

Conclusion: 局域OTOC的空间扩展在弱量子混沌框架下表现为晚期幂律增长，该行为是饱和区域持续扩张的具体体现，为理解量子信息扩散提供了严格界限与物理图像。

Abstract: Local out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) measure the growth of initially small commutators. For bounded local operators, this growth ends when the local commutator becomes of order one, but in an extended system the region over which it is large can continue to expand. We study this spatial proliferation using three related observables: the local squared commutator $C_r(t)$, its spatial sum $\bar C(t) = \sum_r C_r(t)$, and the symmetrically regularised connected dOTOC $D(t)$ of two extensive observables. We show that Lieb--Robinson locality gives $\bar C(t) \lesssim t^d$. We then prove that, in a Gibbs state with uniform exponential clustering, the full dOTOC obeys the same bound, $D(t) \lesssim t^d$, improving our earlier suboptimal $t^{3d}$ bound. We illustrate these results in two quantum many-body systems: a Brownian coupled-cluster model and a finite-temperature SYK chain. In both cases, an exact disorder symmetry relates $D(t)$ to $\bar C(t)$ and allows us to follow the same extensive observable from the pre-scrambling Lyapunov regime into the post-scrambling growth associated with an expanding saturated region. This late-time power-law growth is the concrete realisation of the weak quantum chaos introduced in our earlier work.

</details>


### [198] [Microscopic entropy of regular BTZ black holes in 3d Lovelock gravity at all orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15917)
*Hikmet Özşahin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从微观角度推导了含无穷阶正则化Lovelock修正的三维Lovelock引力中黑洞的半经典熵，利用minisuperspace方法得到任意阶耦合下的约化作用量与边界项，并通过双Wick旋转构造的孤子作为基态，结合无中心荷Cardy公式成功复现熵；进一步推广至稳态情形，验证了转动黑洞下Cardy公式的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解高阶曲率修正（如Lovelock项）下黑洞微观熵的起源，特别是缺乏明确共形对称性与中心荷时如何应用Cardy公式。

Method: 采用minisuperspace形式主义，利用耦合常数生成函数获得全阶闭式约化作用量与边界项；以双Wick旋转静态黑洞所得孤子为基态，计算其质量；将方法扩展至含角动量的稳态构型，导出转动黑洞的质量与角动量。

Result: 成功在任意Lovelock耦合下复现半经典黑洞熵（含BTZ型解），且无需引入中心荷；并验证Cardy公式在旋转情形下依然成立。

Conclusion: 表明即使在缺乏传统CFT描述和中心荷定义的高阶引力理论中，Cardy公式仍可通过适当选取基态（如孤子）和守恒荷（质量、角动量）有效给出黑洞熵，揭示了熵的普适统计起源。

Abstract: We give a microscopic derivation of the semi-classical entropy of the black holes of 3d Lovelock gravity with an infinite tower of regularized Lovelock corrections, which include the regular BTZ black holes found recently. Working in the mini-superspace formalism, we show that the reduced action and its boundary term can be obtained in closed form at all orders in terms of the generating function of the couplings. Assuming the ground state is described by the soliton obtained from the static black hole by a double Wick rotation, we compute the soliton mass and reproduce the semi-classical entropy from the Cardy formula without central charges for arbitrary couplings. We then extend the mini-superspace to stationary configurations, compute the mass and the angular momentum of the rotating black holes obtained by a boost, and verify the Cardy formula in the presence of rotation.

</details>


### [199] [Random wave chaos on momentum-fractionated microstate geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15918)
*Vladan Djukić,Mihailo Čubrović*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了动量分数化在D1-D5理论中三荷超引力解（特别是动量分数化超弦）混沌发展中的作用，发现分数化背景中测地线和波均表现出强混沌，而非分数化单模超弦则更接近可分离性；作者还探讨了该现象与BPS态单调性及微态几何中混沌探针意义的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究动量分数化如何影响D1-D5系统中微态几何的混沌行为，并理解其与BPS态性质及黑洞极限下混沌探针有效性的关系。

Method: 通过分析分数化与非分数化超弦背景下的类光测地线动力学和波动行为来探测混沌，结合Berry随机波假设进行比较。

Result: 分数化超弦背景中测地线和波均呈现强混沌（大混沌区域、高度符合Berry随机波假设），而非分数化单模超弦则混沌较弱、更接近可分离；该差异可能反映单调BPS态比偶然BPS态更不混沌。

Conclusion: 动量分数化是增强微态几何混沌程度的关键因素；所用混沌探针在微态几何中有物理意义，尽管在黑洞极限下退化。

Abstract: We study the role of momentum fractionation in the development of chaos on smooth 3-charge supergravity solutions of D1-D5 theory, specifically momentum-fractionated superstrata. We probe the chaos through the dynamics of null geodesics and waves. On fractionated backgrounds, both geodesics and waves show uniformly strong chaos (large chaotic sea and near-perfect agreement with the Berry random wave hypothesis). In contrast, geodesics and waves on single-mode (non-fractionated) superstrata are closer to separability (although still chaotic). We discuss a possibility that such dichotomy can be related to the expectation that monotone BPS states are less chaotic than fortuitous ones. Finally, we explain why our chaos probes are meaningful for microstate geometries even though they become trivial in the black hole limit.

</details>


### [200] [Symmetric Dual Breather Modes as a Dynamic Origin of Tiny Particle Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15990)
*Yoritaka Iwata,Yasuhiro Takei*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算子理论的框架，通过谱分支刻画标量场论中Z₂对称性破缺，揭示了从对称态到非对称态的非微扰动力学路径，发现动态势垒溢出可自然产生亚电子伏特有效质量尺度，并导致手征选择与中微子质量的动力学起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上自发对称性破缺（SSB）依赖于低能近似下的静态真空选取，但连接对称构型与非对称态的非微扰动力学路径仍不清楚。

Method: 构建算子理论框架，引入‘对称双呼吸子模’这一有效不变流形，利用时变有限维表示追踪其相空间几何；分析振幅跨越临界值φ_v1时进入异宿区的动力学行为，并研究时间平均序参量、势函数二阶导平均及相空间收缩特性。

Result: 发现静态SSB仅为小振幅极限；大振幅下发生动态势垒溢出，导致时间平均序参量为零、有效势曲率抵消；出现由普适标度函数F(y)控制的对数周期发散，自然生成亚eV有效质量；并涌现出Majorana型本征态偏好与左手Weyl方程的动力学起源。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了非微扰波动力学、拓扑质量压制、手征选择与活动型中微子质量的动力学起源，为对称性破缺提供了全新的动态视角。

Abstract: Spontaneous symmetry breaking (SSB) is conventionally understood through static vacuum selection in low-energy approximations. However, the nonperturbative dynamical pathways connecting symmetric configurations to asymmetric states remain an open question. In this paper, we propose an operator-theoretic framework that characterizes $Z_{2}$ symmetry breaking in scalar field theory through spectral branching. Specifically, we identify an effective invariant manifold of the nonlinear Klein-Gordon equation termed the "symmetric dual breather mode," whose phase-space geometry is traced using a $t$-dependent finite-dimensional representation. Our analysis demonstrates that static SSB represents merely a small-amplitude limit ($A \ll φ_{v1}$). As the amplitude increases into the heteroclinic regime ($A > φ_{v1}$), the field dynamically overflows the central barrier, yielding a vanishing time-averaged order parameter ($\langle φ\rangle = 0$) and driving a geometric curvature cancellation ($\langle V''(φ) \rangle_{t} \to 0$). Governed by a universal scaling function $\mathfrak{F}(y)$, the logarithmic period divergence near the heteroclinic boundary naturally generates sub-eV effective mass scales without fine-tuning bare parameters. Furthermore, this dynamic barrier overflow naturally favors Majorana-like self-conjugate states over Dirac-type states. Additionally, nonlinear phase interference along the heteroclinic channel induces asymmetric phase-space contraction, decoupling the right-handed mode and dynamically giving rise to the left-handed Weyl equation. These results bridge nonperturbative wave dynamics, topological mass suppression, chiral selection, and the dynamic origin of active neutrino masses.

</details>


### [201] [Relativistic Covariance from Hamiltonian Hydrodynamics in the Chiral Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16095)
*ZiHao Liu,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从等时哈密顿形式出发推导了无耗散手性流体力学的协变方程，揭示了其中隐含的Lie-Poisson结构，并分析了洛伦兹提升约束对手性哈密顿系数的限制条件及其物理含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使手性流体力学的对称性约束显式化，并为其未来热力学一致的耗散扩展提供基础。

Method: 基于等时哈密顿形式推导协变手性流体力学方程，识别手性括号中的Lie-Poisson结构；对具有宇称不变性的二次哈密顿量，求解洛伦兹提升约束并分析其非线性耦合行为。

Result: 发现当且仅当静止系中π介子传播速度不超过光速时，所有亚光速下的手性哈密顿系数保持有限；同时建立了速度依赖关系与静止系数据之间的对应。

Conclusion: 该哈密顿框架显式体现了对称性约束，为构建热力学相容的耗散手性流体力学提供了坚实起点。

Abstract: We derive the covariant equations of nondissipative chiral hydrodynamics from their equal-time Hamiltonian formulation and identify the Lie--Poisson structure implicit in the chiral brackets. This structure combines fluid motion with local chiral transformations. For a parity-invariant Hamiltonian quadratic in chiral charge densities and pion gradients, without external backgrounds, we derive and solve the constraints imposed by Lorentz boosts. Their coupled nonlinear sector is equivalent to the linear boost transformation laws of a symmetric tensor, which determine the velocity dependence from rest-frame data. For finite, positive rest-frame coefficients, finite ordinary-fluid energy density and thermodynamic derivatives, and a momentum--velocity relation that can be solved in either direction, the chiral Hamiltonian coefficients remain finite at every fluid speed below light speed if and only if the rest-frame pion propagation speed does not exceed the speed of light. This formulation makes symmetry constraints explicit and provides a starting point for future thermodynamically consistent dissipative extensions.

</details>


### [202] [Retarded Green Function For Bopp--Podolsky Electrodynamics in Four-Dimensional De Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16239)
*Wei Fan,Xiao Yan Chew*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维德西特时空中的经典Bopp-Podolsky电动力学，重点分析其横向推迟格林函数与规范不变电磁响应；利用不变双张量分解将传播子方程约化为超几何主方程，揭示了短距离对数奇异性与软化的矢势波前，并指出电磁内区尾部完全由质量项贡献；同时导出了精确的视界正则静态势、广义高斯定律，并处理了全局德西特时空电荷守恒约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空（特别是德西特时空）中建立一致、规范不变且具有良好短距行为的经典高阶电动力学理论，克服标准麦克斯韦理论在量子引力或紫外修正背景下的局限性。

Method: 基于德西特时空的最大对称性，采用不变双张量基展开矢量格林函数；利用BP场算子在守恒源下的可因式分解特性（Maxwell × massive-vector），结合分布理论与超几何微分方程方法求解推迟传播子；通过旋度运算提取规范不变场强核；构造反点源配置以满足全局电荷守恒。

Result: 获得BP推迟格林函数的精确分布结构：短距行为为对数奇异性，波前软化；场强核含光锥δ分布与类时正则尾部，且尾部完全源于质量项；导出视界正则静态势，验证库仑发散抵消；恢复平直空间极限；建立全局德西特上的广义高斯定律及电荷中性约束实现方案。

Conclusion: Bopp-Podolsky理论在德西特时空中具有良好的经典适定性与物理一致性：既保持规范不变性与因果性，又自然软化紫外奇异性，并兼容宇宙学背景的拓扑约束；无需引入量子虚时间路径或特定真空选择。

Abstract: We study classical Bopp--Podolsky (BP) electrodynamics on four-dimensional de Sitter spacetime, focusing on its transverse retarded Green function and gauge-invariant electromagnetic response. Because de Sitter spacetime is maximally symmetric, its vector Green functions admit a covariant expansion in a finite basis of invariant bitensors, with scalar coefficients depending only on the invariant separation. For conserved sources, the fourth-order field operator factorizes into Maxwell and massive-vector operators, yielding the BP retarded kernel as the difference of their transverse Green functions. Using the invariant bitensor decomposition, we reduce the propagator equations to a hypergeometric master equation and determine the distributional structure of its solution. The common leading short-distance singularity of the Maxwell and massive-vector sectors cancels, leaving a logarithmic master singularity and a softened vector-potential wavefront. After one curl, the gauge-invariant field-strength kernel contains a null-cone delta distribution and a regular timelike tail. The smooth Maxwell master tail is annihilated by the curl, so the interior electromagnetic tail is generated entirely by the massive sector. We also derive an exact horizon-regular static potential, demonstrate the cancellation of the Coulomb divergence, recover the flat-space BP potential, and formulate the generalized Gauss law. On global de Sitter spacetime, the compactness of the spatial sections requires the total charge to vanish; we implement this constraint using an antipodal charge configuration with smooth factorized initial data. The analysis is classical and does not assume a quantum Lee--Wick contour or a preferred de Sitter vacuum.

</details>


### [203] [A first order nonabelian antisymmetric tensor model with hidden higher symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16250)
*Peter D. Jarvis,Jean Thierry-Mieg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个具有规范内部对称性的非阿贝尔复数自对偶反对称张量模型，包含质量为零的自对偶反对称张量场及其共轭，并与质量为零的杨-米尔斯规范场相互作用；通过一阶形式在经典层面建立模型，利用狄拉克代数和内部矩阵基的分量展开，并引入辅助占位场（消除后成为曲率），揭示了一种隐藏的内部对称性；具体分析了SU(2)×U(1)规范群情形，并探讨了SU(3)和SU(2/1)作为更高对称性的候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有规范内部对称性的非阿贝尔复数自对偶反对称张量模型，以拓展高维规范理论与张量场理论的统一框架，并探索潜在的隐藏对称性。

Method: 采用一阶形式化方法，在狄拉克代数与内部矩阵基上进行局域场的分量展开，通过迹运算保证洛伦兹与规范不变性，并引入辅助占位场；借助代数运动方程消去辅助场使其成为曲率；利用额外内部矩阵（非规范生成元）实现内部分量混合及辅助曲率与势之间的联系。

Result: 成功构建了满足洛伦兹与规范不变性的非阿贝尔复自对偶张量模型；证明该模型具有由额外内部矩阵诱导的隐藏内部对称性；具体实现了SU(2)×U(1)情形，并论证了SU(3)与SU(2/1)作为更高对称性候选的可行性。

Conclusion: 该模型为非阿贝尔张量场理论提供了新构造路径，揭示了隐藏内部对称性的存在可能性，对统一理论与超对称推广具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We present a nonabelian complex self-dual antisymmetric tensor model, with gauged internal symmetry, comprising a complex multiplet of massless self-dual antisymmetric tensor fields and its conjugate (interacting with massless Yang-Mills gauge fields). We build the model at the classical level, via a first order formulation, assigning local fields to a component expansion over the Dirac algebra and a basis of internal matrices, with Lorentz and gauge invariance ensured by tracing, and applying an appropriate bilinear form. The full expansion requires auxiliary place-holder fields, which are not potentials, but become curvatures (field strengths) after elimination via their algebraic equations of motion. The component expansion over the Dirac algebra is closed under multiplication by additional internal matrices, which are not gauge group generators, but mix internal components, and link auxiliary curvatures with potentials. The model is shown to be invariant under such transformations, and thus possesses a type of hidden internal symmetry. We analyze the specific case of gauge group $SU(2)\! \times \! U(1)$, and as higher symmetry candidates, we consider both the Lie group $SU(3)$ and the Lie superalgebra $SU(2/1)$ (with the additional matrices acting via Lie brackets, or graded Lie brackets, respectively).

</details>


### [204] [Regular-Zero Drinfeld-Sokolov Charges in Doubled Four-Dimensional Chern-Simons Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16271)
*Wentao Zeng,Yifan He,Xian Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维加倍Chern-Simons理论经有限缺陷约化后得到的经典局域守恒荷，通过分析扭函数零点处的仿射规范变换性质，结合Drinfeld-Sokolov层级的不变张量，构造了满足泊松对易关系的局域圆积分，并在特定边界与单值性假设下证明所构荷守恒，最终给出经典局域阿贝尔泊松子代数条件存在性的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建经典可积系统中具有明确几何与代数结构的局域守恒荷，特别是从四维加倍Chern-Simons理论出发，通过有限缺陷约化途径获得物理意义清晰的守恒律。

Method: 利用扭函数在有限简单零点处使非齐次导数项消失的性质，使Gaudin流在该点按普通伴随作用变换；借助Drinfeld-Sokolov层级的Evans相容不变张量作为代数输入，分同一零点、不同零点和混合情形构造局域圆积分；在Stedman边界哈密顿量、周期平凡单值性及相空间无关边界权等假设下分解约化哈密顿量并分析约束结构。

Result: 证明所构造的局域电荷两两泊松对易，在给定假设下守恒；明确区分有限缺陷约化空间、带剩余约束的延拓空间及物理商空间；得出经典局域阿贝尔泊松子代数条件存在的结论。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从加倍Chern-Simons理论有限缺陷约化导出经典局域守恒荷的一般框架，但未涉及函数独立性、极大性或刘维尔完备性等更强性质；高阶零点与非平凡单值性情形留待后续研究。

Abstract: We study classical local conserved charges obtained after finite-defect reduction of doubled four-dimensional Chern--Simons theory. At a finite simple zero, the inhomogeneous derivative term in the affine gauge transformation induced by the twist function vanishes, so the residue evaluations of the two Gaudin currents transform by the ordinary adjoint action. For each simple ideal, we use the Evans-compatible invariant tensors of the principal Drinfeld--Sokolov hierarchy as external algebraic input, construct local circle integrals, and treat the same-zero, distinct-zero, and mixed sectors separately, proving pairwise Poisson commutativity. Under the explicit assumptions of Stedman's simple-pole/simple-zero boundary Hamiltonian, the periodic trivial-monodromy sector, and boundary weights independent of phase-space variables, the reduced Hamiltonian decomposes term by term into finite-pole, infinity, finite-zero, and residual first-class-constraint contributions, which yields conservation of the constructed charges. We distinguish the finite-defect reduced space, the extended space carrying the residual constraint, and the physical quotient. The result is a conditional existence statement for a classical local Abelian Poisson subalgebra; we make no claim of functional independence, maximality, or Liouville completeness. Extensions to higher-order zeros and nontrivial monodromy sectors are outside the scope of this work.

</details>


### [205] [A No-Go Theorem for Nonspherical Bounces with a Single Negative Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16688)
*Guangshang Chen,Jianpeng Dai,Yang Xiao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了在平坦时空中的单实标量场模型中，任何具有零初速度的局域非球对称鞍点至少具有三个负模，从而排除了单负模非球对称bounce解的可能性，为假真空衰变的球对称bounces描述提供了谱学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究单负模要求本身是否能独立于作用量极小化而强制球对称性。

Method: 对平坦时空中光滑势的单实标量场模型，严格分析局域非球对称鞍点的涨落谱，结合反射对称性讨论负模数下界。

Result: 证明任意零初速度局域非球对称鞍点至少有三个负模；额外反射对称性可进一步收紧该下界。

Conclusion: 单负模要求确实排除了非球对称bounce候选解，从而为假真空衰变的标准球对称描述提供了独立于极小化原理的谱学支撑。

Abstract: False vacuum decay is conventionally described by a spherically symmetric bounce with a single negative fluctuation mode. This raises a fundamental question: can the single negative mode requirement itself enforce spherical symmetry, independently of action minimization? For a single real scalar field with a smooth potential in flat spacetime, we rigorously prove that every localized nonspherical saddle with zero initial velocity has at least three negative modes. Additional reflection symmetries further tighten these bounds, as illustrated in a sextic scalar model. Our findings exclude nonspherical bounce candidates with a single negative mode, providing a complementary spectral foundation for the spherical bounce description of false vacuum decay.

</details>


### [206] [About P-adic Numbers and Professor Igor Volovich on the Occasion of his 80th Anniversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16708)
*Paul H Frampton*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文回顾了1987年Volovich提出的p-adic弦理论及其相关非局域场论的费曼规则，并提及作者与Okada后续的合作及1989-1991年间访问莫斯科的相关回忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回顾p-adic弦理论的发展历程，纪念早期工作并补充历史背景与个人经历。

Method: 文献回顾与历史叙述，结合理论物理中p-adic弦和非局域场论的 Feynman 规则构建。

Result: 系统梳理了p-adic弦理论的起源、Feynman规则的建立及其物理应用，并补充了关键时期学术交流的背景信息。

Conclusion: p-adic弦理论不仅具有数学上的新颖性，还在非局域场论等领域展现出多样化的物理应用潜力；早期国际合作（如与Volovich、Okada）对这一方向发展起到了关键作用。

Abstract: We recall a 1987 paper written by Volovich and our subsequent work with Okada which established the Feyman rules for the p-adic string and the related non-local field theory which found diverse physical applications. We add some memories of visits to Moscow during 1989-91.

</details>


### [207] [TheoPhysEval: Assessing AI agents at autonomous theoretical physics problem solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16733)
*A. Ramesh Chandra,Aaghaz Mahajan,Ainesh Sanyal,Mianqi Wang,Shubhadeep Chakraborty,Sudip Chakraborty,Shailesh Chandrasekharan,Rama Govindarajan,Akshit Goyal,Jonathan J. Heckman,Yasir Iqbal,Divya Jaganathan,Saumav Kapoor,Andreas Karch,Pushkar Khandare,Sandeep Krishna,Anupam Kundu,Alok Laddha,Tongyan Lin,Swarnendu Maity,Nilmani Mathur,Shani Nadir Meynet,Alessandro Mininno,Abhijeet Mishra,Vidur Niranjan,Abhishodh Prakash,Pratik Rath,Durbar Roy,Debargha Sarkar,Srikanth Sastry,Gary Shiu,Aninda Sinha,Thomas Van Riet,Praneeth Netrapalli,Suvrat Raju*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套50个未发表的理论物理研究级问题，测试了AI代理（如GPT-6-Astra+Codex）在无提示、使用现实工具（搜索、编程等）条件下自主求解的能力，达到98%准确率，平均成本低于3美元；结果表明AI已具备扎实的理论物理计算能力，但尚未推动原创性突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估当前AI代理在真实科研场景中解决理论物理问题的能力，探究其是否具备推动前沿研究突破的潜力。

Method: 构建50个专家级理论物理问题集，测试多种AI代理在有/无文献检索和编程工具条件下的自主解题表现，并分析其准确性、成本与知识来源（记忆 vs 检索）。

Result: GPT-6-Astra+Codex在工具完备条件下达98%准确率，单题平均成本<3美元；禁用网络搜索后准确率下降甚微，表明高度依赖已有知识记忆。

Conclusion: 最先进AI代理已能高精度完成理论物理中的具体计算任务，但尚不能实现原创性科学突破，可能因缺乏深层物理直觉、问题提出能力或跨领域概念整合能力。

Abstract: We present a suite of 50 unpublished research-level theoretical physics problems with verifiable answers. Without AI assistance, a typical problem in our collection could be solved by a world expert in about a day or less but would take considerably longer for experts in adjacent specialties. We test the ability of off-the-shelf AI agents to tackle these problems autonomously, without human hints. When given access to tools available in a realistic setting, including the ability to search the literature and write scripts for numerical and symbolic work, the best-performing agent, GPT-6-Astra harnessed in Codex, obtains the final answers with a near-perfect accuracy of 98% at an average cost under USD 3 per problem. On average, agents show only small changes in accuracy when denied web search and access to background research papers, suggesting significant memorization of the literature. These results suggest that state-of-the-art agents can accurately perform concrete calculations required across theoretical physics research. We comment on the broader implications of these findings and discuss possible explanations for the current lack of agent-led breakthroughs in theoretical physics despite their outstanding problem-solving abilities.

</details>


### [208] [Out-of-time-order hydrodynamics and scrambling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16888)
*Marta Bucca,Akash Jain,Márk Mezei,Alexey Milekhin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将流体力学作为有效场论（EFT）推广至四时间轮廓，结合局部SL(2,R)对称性，系统研究其对非时序关联函数（OTOCs）尤其是能量流OTOC的预测能力；从SYK晶格微观模型出发导出该EFT，并证实其指数增长满足混沌上界，拓展了流体力学普适性至更复杂量子混沌观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流体力学EFT擅长描述长时长距下的时间有序关联函数，但难以刻画量子混沌核心指标——非时序关联函数（OTOCs）；本文旨在构建能统一描述传统输运与量子信息 scrambling 的广义流体EFT。

Method: 构建基于四时间Schwinger-Keldysh轮廓的全阶导数流体EFT，引入局部SL(2,R)对称性作为scrambling机制；并从低能极限SYK晶格的微观理论严格推导该EFT，确定全部输运系数。

Result: 成功计算能量流OTOC，发现其指数增长速率精确饱和Maldacena-Shenker-Stanford混沌上界，并与冲击波（scramblon）唯象图像一致。

Conclusion: 流体力学自由度的普适性不仅限于时间有序关联函数，亦可自然涵盖OTOC等量子混沌观测量，表明流体EFT是连接宏观输运与微观量子混沌的统一框架。

Abstract: Hydrodynamics as an effective field theory (EFT) has been immensely successful for predicting the behaviour of time-ordered correlation functions in the long-time, large-distance regime. In this paper, we analyse the predictions of hydrodynamics for scrambling as probed by out-of-time order correlators (OTOCs). To do so, we consider an all-derivative order generalisation of the Schwinger-Keldysh EFT of hydrodynamics extended to a four-fold time contour required to capture OTOCs. A crucial ingredient of the construction is a local SL(2,R) symmetry, which has previously been suggested as a mechanism for scrambling. We also derive this exact EFT from the microscopic formulation of the maximally chaotic low-temperature limit of the SYK lattice, thereby determining all transport coefficients for this model. As a novel application, we compute the OTOC of energy currents, find that its exponential growth saturates the chaos bound, and match it to expectations based on a shockwave (or scramblon) effective description. Our work shows that the universality of hydrodynamics degrees of freedom extends beyond traditional time-ordered correlators to more complex observables such as OTOCs.

</details>


### [209] [Metric and dilaton information from high-overtone quasinormal modes of analytic Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16973)
*Yu Shi,Chikun Ding,Yuebing Zhou,Changqing Liu,Weike Deng,Sheng Long*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞内部几何如何通过线性扰动的高阶模谱编码，并利用解析的Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton黑洞模型，探讨了该谱如何反映度规与支撑物质信息；通过引入中性旁观者场耦合到标量场，构建了对物质敏感的探测通道，并分析了不同时空连接（类空、类时、因果边界附近）下谱响应的特征及其对外部可观测谱的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞内部不可被外部观测者直接访问，但其几何结构可能影响线性扰动的高阶模谱；本文旨在揭示该谱如何隐含黑洞内部度规与物质场信息，从而为‘黑洞内部可探测性’提供理论依据。

Method: 采用解析的Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton黑洞解，引入中性旁观者场与标量场耦合以构造物质敏感探针；系统分析不同边界条件（如实端点类空连接、复端点近因果阈值）下准正规模的渐近行为，结合波函数连接条件与局部标度分析，推导谱对外部度规参数与内禀物质参数（如标量坡度）的依赖关系。

Result: 发现动量响应与质量标签依赖可分离度规指数与标量场斜率符号；在因果阈值附近，复端点导致质量谱趋同并产生共享分数修正，而正则传播仍保留标签依赖；该机制在无内视界的临界情形下依然成立，并可局部延拓至类时内部；数值计算的外部谱支持所提重建方案及主导复端点响应。

Conclusion: 高阶模谱确实携带黑洞内部几何与物质信息，且可通过适当设计的探针耦合与波连接分析实现参数分离与局部重建；这为利用引力波等观测手段间接探测黑洞内部结构提供了新路径。

Abstract: Black-hole interiors are hidden from exterior observers, yet their geometry can enter the high-overtone spectrum of linear perturbations. We study how this spectrum encodes metric and supporting-matter information in analytic Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton black holes. Coupling a neutral spectator to the dilaton at fixed UV dimension provides a matter-sensitive channel alongside the momentum response. For real-endpoint spacelike connections, the momentum response and mass-label dependence separate the metric exponents from the signed dilaton slope. Near the causal threshold, a complex endpoint controls the spectral family: the mass profiles approach a common value, producing a shared fractional correction, while regular propagation retains label dependence. This mechanism is present at criticality without an inner horizon and continues locally into timelike interiors. Exterior numerical spectra support the reconstruction and the leading complex-endpoint responses. Local scaling and probe coupling set the candidate responses, while wave connection determines their imprint on the exterior spectrum.

</details>


### [210] [Modular Flow and Holographic Timelike Entanglement Entropy in AdS and Flat Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17165)
*Mehdi Hakami Shalamzari,Zahra Paygozar,Mahdi Kord Zangeneh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了AdS3/CFT2和flat3/CCFT2中类空和类时间隔的模流，并将其推广到体空间，系统地将体模流与极值曲线及全息（类时）纠缠熵联系起来，首次显式构造了与类时间隔相关的类时极值曲线的模流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类时纠缠熵是研究全息理论的重要探针，但其背后模流在体空间中的具体实现尚未明确。

Method: 从边界对称性生成元出发，显式推导边界模流并延拓至体空间，建立体模流与极值曲线及全息纠缠熵之间的系统关联。

Result: 成功构造了AdS3/CFT2和flat3/CCFT2中类空与类时间隔的体模流，复现了标准全息纠缠熵和类时纠缠熵，并验证了平直空间结果可由AdS3结果在平直极限下得到。

Conclusion: 本文首次显式实现了类时极值曲线对应的模流构造，为理解类时纠缠熵的全息机制提供了关键几何与对称性基础。

Abstract: Timelike entanglement entropy provides a useful probe of holographic theoris, yet an explicit bulk realization of the modular flow underlying its timelike extremal curve construction has so far been lacking. We construct modular flows associated with spacelike and timelike intervals in AdS$_{3}$/CFT$_{2}$ and extend this framework to flat$_{3}$/CCFT$_{2}$. Beginning with the boundary symmetry generators, we explicitly derive the boundary modular flows and extend them into the bulk, providing a systematic procedure that relates bulk modular flows to the extremal curves and holographic (timelike) entanglement entropy.
  For spacelike intervals, the resulting bulk modular flows determine the extremal curves, whose integrated line elements reproduce the standard holographic entanglement entropy. We then generalize this approach to timelike intervals, explicitly constructing the modular flows whose fixed points reproduce the extremal spacelike and timelike curves which gives the holographic timelike entanglement entropy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first explicit construction of the modular flow associated with a timelike extremal curve for a timelike interval.
  The same procedure is then carried over to flat$_{3}$/CCFT$_{2}$, which give rise to the holographic (timelike) entanglement entropy. Finally, we show that the flat space results obtained in this work can be recovered from their AdS$_3$ counterparts in the flat space limit.

</details>


### [211] [On the analytic continuation and asymptotic behaviour of the two-point function in massless $φ^4$-theory in Euclidean de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17215)
*Martin Beneke,Andrea F. Sanfilippo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了欧几里得dS空间中质量为零、最小耦合实标量场在λφ⁴相互作用下的两点函数的领头对数项（至O(λ²)），并证明其洛伦兹解析延拓与随机形式主义结果一致，且该随机级数具有Borel可和性，表明欧氏理论可用于提取洛伦兹dS中质量为零关联函数的晚期行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解质量为零标量场在德西特时空中的晚期行为及其量子效应，特别是通过欧氏方法与随机形式主义之间的联系来解决红外发散问题。

Method: 计算欧氏de Sitter空间中λφ⁴相互作用下两点函数的领头对数项（至O(λ²)，即√λ展开的N⁵LO），并进行洛伦兹解析延拓；分析随机形式主义中对应量的级数大阶行为及Borel可和性。

Result: 得到O(λ²)精度下的领头对数修正；验证欧氏结果经解析延拓后与随机形式主义一致；证明随机级数的大阶行为及Borel可和性。

Conclusion: 欧氏de Sitter理论可通过Borel可和的随机级数，可靠地提取洛伦兹de Sitter中质量为零标量场的晚期关联函数行为。

Abstract: For the two-point function of the massless, minimally-coupled, real scalar field in Euclidean de Sitter space with $λφ^4$-interaction, we compute the leading-logarithmic terms in the invariant distance of the two-point function to $O(λ^2)$, which counts as the fifth subleading order (N$^5$LO) in the $\sqrtλ$-expansion. We show that the analytic continuation to Lorentzian signature coincides with the expansion of the analogous quantity computed in the stochastic formalism. We determine the large-order behaviour of the stochastic series expansion and prove its Borel summability, which suggests that the Euclidean theory can be used to extract the late-time behaviour of massless correlation functions in Lorentzian de Sitter space.

</details>


### [212] [New analytic Janus and $S^4$ solutions for $\mathcal{N}=2^*$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17238)
*Fridrik Freyr Gautason*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个三参数的正则解析超引力解族，用于对偶于四维球面上的N=2*规范理论以及具有U(1)^2对称性的Janus界面，并通过五维超引力模型实现解析求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解N=2*规范理论及Janus界面在全息对偶中的超引力描述，需构造能精确对应场论源与真空期望值（vev）的正则解析解。

Method: 采用由IIB型超引力在S^5上一致截断导出的含六个标量场的五维超引力模型，构造具有隐藏守恒量的弯曲域壁解，并解析求解其三参数族。

Result: 获得三参数正则解析超引力解族，明确其参数与场论中源和vev的对应关系，并确定正则性对参数取值范围的限制；初步讨论了Janus解下拉格朗日密度算符单点函数的全息计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为N=2*理论与Janus界面提供了精确可解的全息对偶框架，揭示了弯曲域壁解中隐藏守恒量的关键作用，并建立了场论参数与引力解参数间的定量映射。

Abstract: We construct a three-parameter family of regular analytic supergravity solutions which are dual to (a) the $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ gauge theory on the four-sphere, and (b) $\mathrm{U}(1)^2$-invariant Janus interfaces in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM with or without the $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ mass turned on. To this end we employ a five-dimensional supergravity model coupled to six scalars which arises as a certain consistent truncation of type IIB supergravity on $S^5$. The curved domain wall solutions feature a hidden integral of motion which allows for their analytic treatment. We relate the three parameters of the solution to field theory sources and vevs and show how regularity of the solutions restricts their range. We briefly discuss the holographic evaluation of the one-point function of the field theory Lagrangian for our family of Janus solutions.

</details>


### [213] [Holographic Pseudo Entropy from the Rindler Method in AdS and Flat Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17239)
*Mehdi Hakami Shalamzari,Zahra Paygozar,Mahdi Kord Zangeneh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Rindler方法研究了AdS₃/CFT₂和flat₃/CCFT₂中类时区间上的全息类时纠缠熵（TEE），揭示了其虚部源于连接内外视界的类时测地线，并在平直空间全息中发现平面CCFT₂上TEE实虚部相等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解类时纠缠熵的几何起源及其在不同全息对偶（如AdS/CFT与flat/CCFT）中的表现，尤其是Rindler图景下类时极值曲线的缺失识别问题。

Method: 采用Rindler变换将类时区间映射到旋转BTZ黑洞背景，分析其内外视界上的类空极值曲线及类时极值曲线；推广至平直空间全息，考察宇宙学视界与类时无穷远之间的几何结构，并计算平面CCFT₂上的全息TEE。

Result: 发现类时极值曲线是连接内外视界的类时测地线，长度为iπℓ，贡献固定虚部iπc/6；在flat₃/CCFT₂中，平面情形下TEE的实部与虚部相等，不同于圆柱情形。

Conclusion: 类时纠缠熵的虚部具有普适几何起源——跨视界类时测地线；其在不同边界拓扑（平面vs圆柱）下的实虚部关系揭示了CCFT₂中时空结构与纠缠性质的深刻联系。

Abstract: Via the Rindler method, we study holographic timelike entanglement entropy (TEE) for a general timelike interval in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ and flat$_3$/CCFT$_2$. The TEE is the pseudo entropy of a timelike interval. In the bulk, the imaginary part of the TEE comes from a timelike geodesic, but in the Rindler picture this geodesic has not been identified. After a Rindler transformation to a rotating BTZ black hole, the two spacelike extremal curves lie on the inner and outer horizons, and exchanging $l_u\leftrightarrow \ell l_φ$ takes one to the other. We find the geometric meaning of the timelike extremal curve: a geodesic stretched between the two horizons. Its length, $iπ\ell$, is independent of the interval, and it alone produces the imaginary part $iπc/6$. We follow the same procedure in flat space holography, where the spacelike curve sits on the cosmological horizon and the timelike curve lies between the cosmological horizon and timelike infinity. Evaluating the holographic TEE for a timelike interval in CCFT$_2$ on the plane, we find that, in contrast with the cylinder, the real and imaginary parts are equal.

</details>


### [214] [Love at First Loop: Quantum Deformability of Near-Extremal Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17259)
*Pablo A. Cano,Marina David,Roberto Emparan,Stefano Trezzi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了近极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞在强Schwarzian涨落下的静态Love数行为，发现对于ℓ≥1多极矩，经典为零的静态Love数变为非零，源于自旋-ℓ中间态的有限转动能量破坏了经典抵消；通过推迟格林函数定义并计算其频率依赖性，保守部分需重整化且含不可去除的对数能量依赖，耗散部分在低频下为零并在能隙阈值处出现非解析频率行为和t^{-3/2}晚期尾项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典四维渐近平直广义相对论中黑洞的静态Love数为零，本文旨在探究在近极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞、强Schwarzian涨落及低能标下这一结论如何被量子效应改变。

Method: 基于近视界喉区强Schwarzian涨落的量子系统描述，利用推迟格林函数定义并计算静态Love数的全频率依赖性，分析其保守与耗散部分的物理行为及重整化特性。

Result: ℓ=0时静态Love数仍为零（由标量平移对称性保证），但ℓ≥1时非零；保守部分需重整化，其对数项系数由近视界理论固定，含不可局域消除的能量依赖；耗散部分有限且与方案无关，在能量低于转动能隙时为零，在能隙阈值处呈现非解析频率行为并产生t^{-3/2}晚期尾项。

Conclusion: 经典黑洞静态Love数为零的结论在量子化近极端黑洞背景下被打破，ℓ≥1多极矩表现出非零响应，揭示了近视界量子效应如何重塑黑洞潮汐响应的基本性质。

Abstract: The static Love numbers of black holes vanish in classical, four-dimensional, asymptotically flat General Relativity. We study how this changes for the response of near-extremal Reissner--Nordström black holes to a minimally coupled massless scalar, at energies where the Schwarzian mode of the near-horizon throat fluctuates strongly. The black hole then responds as a quantum system with a spectrum of states. Its static response is a polarizability, given by virtual transitions between them. For $\ell=0$ the static Love number vanishes, by the shift symmetry of the scalar, but for multipoles $\ell\geq1$ the static Love numbers are nonzero: the finite rotational energy of the spin-$\ell$ intermediate states spoils the cancellation responsible for the classical result. Since the throat no longer has a classical geometry from which to read off a tidal response, we define it through the retarded Green's function, and compute its full frequency dependence at leading order in $r_+ω$. Its conservative part requires renormalization: the near-horizon theory fixes the coefficient of its logarithm, and with it a dependence on the energy that no local term can remove, while its finite local terms are matching data of the four-dimensional theory. The dissipative part is finite and scheme independent. For $\ell\geq1$, it vanishes at low frequencies when the energy is below the rotational gap, while at the gap threshold it becomes non-analytic in frequency and produces a late-time $t^{-3/2}$ tail.

</details>


### [215] [Gauge-Invariant Brout--Englert--Higgs Mechanism without Spontaneous Breaking of Local Gauge Symmetry: A Quantum-Reference-Frame Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17264)
*Kei-Ichi Kondo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在规范不变的关联框架下重新表述BEH机制的方法，将希格斯场的规范取向视为动力学内部参考数据，并在此希格斯框架下描述规范联络；该方法避免了对局域规范冗余赋予物理意义上的自发破缺，且能自然导出标准树图级矢量玻色子质量，并区分了不同粒子（如W±、带电费米子与中性通道）所需的规范不变插值算符形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免将物理的自发对称性破缺归因于局域规范冗余（这在概念上不妥），并建立一个真正规范不变、基于内部量子参考系的语言来理解希格斯机制与质量生成。

Method: 将希格斯场的规范取向作为动力学内部参考帧，定义相对于该帧的‘关系型规范联络’；在存在完整或部分希格斯帧的区域上分析动能项诱导的质量二次型；在量子场论中通过重正化的规范不变复合关联函数或残余荷 sectors 中的完全 dressed 算符刻画物理质量。

Result: 成功重构了BEH机制：在正则区域复现标准树图质量；完整希格斯帧给出完全规范不变的关系联络，部分帧仅在G/H方向产生希格斯刚度；应用于电弱理论时，SU(2)_L 规范冗余被关系化，残留U(1)_em高斯定律，W±与带电费米子需电磁dress，中性通道可有局域规范不变插值子。

Conclusion: BEH机制可完全在规范不变、关系性、内部参考系的框架下理解，质量生成是局域动力学现象，不依赖全局对称性实现或拓扑障碍；该视角统一了希格斯dress、FMS复合算符与内部量子参考系语言。

Abstract: The Brout--Englert--Higgs (BEH) mechanism can be formulated in gauge-invariant relational terms without assigning physical spontaneous breaking to a local gauge redundancy. We treat the gauge orientation of the Higgs field as dynamical internal reference data and describe the gauge connection relative to this Higgs frame. On a regular patch where the frame exists, the Higgs kinetic term induces a positive-semidefinite mass-squared quadratic form on the gauge-orbit directions and reproduces the standard tree-level vector masses. A complete Higgs frame yields a gauge-invariant relational connection, whereas a partial frame is defined only up to a stabilizer group $H$ and produces Higgs stiffness only along the coset directions $G/H$. In quantum field theory the normalized frame and relational connection are nonpolynomial composite variables; physical masses are therefore characterized through suitably renormalized gauge-invariant composite correlators, or through fully dressed operators in residual-charged sectors. Applying this construction to the electroweak theory, we show that the Higgs frame relationalizes the $SU(2)_L$ redundancy while leaving a residual electromagnetic $U(1)_{\rm em}$ Gauss law: $W^\pm$ and charged fermionic modes require electromagnetic dressing, whereas neutral channels admit local gauge-invariant interpolators. This formulation connects Higgs dressings, FMS composites, and internal quantum-reference-frame language while keeping local mass generation distinct from global symmetry realization and topological obstructions.

</details>


### [216] [Yang-Baxter deformed sigma models on $\mathrm{SU}(N)/\mathrm{SO}(N)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17362)
*Danila Alexandrov,Alexey Bychkov,Ivan Kalinichenko,Alexey Litvinov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧致对称空间SU(N)/SO(N)上二维sigma模型的Yang-Baxter形变，重点关注其紫外行为，提出紫外不动点由非紧WZW模型SO(N,C)/SO(N,R)和N-1个紧致自由玻色子描述，并确定了该不动点的主修正项及其对应的筛选电流扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Yang-Baxter形变下二维sigma模型的紫外行为，特别是其不动点结构及修正项的物理起源。

Method: 基于对称空间SU(N)/SO(N)及其推广的极大秩对称空间，结合WZW模型与筛选电流理论，分析Yang-Baxter形变后的紫外不动点及其微扰修正。

Result: 发现紫外不动点由非紧WZW模型SO(N,C)/SO(N,R)与N-1个紧致自由玻色子构成，并识别出主修正项对应于特定筛选电流产生的扰动。

Conclusion: Yang-Baxter形变的紫外极限具有清晰的共形场论描述，且可推广至更广的极大秩对称空间，揭示了形变、非紧性与筛选结构之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We study Yang--Baxter deformations of two-dimensional sigma models on compact symmetric spaces $\mathrm{SU}(N)/\mathrm{SO}(N)$, with particular emphasis on their ultraviolet behavior. We claim that the ultraviolet fixed point is described by the $\mathrm{SO}(N,\mathbb C)/\mathrm{SO}(N,\mathbb R)$ non-compact WZW model together with $N-1$ compact free bosons. We determine the leading correction to this fixed point and identify it with the perturbation generated by the corresponding screening currents. We also extend our construction to the broader class of symmetric spaces of maximal rank.

</details>


### [217] [Type I-II/II-I vortices from dimension-six self-interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17437)
*A. Alonso-Izquierdo,A. González-Parra,K. Slawinska,A. Wereszczynski,T. Winyard*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过在阿贝尔-希格斯或金兹堡-朗道模型中引入一个维度六的自相互作用项，揭示了多涡旋解的丰富结构，发现了两种新型涡旋类型：I-II型（短程吸引、长程排斥）和II-I型（短程排斥、长程吸引），并分析了其稳定性与构型特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阿贝尔-希格斯/金兹堡-朗道模型仅支持I型（吸引）和II型（排斥）涡旋，本文旨在探索更高维算符对涡旋相互作用及多体结构的影响，拓展对超导/规范场中拓扑激发的理解。

Method: 在阿贝尔-希格斯或Ginzburg-Landau模型中引入一个维度六的标量场自相互作用项，通过变分与数值求解静态势能极小化问题，系统分析不同参数区域下多涡旋解的存在性、轴对称性、能量稳定性及空间构型（如Abrikosov晶格、团簇、轴对称态）。

Result: 发现除标准I型与II型外，存在I-II型（短程吸引、长程排斥）和II-I型（短程排斥、长程吸引）涡旋；II-I型倾向于形成Abrikosov晶格片段或团簇，I-II型支持稳定的轴对称多涡旋态，但其是否为基态取决于参数与拓扑荷。

Conclusion: 维度六自相互作用显著改变涡旋间有效势的渐进行为，催生新的分类与物态，为理解强耦合超导、类比引力系统及人工规范场中的非平凡拓扑结构提供了新范式。

Abstract: We show that adding a dimension-six self-interaction to the Abelian-Higgs or Ginzburg Landau model results in a remarkable and rich structure of multi-vortex solutions. Besides the standard type I (attractive) and type II (repulsive) regimes, separated by the Bogomol'nyi-Prasad-Sommerfeld limit (BPS), we identify two new possibilities: type I-II vortices, which attract at short distances and repel at long distances; and type II-I vortices, with the opposite behaviour. Specifically, within the type II-I regime, multi-vortex solutions form chunks of the Abrikosov lattice or clusters. Conversely, in the I-II regime, axially symmetric multi-vortices are stable; nevertheless, depending on the model parameters and topological charge (winding number), they can be either the ground state or not.

</details>


### [218] [Evading the Swampland de Sitter Obstruction via Thermodynamic Duality-Induced Horizon Corrections in Unimodular Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17438)
*Reza Baghbani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在热力学对偶原理导出的量子修正视界热力学背景下，幺模引力与幂律非线性电动力学耦合的早期宇宙学意义，推导出含H^4和H^6项的修正弗里德曼方程，自然实现无需基本暴涨子势的暴涨，并满足Swampland猜想，预测的n_s和r与Planck 2018数据高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于热力学对偶原理的量子修正视界热力学如何影响早期宇宙暴涨动力学，并构建一个无需人工设定暴涨子势、且与量子引力框架（如Swampland纲领）兼容的自洽暴涨模型。

Method: 将幺模引力与幂律非线性电动力学耦合，引入含对数和反面积项的Bekenstein-Hawking熵量子修正，由此导出含H^4和H^6高阶项的修正弗里德曼方程；结合相空间分析、Swampland约束检验及暴涨观测量（ns, r）计算进行系统研究。

Result: 发现量子修正可自然驱动早期de Sitter型吸引子，实现稳健暴涨；修正因子F(H)使有效慢滚参数满足Swampland de Sitter与距离猜想；理论预言的谱指数ns和张标比r与Planck 2018观测高度一致。

Conclusion: 由热力学对偶诱导的视界量子修正，为在坚实量子引力框架下实现可检验的暴涨提供了一条可行新路径。

Abstract: We investigate the early-universe cosmological implications of Unimodular Gravity coupled to Power-Law Nonlinear Electrodynamics in the presence of quantum-corrected horizon thermodynamics derived from the principle of thermodynamic duality. By incorporating both logarithmic and inverse-area corrections to the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, we derive modified Friedmann equations exhibiting novel $H^4$ and $H^6$ terms that represent genuine quantum gravity effects. We demonstrate that these higher-order corrections become dynamically significant in the high-energy regime of cosmic inflation, naturally modifying the slow-roll dynamics without requiring a fundamental inflaton potential. A comprehensive phase-space analysis reveals that the quantum corrections drive early-time de Sitter-like attractors, providing a robust mechanism for sustained inflationary expansion. Confronting the model with the Swampland program, we show that the correction factor $\mathcal{F}(H)$ modifies the effective slow-roll parameters, enabling the model to satisfy the Swampland de Sitter and Distance conjectures during the inflationary epoch. We calculate the inflationary observables, including the spectral index $n_s$ and the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, and demonstrate that the model's predictions are in excellent agreement with the Planck 2018 observational constraints. Our results suggest that thermodynamic duality-induced horizon corrections offer a viable and testable pathway for realizing successful inflation within a well-motivated quantum-gravitational framework.

</details>


### [219] [Resonant JPZ algebras and 3D Chern--Simons gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17480)
*Remigiusz Durka,Nelson Merino,D. M. Peñafiel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于六种共振JPZ代数的三维Chern-Simons引力理论，发现其中三种代数B₄、C̃₄和C₄同构于iso(2,1)⊕so(2,1)，但在固定JPZ场识别下给出不同的引力实现；系统推导了不变张量、规范曲率、Chern-Simons作用量、场方程及最大秩耦合部分，并将场方程统一写为ΩσF=0形式，揭示了附加场hᵃ对曲率与挠率的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展三维Chern-Simons引力理论框架，探索由Lorentz生成元Jₐ、平移Pₐ和额外Lorentz型生成元Zₐ构成的共振JPZ代数所允许的引力构造，理解不同代数结构如何导致物理上可区分的引力实现。

Method: 利用半群扩张方法结合共振条件与Jacobi恒等式，系统分类出六种JPZ代数；对每种代数构造对应的Chern-Simons理论，计算不变张量、规范曲率、作用量与场方程；通过直接和同构关系分析局域度规结构，并关联已知全局与渐近解。

Result: 确认六种共振JPZ代数中B₄、C̃₄、C₄同构于iso(2,1)⊕so(2,1)，但因保持原始(J,P,Z)场识别而给出不同引力实现；导出统一形式的场方程ΩσF=0；明确各理论中附加场hᵃ对曲率、挠率及其他规范曲率分量的贡献；完成局域度规扇区分类并联系已有解。

Conclusion: 共振JPZ代数为三维Chern-Simons引力提供了丰富且物理可区分的扩展路径；即使代数同构，原始场识别的保持可导致不同几何与动力学诠释；ΩσF=0形式凸显了hᵃ场在广义曲率结构中的核心作用，为后续研究高阶耦合与渐近对称性奠定基础。

Abstract: In this work we construct three-dimensional Chern--Simons gravity theories for the six resonant $JPZ$ algebras generated by the Lorentz generators $J_a$, translations $P_a$, and an additional Lorentz-like generator $Z_a$. The resonance condition together with the Jacobi identities leaves precisely six configurations arising from the semigroup expansions. Our main result is that three of them, $B_4$, $\widetilde C_4$, and $C_4$, are isomorphic to $iso(2,1)\oplus so(2,1)$, although they yield different gravitational realizations when we keep the original $JPZ$ field identification fixed. For each case $JPZ$, we determine the invariant tensors, gauge curvatures, Chern--Simons action, field equations, and the corresponding maximal-rank coupling sectors. The field equations can be written in the compact form $Ω_σ\mathbf F=0$, which makes explicit how the additional field $h^a$ contributes to the curvature, torsion, and the remaining gauge-curvature components. We use the direct-sum isomorphisms to classify the local metric sectors and to relate them to known global and asymptotic solutions, while keeping the gravitational interpretation tied to the original $(ω,e,h)$ fields.

</details>


### [220] [Beyond the circle: current algebras, geometry and higher gauge structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17781)
*R. Campoamor-Stursberg,A. Marrani,M. Rausch de Traubenberg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将圆替换为流形后产生的电流李代数及其中心和导子扩张，分离了系数代数、调和分析与几何数据，并通过微分形式模去恰当形式描述中心扩张，利用无散度向量场给出显式上循环；同时比较了多个研究团队在该方向上的不同方法，并推广到高阶李代数的中心扩张条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典圆上的电流代数推广到一般流形上，以统一处理不同几何背景下的对称性结构与无穷维李代数扩张问题。

Method: 采用微分几何与上同调方法，分离系数代数、调和分析与几何数据；利用微分形式模恰当形式刻画中心扩张，用无散度向量场构造显式2-上循环，并分析其相容导子满足的稳定子方程；结合调和分析、共形几何与自由微分代数工具进行比较与推广。

Result: 给出了流形上电流李代数中心扩张的一般框架；明确了三球面等紧致流形上的电流–Virasoro扩张；建立了系数代数构造与高阶括号的联系；推导出F阶李代数中心扩张需满足耦合的二元与对称F元条件，并给出具体例子与障碍。

Conclusion: 流形上的电流代数结构可系统地由几何、调和与代数三要素分离刻画；中心扩张本质上由上同调类与无散度场决定；该框架统一了多个物理与数学方向的工作，并自然推广至高阶代数结构。

Abstract: Replacing the circle by a manifold leads to current Lie algebras and their central and derivation extensions. We separate the coefficient algebra, harmonic analysis and geometric data. Differentials modulo exact forms describe central extensions; divergence-free vector fields give explicit cocycles, whose compatible derivations satisfy a stabilizer equation. Compact examples include the three-sphere and its current--Virasoro extension. We also explain the analytic qualifications for SL(2,R) and its symmetric space and the role of transported multiplication in soft geometries. The recent harmonic constructions of Campoamor-Stursberg, Marrani and Rausch de Traubenberg are compared with the cohomological work of Coquereaux, Frappat, Ragoucy and Sorba and Castellani's loop geometry and infinite-dimensional free differential algebras. A coefficient-algebra construction connects currents to higher brackets and clarifies the additional requirements of a dynamical theory. For Lie algebras of order F, central extensions obey coupled binary and symmetric F-ary conditions, with explicit examples and obstructions.

</details>


### [221] [Construction and geometric properties of the fuzzy $CP^3$ onion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17807)
*Kohei Uno,Tomohiro Inagaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逐层增大矩阵尺寸的模糊CP^3构造七维量子空间的矩阵模型，证明其连续极限是CP^3上的度量锥C(CP^3)，而非平坦R^7；该几何结构是模型的预测结果而非人为输入，并在拉普拉斯算子谱、传播子和热扩散等多方面验证了其高精度收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非平直高维量子空间的自然涌现机制，检验模糊几何能否从离散矩阵模型中导出非平凡连续几何（如度量锥），而非预设背景几何。

Method: 构建由同心模糊CP^3构成的七维矩阵模型，利用第二量子化Schwinger玻色子提升的极分解推导层间映射；通过拉普拉斯算子谱、自由传播子及热核行为等可观测量，在不同角动量扇区（p≥1）系统比较其与C(CP^3)及R^7的收敛性。

Result: 模型在20480层时对p≤8角动量模式，其拉普拉斯谱与C(CP^3)匹配精度达1×10^{-9}，而与R^7偏差达8–18%；传播子与热扩散行为也一致复现C(CP^3)的r^6测度与贝塞尔谱；层间映射闭式解等同于Kováčik-Tekel复制规则；CP^1约化情形退化为已知‘模糊洋葱’模型。

Conclusion: 该矩阵模型天然预言C(CP^3)作为七维量子空间的连续极限，表明非平直几何可自洽地从模糊结构中涌现，为量子引力中的背景无关几何提供了新范例。

Abstract: We consider the matrix model of a seven-dimensional quantum space built from concentric fuzzy $CP^3$'s of increasing matrix size, and show that its continuum limit is the metric cone $C(CP^3)$ over $CP^3$, not flat $R^7$. Every ingredient is fixed before any comparison, so this geometry is a prediction rather than an input. The two geometries agree in the $s$ wave ($p=0$) but separate in the higher angular sectors (harmonics on $CP^3$ of degree $p\geq1$): in each sector tested, the spectrum of the free Laplacian converges at second order to that of the cone, and at 20480 layers the cone is matched to $1\times10^{-9}$ for $p\leq8$, whereas flat space misses by 8-18%. The free propagator converges to that of the cone at the same order, and heat diffusion reproduces the $r^6$ measure and the Bessel spectrum of the cone. The maps between neighbouring layers are obtained in closed form from the polar decomposition of the second-quantized Schwinger-boson lift, using a known operator identity, and coincide with the Kováčik-Tekel coefficient-copying prescription. For $CP^1$ the construction reduces, away from the outer boundary, to the Kováčik-Tekel fuzzy onion. Modes along the twistor fibre are exactly degenerate with the harmonics on $S^4$.

</details>


### [222] [Supersymmetric Observables from Surface Defect Anomalies in 4d and 6d SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18086)
*Zi-Xiao Huang,Ma-Ke Yuan,Yang Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了半BPS表面缺陷对四维和六维超共形场论中超对称Casimir能量和超对称Rényi熵的普适对数项的贡献，通过等变积分缺陷反常多项式及其在超对称Rényi背景下的推广，得到了用缺陷't Hooft反常表示的闭合表达式，并通过多种方法进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解半BPS表面缺陷对超对称可观测量（如Casimir能量和Rényi熵）的影响，尤其是其与缺陷反常之间的关系。

Method: 利用缺陷反常多项式的等变积分及其在超对称Rényi背景下的推广，结合chiral代数、五维定位、大N全息对偶及局域手征费米子模型进行交叉验证。

Result: 得到了Casimir能量和Rényi熵对数项关于缺陷't Hooft反常的闭合表达式；四维N=4理论中仅依赖R-对称反常，六维N=(1,0)理论中还需考虑引力反常；并复现了六维N=(2,0)理论中的Wilson面结果。

Conclusion: 缺陷反常（特别是't Hooft反常）完全控制了相关超对称可观测量，该框架统一描述了多种缺陷类型与维度，并经多方法验证可靠。

Abstract: We study the contributions of half-BPS surface defects to supersymmetric Casimir energy and the universal logarithmic term of supersymmetric Rényi entropy in four- and six-dimensional SCFTs. Using equivariant integration of defect anomaly polynomials and its extension to supersymmetric Rényi backgrounds, we obtain closed expressions for these observables in terms of defect 't Hooft anomalies. For Gukov--Witten defects preserving transverse rotations in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = 4$ super Yang--Mills theories, both observables depend on a single R-symmetry anomaly. For $\mathcal{N} = (0, 4)$ defects in six-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = (1, 0)$ theories, additional terms arise from the gravitational anomaly. We provide complementary checks using chiral algebras, five-dimensional localization, and holographic probe branes at large $N$, while localized chiral fermions reproduce the gravitational-anomaly contributions. The framework also recovers Wilson-surface results in six-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = (2, 0)$ theories.

</details>


### [223] [Geometry and holography of $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times\mathrm{S}^2$: towards the field theory dual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18344)
*Miroslava Mosso Rojas,Anayeli Ramirez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次尝试识别超共形量子力学对偶于超对称AdS₂×S²解，并聚焦于Type IIB中保持8个超荷的AdS₂×S²×CY₂×Σ解；提出其对偶N=4量子力学组织在Σ上的二维格点上，由沿x₁的线性quiver构成，每段x₂区间对应一个quiver副本，其秩由对应单元的D1/D5电荷决定；验证了中心荷与全息结果一致，并将SL(2,R)及SL(2,R)×SU(2)非阿贝尔T对偶解纳入该框架，给出全局补全并解释沿x₂的F弦为重子顶点；最后将该构造推广至定义函数在x₁和x₂可分离的一般解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找AdS₂×S²超对称背景的超共形量子力学对偶，填补AdS₂/CFT₁对应关系中的关键空白，并统一理解包括非阿贝尔T对偶在内的广泛解族。

Method: 基于Type IIB中AdS₂×S²×CY₂×Σ八超荷解的brane内容（D1-D5-NS5加D3/D7味brane），构建二维格点结构的N=4量子力学模型：沿x₁为线性quiver，沿x₂分段赋秩；通过中心荷计算验证全息一致性；将已知SL(2,R)与SL(2,R)×SU(2)非阿贝尔T对偶解纳入该框架，并为其添加D3/D7补全以实现全局解；将F弦解释为baryon vertex；最后推广至定义函数可分离的一般解。

Result: 提出了首个系统性AdS₂×S²背景的N=4超共形量子力学对偶构造，覆盖非阿贝尔T对偶族及更广的可分离解类；中心荷匹配全息计算；给出了两个典型T对偶解的完整brane描述与全局补全；沿x₂的F弦被自然解释为重子顶点。

Conclusion: AdS₂×S²×CY₂×Σ八超荷解存在统一的二维格点N=4量子力学对偶，其结构由brane电荷决定，且可推广至含可分离函数的更广泛解族；该框架为AdS₂/CFT₁提供了具体、可计算的微观实现，并深化了非阿贝尔T对偶的物理诠释。

Abstract: We take a first step in identifying the superconformal quantum mechanics dual to supersymmetric AdS$_2\times$S$^2$ solutions. We focus on the Type IIB solutions of the form AdS$_2\times$S$^2\times$CY$_2\timesΣ$ that preserve eight supercharges, with $Σ$ a two-dimensional space parametrised by $(x_1,x_2)$, and read off their brane content, a D1-D5-NS5 intersection with D3 and D7 flavour branes. We propose that their dual $\mathcal{N}=4$ quantum mechanics is organised over a two-dimensional lattice of cells in $Σ$. Namely, it consists of copies of the linear quiver along $x_1$ dual to the U(1)-invariant solutions of the class, one for each interval along $x_2$, with ranks given by the D1 and D5 charges of the corresponding cell. Its central charge agrees with the holographic one at leading order. We show that the family of SL$(2,\mathbb{R})$ non-Abelian T-duals of the AdS$_3\times$S$^2\times$CY$_2$ solutions of massive Type IIA supergravity, constructed in previous work, belongs to this class, as does the SL$(2,\mathbb{R})\times$SU$(2)$ non-Abelian T-dual of AdS$_3\times$S$^3\times$CY$_2$, and work both out in detail. For both we propose global completions that introduce additional D3 and D7 branes. Along $x_2$ we interpret the fundamental strings stretched between the colour branes and the additional D3 and D7 branes as baryon vertices. Finally, we extend this proposal from the non-Abelian T-duals to the solutions of the class whose defining functions factorise in $x_1$ and $x_2$, including those in which the AdS$_2$ and S$^2$ factors have the same warp factor.

</details>


### [224] [Giant Graviton Expansions from Symmetric Functions and Free Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18452)
*Min-xin Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过代数方法严格证明了四维N=4 U(N)超杨-米尔斯理论中两个关于巨型引力子展开的重要关系：一是Gaiotto-Lee单求和展开，利用Hall-Littlewood正交性和算子迹计算证明；二是带味Schur指标的对称双求和展开，通过其自由费米子模乘积的因式分解证明。


<details>
