  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为严格验证巨型引力子展开在超对称指标中的有限秩修正表达，并厘清不同展开形式（单求和、双求和、三类粒子展开）之间的数学基础与适用范围。

Method: 采用Hall-Littlewood正交性与算子迹计算证明单求和展开；利用自由费米子模式乘积的精确因式分解证明对称双求和展开；并明确指定膜指标的解析延拓及系数级逐项合理性。

Result: 成功给出两个关键展开式（Gaiotto-Lee单求和与 flavored Schur 的对称双求和）的严格代数证明，明确了各自适用的展开约定，并指出这些证明未覆盖Imamura提出的三类粒子完整展开。

Conclusion: 两项证明巩固了巨型引力子图像在指标计算中的代数基础，但揭示了三类粒子展开及Schur指标模性质所引发的新物理与数学问题，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Giant graviton expansions express finite-rank corrections to supersymmetric indices in terms of indices for wrapped D3-branes. We give detailed algebraic proofs of two previously proposed relations for four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ $U(N)$ super-Yang--Mills theory. The first is the Gaiotto--Lee single-sum expansion of the full superconformal index, proved by Hall--Littlewood orthogonality and an elementary calculation of operator traces. The second is the symmetric double-sum expansion of the flavored Schur index, proved by factorizing its exact free-fermion mode product. We specify the analytic continuation of the brane indices and justify the sums coefficient by coefficient. The two proofs apply to different expansion prescriptions and do not establish the full three-species expansion of Imamura. We discuss the additional physical and mathematical questions raised by that expansion and by the modular properties of the Schur index.

</details>


### [225] [Black Strings and Black Holes: Worldsheet Spectra and Near-Extremal Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18591)
*Ziyi Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了SL(2,R)×U(1)/U(1)规范WZW模型的欧几里得配分函数，分析了其在不同几何（光滑无 horizon 几何与 Horne-Horowitz 黑弦）下的世界面谱与时空谱，并探讨了二维与三维近极值黑洞/黑弦中的态密度及对数熵行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非紧致共形场论（如SL(2,R)相关模型）在AdS/CFT对应中对黑洞微观态与熵（尤其是AdS₂喉区的3/2 log T行为）的贡献，特别是通过配分函数解析揭示离散级列表示所对应的物理态。

Method: 利用全息积分计算世界面特征标分解；直接执行未折叠模积分以获得时空谱；分析维数约化后的二维带电黑洞与三维黑弦的近极值极限；识别离散级列表示中产生对数熵的候选态塔。

Result: 成功恢复无规范极限下的Maldacena-Ooguri-Son谱；为二维带电黑洞提出两个能复现AdS₂喉区3/2 log T熵行为的态塔；发现这些态仅存在于配分函数发散温度以下；给出了近极值极限下这些态对全配分函数的可能贡献。

Conclusion: SL(2,R)规范WZW模型中的离散级列表示可自然导出AdS₂对数熵，支持其作为近极值黑洞微观描述的有效性；但该机制受限于温度阈值，暗示需进一步理解发散起源及更高阶修正。

Abstract: We study aspects of the Euclidean partition function of the $\frac{SL(2,\mathbb R)\times U(1)}{U(1)}$ gauged WZW model. The target space geometry is either a smooth horizonless geometry with a linear-dilaton asymptotic region, or the Horne--Horowitz black string, depending on which circles we unwind as the time-direction. We first study the worldsheet spectrum and obtain the character decomposition via the holonomy integrals. Next, we obtain the spacetime spectrum of the horizonless geometry by directly performing the unfolded modular integrals, and recover the Maldacena-Ooguri-Son spectrum in the ungauged limit. When the chirally charged three-dimensional black string is dimensionally reduced, the target space is instead a two-dimensional charged black hole. We study the near extremal regime of the black hole and black string. For the two dimensional black hole, we propose two candidate towers of states that produce the expected $\frac{3}{2}\log T$ of the AdS$_2$ throat from the worldsheet discrete series representation. These towers appear only below the temperature at which the full amplitude becomes divergent. We comment on the contribution of these states in the near-extremal limit of the full partition function.

</details>


### [226] [Carrollian Yang-Mills theories in the homogeneous temporal gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18715)
*Carmelo P. Martin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在均匀时间规范下，使用BRST路径积分方法对Carrollian Yang-Mills理论（电型与磁型）进行量子化分析，发现电型理论为自由理论，而磁型理论中仅存在树图级三、四点函数依赖耦合常数，且仅含基本场的格林函数无圈图修正，威尔逊圈相关格林函数在磁型中平凡而在电型中非平凡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carrollian极限下的Yang-Mills理论在极端相对论极限（如近光速或强引力场边界）中具有基础意义，但其量子化性质尚不明确，尤其电型与磁型Carrollian理论的可重整性、相互作用结构及拓扑可观测量（如威尔逊圈）行为亟待系统研究。

Method: 采用齐次时间规范（homogeneous temporal gauge），结合BRST对称性与路径积分量子化框架，分别处理SU(N)规范群下电型与磁型Carrollian Yang-Mills理论，通过分析费曼规则、顶点结构及圈图贡献，计算基本场格林函数与威尔逊圈关联函数。

Result: 电型Carrollian Yang-Mills理论所有相互作用顶点消失，成为自由理论；磁型理论中，仅ξ_i场的三、四点函数在树图级依赖耦合常数，其余基本场格林函数无圈图修正；磁型中仅含威尔逊圈的格林函数恒为1（平凡），而电型中非平凡。

Conclusion: Carrollian Yang-Mills理论的量子结构高度依赖于电/磁分裂：电型完全无相互作用，磁型虽具有限相互作用但被严格限制在低阶树图，且威尔逊圈动力学仅在电型中活跃，揭示了Carroll对称性对规范理论量子行为的根本约束。

Abstract: We quantize, in the homogeneous temporal gauge, both the electric and the magnetic Carrollian Yang-Mills theories with SU(N) gauge symmetry in the fundamental representation. We use the BRST method implemented in the path integral. We show that in the first case all the interaction vertices vanish, thus, giving a free theory. In the magnetic case, there are no loop contributions to the Green functions of the elementary fields --i.e., Green functions not involving composite operators-- and the only connected parts of such Green functions with a dependence on the coupling constant are the tree level contributions to the three and four point functions of the field $ξ_i$. We also show that in the magnetic case the Green functions involving only Wilson loops are trivial and not so in the electric case.

</details>


### [227] [Imaginary HTEE: Probing Criticality and Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18716)
*M. Ali-Akbari,M. M. Daryaei Goki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电渐近AdS5黑洞背景下的全息类时间纠缠熵（HTEE）虚部，发现其在临界点附近呈现平方根发散行为，并提取出临界指数θ_I = 1/2，表明虚部HTEE可作为强耦合系统中临界性的非局域量子信息探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强耦合系统中临界现象的非局域量子信息表征，特别是利用全息原理下类时间纠缠熵的虚部来探测相变和量子相干性。

Method: 基于全息对偶，在带电渐近AdS5黑洞背景下计算虚部全息类时间纠缠熵（HTEE），将其与伪熵虚部等同，并解析推导其随化学势μ和温度T变化的行为，尤其分析其在热力学稳定与不稳定分支上的临界特性。

Result: 虚部HTEE在无电荷极限下为零，完全由非零化学势诱导；当μ/T趋近临界值时，其导数呈逆平方根发散，得到临界指数θ_I = 1/2；该指数同样支配固定化学势下的温度响应；临界点处初末态达到最大量子相干。

Conclusion: 虚部HTEE是一种有效的非局域量子信息探针，可用于刻画强耦合系统中的临界行为，且其临界指数揭示了量子相干性在相变点的普适增强。

Abstract: We study the imaginary part of the holographic timelike entanglement entropy (HTEE) in a charged asymptotically AdS$_5$ black-hole background whose dual field theory exhibits a critical point in the presence of a chemical potential $μ$ and temperature $T$. Identifying the imaginary part of the HTEE with the imaginary part of the pseudoentropy, we derive an analytical expression for this quantity and investigate its behavior on the stable and unstable thermodynamic branches. We find that the imaginary HTEE vanishes in the uncharged limit and is induced entirely by a nonzero chemical potential, signaling the emergence of quantum coherence between the initial and final states. As the dimensionless ratio $μ/T$ approaches its critical value, we obtain an inverse-square-root divergence in the derivative of the imaginary HTEE and extract the critical exponent $θ_I = 1/2$ analytically. We further show that the same exponent governs the temperature response at fixed chemical potential. Our results establish the imaginary part of the HTEE as a non-local quantum-information probe of criticality in strongly coupled systems, and they imply that the initial and final states become maximally coherent at the critical point.

</details>


### [228] [Late-time electric screening in dS from EAdS loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18782)
*Ernesto Bianchi,Sacha Ployet,Charlotte Sleight,Massimo Taronna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在任意维度和标量质量的dS_{d+1}空间中，利用EAdS重表述的in-in形式论解析计算了标量QED中规范场的一圈两点函数；发现Dirichlet规范场获得Schwinger型质量，其纵向模由满足相反边界条件的带电标量复合体提供，而Neumann模受规范不变性保护；该结果重现了边界多重态重组现象，并通过谱Cutkosky规则将核的不连续性与两粒子割相联系，最终将一圈修正的Dirichlet光子解释为dS共振（即准正规模）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解dS时空中量子场论中规范场的圈修正行为，特别是其质量生成机制、边界算符反常维数及与准正规模物理的联系。

Method: 采用EAdS重表述的in-in形式论进行解析计算；引入谱Cutkosky规则分析Casimir平面主级数割上的不连续性；利用超几何函数{}_7F_6和Gamma函数表达圈修正，并通过色散关系提取非局域部分。

Result: 1) Dirichlet规范场获得Schwinger型质量，纵向模源于带电标量复合体；2) 边界电流获得反常维数γ_J，电场晚期衰减行为被修改；3) 一圈修正的Dirichlet光子在Casimir复平面第二片上表现为极点，可解释为dS共振/准正规模；4) 建立谱Cutkosky规则，将核的不连续性归结为双粒子壳层割，且割部分简化为Gamma函数乘积。

Conclusion: 标量QED中dS规范场的一圈效应不仅导致质量生成与反常维数，更自然地对应于dS中的准正规模，揭示了dS量子场论中bulk共振与boundary CFT数据之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We compute the one-loop gauge-field two-point functions analytically in scalar QED on dS$_{d+1}$ for general dimension and scalar mass, using the EAdS reformulation of the in-in formalism arXiv:2007.09993. The Dirichlet EAdS gauge field acquires a Schwinger-type mass, with its longitudinal mode supplied by a composite of charged scalars obeying opposite boundary conditions, while the Neumann mode remains protected by gauge invariance. In dS, the Dirichlet correction induces an anomalous dimension $γ_J$ for the boundary current and modifies the late-time falloff of the electric field. This bulk calculation reproduces the boundary multiplet-recombination result of arXiv:2509.18888. Summing over the in-in contour branches gives the retarded dS bubble kernel, making direct contact with the Bros-Moschella framework. We establish a spectral Cutkosky rule that expresses the discontinuity of this kernel across the principal-series cut in the Casimir plane in terms of a two-particle cut, with both internal scalar lines placed on shell. Although the full bubble coefficient involves a ${}_7F_6$ hypergeometric function, the cut collapses to a closed product of Gamma functions and determines the complete non-local part of the kernel through a dispersion relation. Analytic continuation of the same cut reproduces the complete one-loop result for $γ_J$. In the complex Casimir plane, the loop-corrected Dirichlet photon appears as a pole on the second sheet and is therefore naturally interpreted as a dS resonance, or quasinormal mode, whose frequency is shifted at one loop.

</details>

### [229] [Ultralight scalars and invisible gravitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14119)
*Massimo Giovannini*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了超轻玻色子在暴胀后宇宙演化中对非绝热模与曲率扰动绝热谱相对权重的影响，指出该影响依赖于暴胀后膨胀速率的时间线，并给出了相关观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免超轻玻色子引起的非绝热模与曲率扰动绝热谱相比过大，从而与大尺度观测相冲突。

Method: 分析暴胀后不同时期（特别是减速膨胀阶段）的宇宙膨胀速率对非绝热与绝热扰动相对幅度的影响，并结合核合成与高频原初引力波谱等观测限制进行约束。

Result: 若暴胀后减速膨胀阶段比辐射主导时期更慢且持续足够久，则非绝热与绝热分量之比可满足大尺度观测约束；反之，若存在长时间快于辐射主导的膨胀阶段，则排除超轻玻色子的存在。

Conclusion: 超轻标量场的质量范围、减速阶段持续时间及辐射主导延迟时间均受到轻核合成与高频原初引力波谱的联合限制。

Abstract: Unless the tensor to scalar ratio is small enough, the non-adiabatic mode associated with the ultralight bosons may be dangerously comparable with the adiabatic spectrum of curvature inhomogeneities. We point out here that this statement depends on the timeline of the post-inflationary expansion rate. Provided the decelerated evolution is slower than radiation for a sufficiently long period, the relative weight of the non-adiabatic and adiabatic components does not conflict with the direct constraints coming from the large-scale observations. Conversely the presence of ultralight bosons excludes the presence of long stages expanding faster than radiation. The synthesis of the light nuclei and the spectrum of the relic gravitons at high frequencies set the relevant constraints on the duration of the decelerated stages, on the delayed dominance of radiation and on the mass range of the ultralight scalars.

</details>


### [230] [Where Cluster Galaxies Reside and How Strongly They Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14167)
*Isaque Dutra,Priyamvada Natarajan,Cian Roche*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过比较哈勃太空望远镜观测到的强引力透镜星系团与TNG-Cluster模拟中类似星系团，发现观测中星系-星系强透镜（GGSL）发生率显著高于ΛCDM模拟预测，该差异源于星系成员内部质量集中度不足及其在星系团势场中位置分布的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测到的星系-星系强透镜（GGSL）概率比ΛCDM模型预测高近一个数量级，但尚不清楚这是由星系内部结构还是其所处环境（星系团势场）主导所致。

Method: 将HST观测的强透镜星系团与TNG-Cluster模拟中红移匹配、主爱因斯坦半径匹配的类比样本进行对比；对每个成员星系分别测量其孤立状态和嵌入星系团场中的透镜强度，并据此分类为Major/Minor Perturbers（临界区）或Intrinsic/Boosted/Inert Members（亚临界区）；进一步检验质量守恒的暗物质收缩模型（模拟核坍缩自相互作用暗物质）的影响。

Result: 观测星系团中位于星系团临界区域的成员占比（33.7%）显著高于TNG-Cluster（21.6%）；Major与Minor比例相似；但在亚临界区，观测中Intrinsic和Boosted Lenses比例更高（Boosted Lens达25.8% vs 模拟13.5%），Inert Member更少；该差异在固定子晕质量及计入视线方向结构后仍存在；引入模拟核坍缩暗物质可部分恢复条件透镜比例，但无法恢复临界区占有率。

Conclusion: GGSL观测-模拟差异同时源于模拟星系成员中心质量集中度不足，以及它们在星系团透镜势场中空间分布的系统性偏差。

Abstract: The efficiency of galaxy-galaxy strong lensing (GGSL) in clusters depends jointly on the inner mass distributions of member galaxies and their locations within the cluster potential. Observed GGSL probabilities exceed $Λ$CDM predictions by nearly an order of magnitude, but the relative roles of internal structure and environment remain unclear. We disentangle these effects by comparing HST strong-lensing clusters with TNG-Cluster analogs at similar lens redshifts, matched by their primary Einstein radius. For each member, we measure its lensing strength in isolation and when embedded in the cluster field. Members in cluster-critical regions are classified as Major or Minor Perturbers depending on whether they strongly lens in isolation; those in subcritical regions are classified as Intrinsic Lenses, Boosted Lenses, or Inert Members according to whether they lens independently, only with environmental assistance, or not at all. Observed clusters contain 33.7% of selected members in cluster-critical regions, compared with 21.6% in TNG-Cluster, while their Major-to-Minor ratios are similar. Outside these regions, observations contain substantially more Intrinsic and Boosted Lenses and fewer Inert Members, with Boosted Lens fractions of 25.8% and 13.5%, respectively. The discrepancy persists at fixed subhalo mass and after including line-of-sight structure. A mass-conserving contraction mimicking core-collapsed self-interacting dark matter largely recovers the conditional lensing fractions but not cluster-critical occupancy. The GGSL discrepancy therefore reflects both insufficient central concentration in simulated members and differences in their locations within the cluster lensing field.

</details>


### [231] [TDCOSMO 2026: A 3% measurement of the Hubble constant from strong lensing and stellar kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14781)
*TDCOSMO Collaboration,Simon Birrer,Michele Cappellari,Frédéric Courbin,Christopher D. Fassnacht,Pierre Fleury,Joshua A. Frieman,Aymeric Galan,Xiang-Yu Huang,Daniel P. Johnson,Sanjana Khanolkar,Shawn Knabel,Hrvoje Krizic,Martin Millon,Takahiro Morishita,Veronica Motta,Pritom Mozumdar,Eric Paic,Hadrien Paugnat,Allan G. Schweinfurth,Anowar J. Shajib,William Sheu,Dominique Sluse,Massimo Stiavelli,Sherry H. Suyu,Tommaso Treu,Felipe Urcelay,Devon M. Williams,Kenneth C. Wong*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用11个具有时间延迟测量的强引力透镜类星体（TDCOSMO-2026样本），结合改进的盲分析方法（如JWST数据、轴对称动力学建模、Euclid模拟等），在ΛCDM模型下测得哈勃常数H₀ = 71.1⁺²·⁴₋₂·₃ km·s⁻¹·Mpc⁻¹（3.3%精度）；联合SLACS/SL2S样本后精度提升至3.0%；结果在多种暗能量模型中稳健，并对w = −1给出2.3–2.5σ偏离迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决哈勃常数测量中的系统误差与模型依赖性问题，提升强引力透镜时间延迟法的精度与可靠性，并检验ΛCDM及各类扩展宇宙学模型的一致性。

Method: 基于TDCOSMO-2026强透镜类星体样本，采用JWST-NIRSpec/NIRCam高分辨率动力学与成像数据，实施轴对称动力学建模（取代球对称加修正因子方法），并使用Euclid Flagship模拟估算视线方向物质汇聚（κ），联合DES-Dovekie超新星Ωₘ先验或DESI-DR2 BAO数据进行贝叶斯宇宙学参数推断。

Result: 在平坦ΛCDM下得H₀ = 71.1⁺²·⁴₋₂·₃ km·s⁻¹·Mpc⁻¹（3.3%）；联合SLACS+SL2S后达3.0%；在wCDM、w₀wₐCDM和w_φCDM中均一致；联合DESI-DR2与DES-Dovekie时，w = −1被排除2.3–2.5σ；例如w₀ = −0.88⁺⁰·⁰⁴₋₀·₀₅（w_φCDM）与w₀ = −0.85⁺⁰·⁰⁷₋₀·₀₆（w₀wₐCDM），wₐ ≈ −0.48 ± 0.45。

Conclusion: TDCOSMO-2026显著提升了强透镜测H₀的精度与稳健性；其结果与SLACS/SL2S高度一致；对标准ΛCDM中w = −1构成温和但多模型一致的张力，暗示可能需考虑动态暗能量。

Abstract: We present cosmological constraints from 11 strongly lensed quasars with measured time delays (hereafter, the TDCOSMO-2026 sample). In addition to expanding the sample, we improve many aspects of our blind analysis with respect to our 2025 analysis. Among other improvements, spatially resolved kinematics are available for nine systems, including six with JWST-NIRSpec, compared to one in 2025; for three lens models, JWST-NIRCam images now replace HST; axisymmetric dynamical modeling is applied directly to the spatially resolved data, instead of an axisymmetric correction factor applied to spherical models; and the line-of-sight convergence is now based on the Euclid Flagship simulations instead of the Millennium Simulation. Our baseline and primary result is based on the TDCOSMO-2026 sample, in combination with a prior on $Ω_{\rm m}$ from the DES-Dovekie supernova sample. In the flat $Λ$ cold dark matter ($Λ$CDM) cosmology, we find $H_0=71.1^{+2.4}_{-2.3}$ km.s$^{-1}$.Mpc$^{-1}$, a 3.3% uncertainty that accounts for the mass-sheet degeneracy. The SLACS and SL2S samples are in excellent agreement with the TDCOSMO-2026 sample, improving the precision on $H_0$ in flat $Λ$CDM to 3.0%. Using the DESI-DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO) likelihood instead of DES-Dovekie yields fully consistent results. We also present constraints in the open $Λ$CDM, $w$CDM, $w_0w_a$CDM, and $w_φ$CDM cosmologies; the TDCOSMO $H_0$ inference is robust and consistent across all of them. The combination of the TDCOSMO+SLACS+SL2S likelihood with those from DESI-DR2 and DES-Dovekie disfavors $w=-1$ at the $2.3$-$2.5σ$ level in all three dark energy extensions that we tested. We find, e.g., $w_0=-0.88^{+0.04}_{-0.05}$ in $w_φ$CDM and $w_0 = -0.85^{+0.07}_{-0.06}$ in $w_0w_a$CDM, while $w_a = -0.48^{+0.46}_{-0.44}$ remains consistent with zero.

</details>


### [232] [Gravitational waves from strong first-order phase transitions in an expanding Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14806)
*Jintao Zou,Zizhuo Zhao,Yongtao Jia,Ligong Bian*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过三维格点数值模拟研究了宇宙膨胀背景下一级相变产生的引力波，发现声波贡献远超泡碰撞，且声波谱呈现双峰结构；旋转模式随相变强度增大而增强，声波源寿命随相变强度减弱而延长；在缓燃情形下，引力波谱紫外端更陡、声波源寿命更短；与闵氏时空模拟相比，归一化引力波幅度约小一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙中一级相变产生的引力波信号对于探测早期宇宙物理至关重要，尤其需厘清不同产生机制（如泡碰撞、声波、湍流）的相对贡献及演化特性。

Method: 采用标量场-流体耦合模型，在膨胀宇宙背景下开展三维格点数值模拟，重点研究慢速、强耦合的一级相变过程，并分析引力波谱、声波谱及流体动力学行为。

Result: 声波对引力波的贡献显著主导泡碰撞；声波谱呈现两个峰值，分别对应平均泡间距和声壳宽度；碰撞中激发旋转模式，其幅度随相变强度增大；声波源寿命随相变参数α减小而增长，弱相变下可持续超哈勃时间；缓燃情形导致引力波谱紫外端更陡、声波源寿命缩短；归一化引力波幅度比闵氏时空模拟结果约小一个数量级。

Conclusion: 在膨胀宇宙中，声波是强一级相变引力波的主要来源，其谱形与寿命强烈依赖于相变强度和传播模式（如缓燃），且整体信号幅度低于平直时空预期，这对未来引力波观测的数据解读具有关键意义。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational waves generated by first-order phase transitions (PTs) in an expanding Universe. Using a scalar field-fluid coupled model, we perform three-dimensional lattice numerical simulations of slow first-order PTs with strong strength. We observe that the contribution from sound waves to the gravitational wave (GW) signal significantly dominates over that from bubble collisions. The sound wave spectra exhibit two peaks located at the mean bubble separation and the sound-shell width. Rotational modes are generated in the fluid during the bubble collisions, and their magnitude grows with the PT strength. The lifetime of the sound wave source increases with decreasing $α$: for weaker transitions the source can persist for longer than a Hubble time, while for stronger transitions it decays within a Hubble time. When the walls propagate as deflagrations, the GW spectral shape changes drastically, with steeper ultraviolet tails and a shorter-lived sound wave source. We also observe that the magnitude of the normalized GWs is roughly an order of magnitude smaller than obtained in the Minkowski spacetime simulations.

</details>


### [233] [TDCOSMO 2026: new insights on the Hubble tension from strong lensing time delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14881)
*TDCOSMO Collaboration,Simon Birrer,Michele Cappellari,Frédéric Courbin,Christopher D. Fassnacht,Pierre Fleury,Joshua A. Frieman,Aymeric Galan,Xiang-Yu Huang,Daniel P. Johnson,Sanjana Khanolkar,Shawn Knabel,Hrvoje Krizic,Martin Millon,Takahiro Morishita,Veronica Motta,Pritom Mozumdar,Eric Paic,Hadrien Paugnat,Allan G. Schweinfurth,Anowar J. Shajib,William Sheu,Dominique Sluse,Massimo Stiavelli,Sherry H. Suyu,Tommaso Treu,Felipe Urcelay,Devon M. Williams,Kenneth C. Wong*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文基于TDCOSMO-2026样本（11个多重成像类星体）结合HST/JWST成像与JWST/VLT/Keck光谱动力学建模，独立于本地距离阶梯测得H₀ = 71.1⁺²·⁴₋₂·₃ km·s⁻¹·Mpc⁻¹（3%精度），并证实Hubble张力在排除超新星、造父变星或红巨星支等任一SN相关方法后仍显著存在（如'无SN'组合下3.7σ，完全排除本地距离阶梯后仍达2.7σ）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前宇宙学中由晚期宇宙测量（如本地距离阶梯）与早期宇宙观测（如普朗克CMB）所得Hubble常数不一致所引发的'Hubble张力'问题，提供一种独立于本地距离阶梯的新测量路径。

Method: 采用盲分层分析法处理11个强引力透镜类星体的时间延迟数据；结合HST与JWST高分辨成像构建透镜质量模型，并联合JWST-NIRSpec、VLT-MUSE和Keck-KCWI光谱数据建立轴对称动力学模型；引入DES-Dovekie超新星数据约束Ωₘ，并考虑质量片简并的影响。

Result: 得到H₀ = 71.1⁺²·⁴₋₂·₃ km·s⁻¹·Mpc⁻¹（3%精度），是除传统本地距离阶梯外最精确的晚期宇宙H₀测量；构造'无SN'组合（以DESI-BAO替代DES-Dovekie约束Ωₘ）后仍与Planck CMB预测存在3.7σ张力；即使完全排除本地距离阶梯，张力仍达2.7σ。

Conclusion: 该工作进一步证实Hubble张力并非源于某单一晚期宇宙探针的系统误差，而更可能指向新物理或未被认知的系统性偏差，强化了对ΛCDM模型扩展或修正的理论需求。

Abstract: We discuss the measurement of the Hubble constant ($H_0$) in flat $Λ$ cold dark matter cosmology based on the blind hierarchical analysis of 11 multiply-imaged quasars (hereafter the TDCOSMO-2026 sample) and described in greater detail in a companion paper. In addition to the time delays, we use lens models based on high-resolution imaging from the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and from the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST), analyzed jointly with axisymmetric dynamical models based on JWST-NIRSpec, VLT-MUSE, and Keck-KCWI spectroscopy. Our primary result $H_0=71.1^{+2.4}_{-2.3}$ km.s$^{-1}$.Mpc$^{-1}$ is based on the TDCOSMO-2026 sample, in combination with $Ω_{\rm m}$ constraints from the DES-Dovekie supernova (SN) dataset. Our 3% precision accounts for the mass-sheet degeneracy. Importantly, our measurement is independent of the local distance ladder (LDL). Among individual late-universe probes, only the traditional LDL is more precise. We discuss our result in the context of the Hubble tension, the difference between $H_0$ measured in the late universe and the prediction based on early-universe probes such as the cosmic microwave background (CMB). We construct combinations of independent late-universe probes and show that the tension with the Planck CMB prediction is robust to excluding any one of the methods based on Type-Ia Supernovae, Cepheids, or the Tip of the Red Giant Branch. For example, we construct a 'no-SN' $H_0$ measurement, by replacing the DES-Dovekie constraint on $Ω_{\rm m}$ with that from DESI baryon acoustic oscillations, and then combining TDCOSMO-2026 with independent non-SN late-universe probes. This 'no-SN' late-universe probe is in 3.7$σ$ tension with Planck. Even after completely excluding the LDL, the tension with Planck persists at the 2.7$σ$ level.

</details>


### [234] [Joint Constraints on the Hubble Constant and Cosmic Baryon Density from Localized Fast Radio Bursts and DESI DR2 Baryon Acoustic Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15536)
*Ji-Guo Zhang,Tian-Nuo Li,Guo-Hong Du,Wan-Peng Sun,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用快速射电暴（FRB）的色散量（DM）和重子声学振荡（BAO）的互补物理敏感性，无需CMB或BBN先验，同时测定了哈勃常数H0和宇宙重子密度Ωb。结合120个定位FRB与DESI DR2 BAO数据，在ΛCDM模型下得到H0 = 71.4^{+2.2}_{-2.5} km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹、Ωb = 0.0503^{+0.0025}_{-0.0021}，精度分别为3.3%和4.6%；结果与SH0ES、CMB及BBN+CMB一致；但采用IllustrisTNG模型并固定弥散气体重子分数后，H0中心值降至68.8±1.0，表明星系际DM建模是主导系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决哈勃常数危机和独立测定宇宙重子密度，避免对CMB或BBN等外部先验的依赖，探索FRB与BAO联合分析的新途径。

Method: 联合分析120个已定位的快速射电暴（提供色散量DM，反映沿视线方向的自由电子柱密度）与DESI DR2的重子声学振荡（BAO）数据（约束宇宙膨胀几何），在ΛCDM框架下同时拟合H0和Ωb；考虑不同DM分布模型（如IllustrisTNG）以评估系统误差。

Result: 获得H0 = 71.4^{+2.2}_{-2.5} km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹（3.3%精度）和Ωb = 0.0503^{+0.0025}_{-0.0021}（4.6%精度）；二者均与SH0ES、CMB及BBN+CMB结果一致；改用IllustrisTNG DM模型后H0 = 68.8±1.0 km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹，凸显星系际DM建模为最大系统不确定性。

Conclusion: FRB与BAO具有高度互补性，其联合分析可独立、同步地高精度限制H0和Ωb，为解决哈勃张力和检验标准宇宙学模型提供了新方法，但需进一步改进星系际介质建模。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) probe free-electron columns along cosmological sightlines through their dispersion measures (DMs), whereas baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO) constrain cosmic expansion geometry through acoustic distance ratios. In this work, we leverage their distinct physical sensitivities to simultaneously determine the Hubble constant ($H_0$) and cosmic baryon density ($Ω_{\rm b}$) without external cosmic microwave background (CMB) or Big Bang nucleosynthesis (BBN) priors. Combining 120 localized FRBs and DESI DR2 BAO, we obtain $H_0=71.4^{+2.2}_{-2.5}\ {\rm km\ s^{-1}\ Mpc^{-1}}$ and $Ω_{\rm b}=0.0503^{+0.0025}_{-0.0021}$ in $Λ$CDM, corresponding to precisions of $3.3\%$ and $4.6\%$, respectively. The inferred $H_0$ is statistically consistent with both SH0ES and CMB, and $Ω_{\rm b}$ agrees with the joint constraint from BBN and CMB. Adopting IllustrisTNG-based DM distributions and fixing the diffuse-gas baryon fraction tightens the constraint to $H_0=68.8\pm1.0\ {\rm km\ s^{-1}\ Mpc^{-1}}$, but lowers the central value toward the CMB-inferred value, identifying extragalactic DM modeling as the dominant systematic uncertainty. These results establish FRB-BAO complementarity as a new avenue for simultaneously constraining $H_0$ and $Ω_{\rm b}$.

</details>


### [235] [How much can shape degeneracies affect the Hubble constant measurement?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15999)
*Ashish K. Meena,Prasenjit Saha,Liliya L. R. Williams,Derek Perara*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了星系团透镜模型中的形状简并性（ShaDes）对时间延迟测量的影响，并指出这种简并性会显著影响哈勃常数H₀的测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团透镜模型仅依赖观测到的透镜图像位置进行约束，导致大量质量扰动存在简并性（即形状简并性，ShaDes），这些扰动可任意幅度地再现所有图像，从而影响时间延迟的准确推断。

Method: 通过分析形状简并性对透镜势的扰动效应，结合收敛率正定性、像多重性和奇偶性等物理约束，在实际星系团MACS J0138.0−2155中定量估计其对超新星SN-Encore和SN-Requiem时间延迟的影响。

Result: 在MACS J0138.0−2155中，ShaDes可使SN-Encore的观测时间延迟偏移约7天（占实测值39.8天的17.5%），并使SN-Requiem的预测重现时间偏移约112天（占预测值3000–4000天的2.8–3.7%）；这些偏差将直接、等量地传递至H₀的误差中。

Conclusion: 形状简并性是当前强透镜宇宙学中尚未被充分认识但不可忽略的系统误差来源，其影响在独立模型比对中不可见，需在H₀测量中显式建模与约束。

Abstract: Cluster lens models are constrained almost entirely by observed lensed image positions, leaving a large space of mass perturbations with arbitrary amplitudes such that they reproduce every image exactly. Such degeneracies are known as shape degeneracies (ShaDes). We show that these ShaDes shift the observed time delays, by the difference of the perturbing potential between the two images, without depending on the number of source/lens planes. Bounding their amplitude by preserving the positivity of the convergence, image multiplicity, and parity, in MACS J0138.0$-$2155, they can contribute at least $\simeq7$ days to the delay between the detected images of SN-Encore (i.e., $17.5\%$ of the measured value of $39.8$ days) and $\simeq112$ days (i.e., $2.8$--$3.7\%$ of predicted value of $3,000-4000$ days) to the reappearance intervals predicted for SN-Encore and SN-Requiem. These error bars transfer one-to-one to $H_0$, and remain invisible in comparisons of independent models.

</details>


### [236] [Detectability of 21-cm Power Spectra from the Epoch of Reionization using Modern Radio Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16194)
*Ali Bagheri Koochaksaraei,Lily Mobahat,Sara Gholamhosseinian,Javad T. Firouzjaee*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用21SSD模拟数据，结合HERA、MWA、GMRT和LOFAR等射电望远镜的灵敏度预测，评估不同再电离时期天体物理模型（X射线与莱曼带辐射效率等参数）对21-cm功率谱可观测性的差异，旨在约束星系际介质热历史的物理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于再电离时期星系际介质热历史的物理模型仍缺乏充分观测约束，存在不确定性，需借助数值模拟与观测灵敏度分析加以澄清。

Method: 基于涵盖45种参数组合的21SSD高分辨率再电离模拟，计算全球21-cm亮温度及功率谱，并结合HERA、MWA、GMRT和LOFAR四大射电阵列的仪器特性建模其热噪声与灵敏度阈值，进行模型可探测性分析。

Result: 识别出不同X射线/莱曼带辐射参数组合下哪些模型的21-cm功率谱信号能被当前各望远镜在给定置信水平下分辨，从而给出可观测性边界。

Conclusion: 通过将模拟结果与现有射电观测能力对比，本文为限制再电离时期关键天体物理参数提供了可操作的观测判据，有助于缩小星系际介质热演化模型的参数空间。

Abstract: One of the unsolved questions in astrophysics and cosmology is the development of a robust physical model to explain the thermal history of the intergalactic medium during the epoch of reionization. Various astrophysical models have been proposed, but current models remain only partially constrained by available observations. To clarify this ambiguity, hydrodynamical, semi-numerical, and N-body simulations can help solve this issue. One notable simulation from the epoch of reionization is the 21SSD simulations, which provide high-resolution results for the global 21-cm brightness temperature and the 21-cm power spectrum during the epoch of reionization. The simulation includes 45 models spanning three key astrophysical parameters of X-ray emissivity, Lyman-band emissivity, and the X-ray hard-to-soft emissivity ratio. Distinct values of these parameters result in different models of this simulation. In this work, four active radio observatories, including HERA, MWA, GMRT, and LOFAR have been considered to investigate the sensitivity and thermal noise of the 21-cm power spectrum. Each observatory has a particular sensitivity based on its instrumental characteristics, which helps assess which models lie above the forecast sensitivity thresholds of current instruments.

</details>


### [237] [Sensitivity of calibration-free DESI DR2 BAO--supernova constraints to a distance-duality tilt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16496)
*Stéphane Fay,Mikel Artola,Leandros Perivolaropoulos*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了BAO和Ia型超新星数据之间可能存在的距离对偶关系（CDDR）破缺，通过参数化偏离η(z) = D_L / [(1+z)^2 D_A] = α(1+z)^ε，研究其对CPL动力学暗能量模型相对ΛCDM偏好的影响；结果表明引入ε可显著削弱CPL偏好，且该效应比引入空间曲率Ω_k更强，同时CDDR破缺的约束在多种分析设置下稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI BAO观测显示对动力学暗能量存在中等偏好，挑战ΛCDM模型；但该偏好可能源于BAO与SN数据在哈勃图形状与偏移上的张力，暗示宇宙距离对偶关系（CDDR）可能破缺。

Method: 采用η(z) = α(1+z)^ε参数化CDDR偏离，在ΛCDM和CPL模型中联合拟合DESI DR2 BAO与多个未校准SN数据集（Pantheon+、DES-Dovekie、Union3、Union3.1），并检验不同η(z)形式、数据子集剔除及校准先验（Planck声速视界、SH0ES绝对星等）下的影响。

Result: 允许ε ≠ 0显著削弱CPL相对于ΛCDM的偏好，削弱程度超过引入Ω_k；该效应在Union3.1中相当，在其他SN数据集中更显著；η(z)约束对BAO探针剔除或Pantheon+低红移截断稳健，但DES-Dovekie偏好在z < 0.1 SN剔除后消失；校准分析得α ≃ 0.942 ± 0.014；映射到光子损失或变G机制的限制远弱于现有物理约束。

Conclusion: BAO与SN数据间的张力很可能反映CDDR的系统性偏离而非真实动力学暗能量信号；当前CDDR破缺证据尚不足以支持新物理，但凸显了距离校准与模型假设对暗能量推断的关键影响。

Abstract: Recent measurements from DESI baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO) have reported a moderate preference for dynamical dark energy, challenging the standard $Λ$CDM paradigm. Nonetheless, such a trend could reflect a tension between the shape and the offset of the Hubble diagrams predicted by current BAO and Type Ia supernovae (SN) data, which may underpin a possible breakdown of the cosmic distance duality relation (CDDR) linking luminosity distances $D_{\rm L}$ and angular-diameter distances $D_{\rm A}$. We explore this possibility by parametrizing deviations from this relation as $η(z) \equiv D_{\rm L} / [{(1+z)}^2 D_{\rm A}] = α{(1+z)}^\varepsilon$, and investigate how the preference for a Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) dynamical dark energy is modified once $\varepsilon \!\neq\! 0$ is allowed. Using DESI DR2 and uncalibrated SN data (Pantheon$+$, DES-Dovekie, Union3, and Union3.1), we find that allowing $\varepsilon$ to vary freely in both $Λ$CDM and CPL reduces the CPL preference with respect to $Λ$CDM by a larger amount than the addition of spatial curvature $Ω_k$; only Union3.1 exhibits comparable reductions. We also find that the preference reduction is robust to alternative $η(z)$ parametrizations, and that the $η(z)$ constraints remain stable under the removal of BAO tracers or low-redshift cutoffs for Pantheon$+$, whereas the DES-Dovekie preference largely disappears once $z < 0.1$ SN are removed. In a separate calibrated analysis, illustrative Planck sound-horizon and SH0ES absolute-magnitude priors give $α\simeq 0.942 \pm 0.014$, quantifying the normalization mismatch under those assumptions. We also map the CDDR violation onto photon-loss and varying-$G$ scenarios, concluding that the cosmological constraints are much weaker than existing constraints on these physical mechanisms.

</details>


### [238] [CosmoAgent: AI Agent for Theoretical Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16536)
*Stephon Alexander,Loukas Gouskos,Savvas M. Koushiappas,Cooper Niu,Wenrong Sun*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出CosmoAgent框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）自动搜索暗能量与修正引力模型，通过协变语法引导LLM生成修正引力拉格朗日量，并结合自研计算流程推导运动方程、求解背景演化、拟合晚期宇宙观测数据（SN与BAO），以AIC准则评估模型；8个主流LLM均找到名义AIC优于标准ΛCDM的模型（最佳ΔAIC = −3.328），多数恢复二次精质模型，部分发现诱导引力与标量-高斯-博内特结构，但改进幅度尚不足以确立对ΛCDM的观测偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论构建依赖人工试错，难以系统探索修正引力模型空间；需引入AI方法提升理论发现效率与广度，以应对暗能量本质这一根本性宇宙学问题。

Method: 构建基于协变语法的LLM智能体框架CosmoAgent；开发集成化计算流水线，自动完成拉格朗日量解析推导、运动方程生成、背景宇宙学数值求解及观测数据似然评估；采用AIC作为模型选择准则；在8个前沿LLM（ChatGPT、Claude、Gemini等）上开展基准测试与探索性搜索。

Result: 所有8个测试LLM均生成AIC低于平直ΛCDM的模型，最优ΔAIC = −3.328；7个LLM成功复现二次精质模型；探索性搜索还发现诱导引力与标量-高斯-博内特类结构；但AIC改进幅度小，未达统计显著性阈值。

Conclusion: CosmoAgent验证了LLM在理论物理建模中的可行性，为AI驱动的基础物理研究提供新范式；当前结果表明LLM可有效辅助模型发现，但尚不能替代物理洞察与严格贝叶斯模型比较。

Abstract: We present CosmoAgent, an agentic AI framework using large language models (LLMs) to search for dark-energy and modified-gravity models. We define a covariant grammar that allows LLM agents to explore the space of modified-gravity Lagrangians. An in-house computational pipeline derives the equations of motion, solves the background dynamics, and evaluates the likelihood using late-time expansion data, including the DES 5-year type Ia supernova sample and the DESI Data Release 2 BAO measurements. Theories are ranked by the Akaike Information Criterion (AIC) to balance simplicity and likelihood. We benchmarked 8 frontier LLMs from ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Kimi, DeepSeek, and Qwen, and all LLMs are able to find theories with a lower nominal AIC than flat $Λ$CDM, with a best improvement of $Δ$AIC = -3.328. Seven recover quadratic quintessence. Exploratory searches also return induced-gravity and scalar-Gauss-Bonnet structures. The modest AIC improvements do not establish an observational preference for these models over flat $Λ$CDM. The code is publicly available at wsunamoutlook-prog/AI-Modified-Gravity.

</details>


### [239] [The XMM Cluster Survey: Cross-Calibration of eROSITA and XMM using Dark Energy Survey Y3 Galaxy Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16741)
*J. E. Pilling,D. J. Turner,P. A. Giles,A. K. Romer,R. Wilkinson,P. J. Rooney,M. Hilton,S. Bhargava,J. Hall,R. Wang,A. Abdulghafour,B. J. Maughan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过使用DES Y3RM星系团样本，对eROSITA和XMM望远镜进行了交叉校准，发现两者在X射线温度测量上存在约28%的系统性偏差，并拟合了软X射线光度和温度与红序列丰富度的标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现eROSITA与XMM望远镜数据联合分析，需对其X射线观测结果进行精确交叉校准，尤其针对DES Y3RM星系团样本。

Method: 利用XGA Python模块测量eROSITA数据；以A3158和eeHiFLUGCS样本验证方法；构建匹配XMM（XCS分析）与eROSITA（eRASS1）的DESY3RM星系团样本（共114个）；比较温度与软X射线光度等物理量，并拟合L_X,soft–λ_RM和T_X–λ_RM标度关系。

Result: eROSITA与eRASS1温度一致；XMM温度平均比eROSITA高28±7%；L_X,soft–λ_RM关系基本一致，而T_X–λ_RM存在明显归一化偏移，尤其在高丰富度系统中。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了eROSITA与XMM在DESY3RM星系团上的可靠交叉校准，为后续多波段联合研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We present the cross-calibration of the eROSITA and XMM telescopes, using galaxy clusters detected by redMaPPer from the first three years of Dark Energy Survey observations (DESY3RM). We use the X-ray: Generate and Analyse (XGA) Python module to measure eROSITA properties, and validate our methodology using a deep pointed eROSITA CAL-PV observation of A3158, and a sample of 119 clusters from the expanded HIgh FLux Galaxy Cluster Sample (eeHiFLUGCS). Next, we construct a sample of DESY3RM clusters matched to XMM observations analysed by the XMM Cluster Survey (XCS), and to clusters in the first release of the eROSITA All-Sky Survey (eRASS1). This produced a sample of 114 DESY3RM clusters with both XMM and eROSITA detections. Our resulting X-ray temperatures determined from eROSITA were compared to eRASS1 catalogue values, showing consistency between the two analyses. Comparisons between XMM and eROSITA temperatures reveal a systematic offset, with XMM temperatures 28$\pm$7% higher on average. Finally, we fit $L_{\rm X,soft}$-$λ_{\rm RM}$ and $T_{\rm X}$-$λ_{\rm RM}$ scaling relations for both eROSITA and XMM using the same cluster sample, finding minimal differences in the $L_{\rm X,soft}$-$λ_{\rm RM}$ relation but a clear normalisation offset in the $T_{\rm X}$-$λ_{\rm RM}$ relation, particularly for the highest richness systems. This study will enable future work combining eROSITA and XMM observatories for DESY3RM clusters.

</details>


### [240] [A census of Dark Energy Survey Year 3 redMaPPer galaxy clusters in a high exposure eROSITA All Sky Survey region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16742)
*P. A. Giles,A. K. Romer,J. Pilling,R. Wilkinson,D. J. Turner,T. Jeltema,A. Farahi,M. E. Ramos-Ceja,F. Balzer,M. Kluge,S. Grandis,E. Bulbul,M. Hilton,S. Bhargava,E. Aljamal,A. E. Evrard,E. W. Upsdell,P. T. P. Viana,A. Abdulghafour,M. Aguena,S. S. Allam,O. Alves,F. Andrade-Oliveira,D. Bacon,D. Brooks,R. Camilleri,A. Carnero Rosell,J. Carretero,M. Costanzi,L. N. da Costa,J. De Vicente,S. Desai,P. Doel,C. Doux,B. Flaugher,J. Frieman,J. Garc,M. Gatti,D. Gruen,G. Gutierrez,S. R. Hinton,D. L. Hollowood,K. Honscheid,K. Kuehn,O. Lahav,S. Lee,J. L. Marshall,J. Mena-Fernández,F. Menanteau,R. Miquel,R. C. Nichol,R. L. C. Ogando,A. A. Plazas Malagón,S. Samuroff,E. Sanchez,D. Sanchez Cid,M. Smith,M. E. da Silva Pereira,M. Soares-Santos,E. Suchyta,M. E. C. Swanson,C. To,D. L. Tucker,V. Vikram,A. R. Walker,N. Weaverdyck,J. Weller*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文交叉匹配了DES Y3 RM星系团目录与eRASS1 X射线巡天目录，在特定深曝光天区中识别出57个光学星系团，其中45个在eRASS1目录中有X射线对应体，12个无匹配；对这12个未匹配团进行了强制测光分析，并结合XMM、ACT、SPT等外部数据发现其中5个存在热气体辐射，表明其物理真实性，但X射线未探测可能源于当前eRASS1深度不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为检验eROSITA第一轮全天巡天（eRASS1）对光学选星系团的X射线探测完备性，并探究X射线光度与光学丰富度关系在不同探测深度下的系统偏差。

Method: 限定于高曝光区域（R.A. 60°–90°, Dec. −58°–−48°）和红移0.2–0.65、richness > 50的DES Y3 RM星系团样本（共57个），与eRASS1星系团目录进行位置匹配；对12个未匹配团开展eRASS1强制光度测量；并调用XMM、ACT、SPT等外部数据验证其X射线/ SZ信号。

Result: 57个光学团中45个有eRASS1匹配，12个无；强制光度分析显示其X射线信号普遍较弱；外部数据证实其中5个存在热气体；未匹配团倾向于更高红移、更低richness，但无明确物理机制解释缺失X射线探测。

Conclusion: eRASS1当前深度不足以完整探测中等光度星系团的X射线辐射，L–λ关系在全样本与匹配子样本间统计一致；未来更深eRASS数据或靶向观测将有助于揭示该差异的本质。

Abstract: We present the cross-match of galaxy clusters detected by redMaPPer using the first three years of Dark Energy Survey (DESY3RM) operations, and the first release of the eROSITA-All Sky Survey (eRASS1) taken by eROSITA on board the Spectrum Roentgen Gamma telescope. We define a sky region ($60^{\circ}<{\rm R.A.}<90^{\circ}$ and $-58^{\circ}<{\rm Dec}<-48^{\circ}$) whereby the mean eRASS1 exposure time of clusters falling within this footprint is $\sim$2.5 times longer than the mean exposure time of the full eRASS1--DESY3RM overlap. Limiting to a redshift range $0.2 < z < 0.65$ and redMaPPer estimated richnesses to $>$50, we obtain a sample of 57 clusters. We match these clusters to the cluster catalogue constructed for the eRASS1, finding 45 clusters with a match and 12 with no match in the catalogue. X-ray properties of the 12 unmatched clusters are estimated via a forced photometry analysis. We investigate the form of the luminosity-richness relation, finding the relation using the full 57 cluster sample is statistically consistent with using only the 45 matched clusters, and will require the deeper data of future eRASS releases (or dedicated X-ray follow-up) to explore further potential differences. Using external data from XMM, ACT and SPT, we find detections of 5 of the 12 unmatched clusters, indicating the presence of virialised gas. Finally, we found the 12 unmatched clusters occupy a higher redshift range and lower richness range (compared to the matched clusters), and find no evidence for an underlying physical reason for the lack of X-ray emission.

</details>


### [241] [Self-Interacting Dark Matter: From Particle Physics Theories to Structure Formation Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16968)
*Martin Rosenlyst,Vinh Tran,Oliver Zier,Xuejian Shen,Mark Vogelsberger,Thejs Brinckmann,Mads T. Frandsen,Jesús Zavala,Frederik Holt Dihver Christensen,Mikkel Theiss Kristensen*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的理论到模拟框架，用于自相互作用暗物质（SIDM），将微观暗区物理与非线性结构形成联系起来，并在AREPO-2代码中实现了高效、可扩展的SIDM模拟功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接微观暗区物理与非线性结构形成，弥补现有SIDM模拟在各向异性、非弹性及耗散相互作用等方面的不足。

Method: 系统化典型SIDM模型的粒子物理要素，映射微观参数至结构形成模拟输入；在AREPO-2中实现各向同性/各向异性散射、多态/非弹性反应、耗散散射及暗物质衰变模块；引入各向异性混合SIDM方案以提升计算效率。

Result: 验证了多种测试（如束流晶格偏转、热化、均匀盒衰变、孤立晕核坍缩、宇宙学盒子与zoom-in模拟），结果符合解析预测、已有基准及等效数值方法的一致性；对强前向峰值相互作用，混合方案提速达约100倍。

Conclusion: 该模块化框架成功将暗区模型与非线性天体物理预言相连接，支持超越标准各向同性、弹性SIDM的微观相互作用探索。

Abstract: We develop a unified theory-to-simulation framework for self-interacting dark matter (SIDM), connecting microscopic dark-sector physics to nonlinear structure formation. We systematize particle-physics ingredients of representative SIDM models and map microscopic parameters and interactions onto structure-formation simulation inputs. These include internal-state populations and reaction networks, velocity- and angle-dependent cross sections, inelastic and dissipative energy exchange, decay rates and recoil kinematics, and modifications to cosmological initial conditions. The numerical framework builds on the efficient, scalable SIDM infrastructure introduced in our companion work for the moving-mesh code AREPO-2, providing substantial performance gains over AREPO-1. We implement isotropic and anisotropic self-scattering with constant or velocity-dependent cross sections, multi-state and inelastic reactions, and dissipative scattering, complemented by a dedicated dark-matter decay module. For strongly forward-peaked interactions, the anisotropic hybrid SIDM scheme combines frequent small-angle drag-diffusion with explicit rare large-angle scattering, achieving speed-ups of up to $\sim100\times$ relative to fully anisotropic Monte Carlo in the tested configurations. We validate the implementations through beam-lattice deflection experiments, thermalization and uniform-box decay tests, isolated-halo gravothermal core-collapse simulations, and cosmological box and zoom-in simulations. These tests recover analytical predictions, establish consistency between equivalent numerical treatments, and reproduce published inelastic and dissipative SIDM benchmarks. The resulting modular framework connects dark-sector models to nonlinear astrophysical predictions, enabling exploration of microscopic interactions beyond standard isotropic, elastic SIDM.

</details>


### [242] [The Cosmic Radio Dipole and Observer Effects in SKA all-sky surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17022)
*D. Bertacca,L. Böhme,B. Bottazzi Baldi,C. Burigana,M. Y. Elkhashab,C. L. Hale,S. von Hausegger,H. R. Klöckner,M. Land-Strykowski,G. F. Lewis,V. Mittal,O. T. Oayda,D. J. Schwarz,P. Tiwari,T. Trombetti,J. D. Wagenveld*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了平方公里阵列（SKA）如何通过大尺度HI和连续谱巡天，解决宇宙学原理中观测到的宇宙射电偶极异常问题，特别是其远超理论预测的幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来观测到的宇宙射电偶极各向异性显著大于基于本地观测者运动的理论预期（约3倍，显著性超5σ），对宇宙学原理（如宇宙均匀性和各向同性）构成挑战，亟需更精确观测验证。

Method: 采用理论预报方法，量化SKA大型HI与连续谱巡天在约束星系计数中的运动学物质偶极、物质功率谱的偶极调制效应以及弥漫射电辐射的运动学谱畸变等方面的能力。

Result: SKA有望以前所未有的精度测量与观测者运动相关的多种效应，从而明确检验宇宙射电偶极异常是否源于新物理或系统误差。

Conclusion: SKA凭借其高灵敏度与大视场，将成为检验宇宙学基本假设——尤其是宇宙学原理——的关键设施，有望最终厘清宇宙射电偶极异常的本质。

Abstract: The Square Kilometre Array (SKA) will observe our Universe on the largest scales and so enable the study of fundamental assumptions in cosmology, such as cosmic homogeneity and isotropy. Observations over recent years have reaffirmed an unexpected challenge to the Cosmological Principle first seen in measurements of the cosmic radio dipole. This dipole anisotropy is thought to be caused by our local observer motion, however, it is observed to be about three times larger than predicted, with significances exceeding 5$σ$. The SKA is in a unique position to shed light on this anomaly by measuring this and other effects related to our observer motion. To quantify this, we forecast the capability of large-angle HI and continuum surveys of the SKA to constrain the kinematic matter dipole in galaxy number counts, the modulation of the matter power spectrum by a kinematic dipole, and kinematic spectral distortions of diffuse radio emission.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond the Kullback--Leibler divergence: the likelihood-ratio distribution as a detectability measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17186)
*Amirhossein Samandar,Mikel Martin Barandiaran,Linn Htat Lu,Andrew H. Jaffe,Deyan P. Mihaylov,Glenn D. Starkman,Cynthia Trendafilova,Yashar Akrami,Stefano Anselmi,Javier Carrón Duque,Craig J. Copi,Fernando Cornet-Gomez,Arthur Kosowsky,Anna Negro,Ricardo G. Rodrigues,Andrius Tamosiunas*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度与模型发现潜力及参数估计精度之间的关系，特别是在零假设与含连续参数的备择假设之间。通过推导多元高斯分布下似然比的分布及其累积量，并引入‘峰显著性’作为检测统计量，作者在多个宇宙学实例中验证了KL散度并非唯一决定因素，还需结合参数空间大小、似然比分布形态及边界效应进行具体校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL散度常被用作模型区分能力的平均度量，但其与实际探测能力（如发现显著性）和参数估计精度的关系尚不明确，尤其在参数化备择模型和特定检测统计量下需具体分析。

Method: 基于多元高斯假设推导似然比分布与累积量；定义并使用‘峰显著性’（likelihood-profile peak prominence）作为搜索型检测统计量；通过在零模型下大量模拟数据校准显著性阈值；在玩具高斯模型及三个宇宙学问题（宇宙拓扑、张标比、暗能量）中开展实证分析。

Result: 发现探测能力不仅依赖KL散度大小，还显著受参数空间体积、似然比分布形状以及模型是否靠近参数边界影响；KL散度与发现潜力及参数估计精度之间无普适映射关系，必须针对每类模型与检测统计量单独校准。

Conclusion: KL散度是模型区分性的有用理论指标，但不能直接预测实际探测性能；实际数据分析中需结合具体参数结构、统计量设计与模拟校准，才能可靠评估发现潜力与参数约束能力。

Abstract: The Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence is the expectation value of the log-likelihood ratio between two specified models, evaluated with respect to one of the two models. It provides an average measure of the distinguishability of the models. We examine its connection to discovery potential and the accuracy of parameter estimates when one model represents a null hypothesis, and the alternative is a family of models characterized by one or more continuous parameters. For data drawn from a multivariate Gaussian distribution, we derive the log-likelihood-ratio distribution and its cumulants under two such hypotheses. We then search over the alternative model's parameter space and use peak prominence, the height of a likelihood-profile peak above its surrounding baseline, as an operational detection statistic. Repeating this search on simulated data drawn from the null model allows us to calibrate the prominence threshold for a specified false-positive rate. We study a toy Gaussian model as well as three cosmological examples involving cosmic topology, the tensor-to-scalar ratio, and dark energy. In all these cases, detectability of a particular model depends not only on KL divergence, but also on the size of the model parameter space, the likelihood-ratio distributions, and the distance of the model from any parameter space boundaries. The relationship between KL divergence, discovery potential, and parameter estimation must therefore be calibrated for each family of models and detection statistic under consideration.

</details>


### [244] [Interpreting the stacked kinetic SZ signal II: the degeneracy between satellite galaxies and baryonic feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17200)
*Lurdes Ondaro-Mallea,Boryana Hadzhiyska,Raul E. Angulo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了kSZ信号在星系样本周围堆叠时受卫星星系和重子反馈影响的退化问题，发现卫星星系虽仅占样本20-30%，却贡献50-80%的信号，其偏移中心位置效应与反馈导致的气体延展分布相似，导致当前数据分析中若忽略卫星成分会显著低估气体分数、高估反馈强度；需结合引力透镜与成团性观测将卫星比例定准至10%以内，才能获得稳健约束；未来巡天还将揭示卫星引起的各向异性气体分布贡献（约10%），需依赖模拟建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kSZ信号堆叠分析是探测星系周围热气体和重子反馈的重要手段，但其物理解释受限于样本中卫星星系的污染效应，亟需厘清卫星与反馈的各自贡献及相互退化关系。

Method: 基于FLAMINGO流体动力学模拟与类DESI星系样本（LRG、BGS、ELG），定量分离卫星星系与重子反馈对kSZ堆叠信号的影响，并评估其在当前（DESI×ACT）与未来巡天中的可观测性与建模需求。

Result: 卫星星系虽只占样本20–30%，却贡献50–80%的kSZ堆叠信号；其空间偏移导致的信号压制与反馈效应高度退化；忽略卫星会导致气体分数被低估达因子2、反馈强度被高估；当前数据下需卫星比例先验精度优于10%才能避免偏差；卫星引发的各向异性气体信号贡献约10%，将在未来巡天中变得统计显著。

Conclusion: 卫星星系是kSZ堆叠分析中不可忽略的关键系统误差源，必须通过多探针（如弱引力透镜、成团性）联合标定其占比，并发展包含卫星物理的模拟模型，方能从下一代kSZ观测中提取可靠的重子反馈与气体分布信息。

Abstract: The kinetic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (kSZ) signal stacked around a galaxy sample traces the gas around halos and is a powerful probe of baryonic feedback. Its interpretation, however, depends on the satellite galaxies in the sample. Using the FLAMINGO hydrodynamical simulations and DESI-like samples of luminous red galaxies (LRGs), bright galaxies survey (BGS), and emission-line galaxies (ELGs), we show that satellites and feedback have similar effects: both suppress the small-scale signal. This degeneracy arises because stacking on satellites, which are offset from the centre of their host haloes, mimics the extended gas distribution produced by feedback. Although satellites make up only 20-30% of each sample, they account for 50-80% of the signal because they occupy more massive halos than centrals; thus their imprint on the stacked profiles can be as large as that of feedback. At the precision of current DESI$\times$ACT data, neglecting satellites in the modelling biases the inferred gas fractions low, by up to a factor of two, and overestimates the feedback strength, particularly for the LRG and BGS samples. Remaining agnostic about the satellite population avoids this bias but reduces the constraining power, so robust constraints would require knowing the satellite fraction to better than 10%, for instance from lensing and clustering data of the same galaxy samples. Finally, a further contribution from satellites will become statistically significant with upcoming surveys: satellites trace the anisotropic gas distribution around their host haloes, an effect that contributes 10% of the signal, and will likely require simulation-based models to capture.

</details>


### [245] [MeV dark matter decay forecasts for the COSI mission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17217)
*Robin Anthony-Petersen,Orion Ning,Yu Watanabe,Benjamin R. Safdi,Maíra Dutra,Francesca Calore,Savitri Gallego,William Guan,Christopher M. Karwin,Alexander Kusenko,Israel Martinez-Castellanos,Shigeki Matsumoto,Michela Negro,Uwe Oberlack,Field R. Rogers,Thomas Siegert,Tadayuki Takahashi,Haoyu Xie,Hiroki Yoneda,Andreas Zoglauer,Steven E. Boggs,Dieter H. Hartmann,Carolyn A. Kierans,John A. Tomsick*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用即将发射的COSI望远镜在MeV能区开展暗物质衰变线信号搜寻的灵敏度预期，通过全天空模拟表明其将在0.4–10 MeV质量范围内将暗物质寿命限制提升一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeV能区（'MeV gap'）是暗物质衰变搜寻中长期被忽视但理论动机充分的能区，多个模型（如类轴子、惰性中微子、隐秘扇区）预言该能区存在单色谱线信号。

Method: 基于COSI望远镜的预测仪器响应，开展针对银河系晕中暗物质衰变的全天空蒙特卡洛模拟，并据此外推其2年观测对暗物质寿命的探测灵敏度。

Result: COSI在0.4–10 MeV暗物质质量范围内，对衰变寿命的探测灵敏度将比现有实验提高一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: COSI有望成为填补MeV暗物质衰变搜寻空白的关键设施，显著推进该能区的暗物质物理探索。

Abstract: The decay of dark matter (DM) is a generic prediction of many particle physics models, producing distinctive monochromatic signatures in environments such as the Galactic halo. Searches for decaying DM in the elusive 'MeV gap' have remained conspicuously under-explored, despite well-motivated models including axion-like particles, sterile neutrinos, and hidden-sector scenarios predicting signatures in this energy range. The upcoming Compton Spectrometer and Imager (COSI) telescope will help bridge this gap. Designed to probe this energy band with high spectral resolution, low background, and wide field of view, COSI is well-suited for DM line searches at the MeV scale. In this work, we make projections for COSI's 2-year sensitivity for DM decay in the Milky Way halo using dedicated all-sky simulations incorporating COSI's predicted instrument response. We conclude that COSI will improve the sensitivity to the DM lifetime by more than an order of magnitude over the DM mass range 0.4 to 10 MeV.

</details>


### [246] [Mapping Dark Matter in Merging Clusters with Weak Lensing for CSST: Morphology and Filament Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17857)
*Xinyue Chen,Ran Li,Qiao Wang,Hengjie Lin,Liang Gao,Xiangkun Liu,Huanyuan Shan,Ji Yao,Ming Li*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用CSST模拟数据和深度学习框架MIU2Net，成功恢复了非球形暗物质形态（如拉长晕和连接丝状结构），精度高、噪声鲁棒性强，优于传统Kaiser-Squires方法，为研究星系团组装与宇宙网连接提供新手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱引力透镜是探测星系团尺度暗物质分布及周围宇宙网的直接手段，但传统重建方法在恢复非球形结构（如拉长晕、丝状连接）方面存在局限，需更高效、鲁棒的方法。

Method: 基于N体模拟生成CSST风格的模拟剪切图，应用深度学习模型MIU2Net，在宽场与深场源密度下重建暗物质质量分布，并与传统Kaiser-Squires方法对比；评估中心定位精度、形状/取向恢复能力及丝状结构稳定性（用归一化不稳定性参数I_F量化）。

Result: MIU2Net可将晕中心定位在约0.1 h^(-1) Mpc内；准确恢复拉长晕的形状与取向；丝状结构路径稳定（I_F从宽场到深场下降，低红移0.041→0.037，高红移0.051→0.045）；恢复的丝状形态与真实图一致达百分比水平；相比Kaiser-Squires，更好保留低对比度各向异性结构并抑制小尺度噪声。

Conclusion: CSST弱透镜数据结合机器学习质量重建，有望实现对星系团组装历史、各向异性物质吸积以及大质量晕与宇宙网关联的统计性研究。

Abstract: Weak gravitational lensing offers a direct probe of the projected dark matter distribution in cluster-scale structures and the surrounding cosmic web. We investigate the capability of the China Space Station Telescope (CSST) to recover non-spherical dark matter morphologies using mock reduced-shear maps generated from cosmological N-body simulations. We apply the deep-learning-based MIU2Net mass-reconstruction framework to CSST-like wide- and deep-field source densities, focusing on two representative systems: filament-connected halo pairs and elongated halos. MIU2Net accurately recovers halo centers, elongated mass distributions, and the filamentary structures connecting halo pairs in both survey configurations. Halo centers are localized within about 0.1 h^(-1) Mpc, and elongated-halo shapes and orientations are reliably recovered. Filament paths are stable against shape noise, with mean normalized instability parameters I_F, defined as the mean deviation from the true filament locus in units of R_200, decreasing from 0.041 to 0.037 in the low-redshift bin and from 0.051 to 0.045 in the high-redshift bin from the wide to deep field. The recovered filament shapes remain consistent with the true maps at the percent level. Compared with conventional Kaiser-Squires reconstruction, MIU2Net better preserves low-contrast anisotropic structures while suppressing small-scale noise. These results demonstrate that CSST weak-lensing data, combined with machine-learning-based mass reconstruction, will enable statistical studies of cluster assembly, anisotropic matter accretion, and the connection between massive halos and the filamentary cosmic web.

</details>


### [247] [CosmoForge II. An exact treatment of arbitrary linear filters in quadratic maximum-likelihood power spectrum estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18012)
*Giacomo Galloni*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将二次最大似然（QML）估计器扩展到经滤波处理的CMB地图的方法，精确处理时间序列数据滤波过程，避免了传统伪功率谱方法中对模拟转移函数的依赖，并在多种滤波条件下验证了其接近像素似然最优误差界的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面CMB实验中滤波会损害大尺度B模信号，现有伪Cℓ方法需依赖模拟校准的转移函数，缺乏严格性与鲁棒性。

Method: 将CosmoForge中的QML估计器和像素似然方法严格推广至滤波后的地图：线性滤波仅共轭估计器各组分，不改变其结构；同时对比纯伪Cℓ（含B净化）、矩阵E/B净化伪Cℓ及像素似然等方法。

Result: QML误差σ(r)始终比像素似然界高2–4%；纯伪Cℓ在不同滤波下σ(r)比QML高1.21–3.7倍，而矩阵净化伪Cℓ仅高最多10%（常仰角扫描下）；高噪声时各方法差距缩小；QML无需调参与模拟转移函数即接近最优。

Conclusion: CosmoForge框架下的QML方法能稳健、近优地估计滤波CMB地图的功率谱与r参数，优于依赖模拟校准的传统伪Cℓ方法。

Abstract: Ground-based cosmic microwave background (CMB) experiments filter their time-ordered data to remove $1/f$ noise and ground pickup, which removes or mixes the large angular scales where primordial $B$ modes peak. A common remedy is a pseudo-$C_\ell$ estimator with harmonic $B$-purification and a transfer function measured from simulations. Another applies it after a matrix-based $E/B$ purification of the maps. We extend the quadratic maximum-likelihood (QML) estimator of CosmoForge to filtered maps, treating the filter exactly: a linear filter conjugates the estimator's ingredients without altering its structure. The same holds for the pixel-based likelihood of CosmoForge, which gives the smallest error bar achievable on the same maps. We compare all methods in a simplified setup under several map-level filters through the error bars on bandpowers and on the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$. QML sits $2$ to $4\%$ above the pixel-likelihood bound on $σ(r)$ in every filter state. At low noise, the pure pseudo-$C_\ell$ $σ(r)$ is $1.21$ times QML's without filtering, $3.0$ times under a scan filter along stripes of constant declination, and $3.7$ times under one from a toy constant-elevation scan. The matrix-purified pseudo-$C_\ell$ $σ(r)$ is at most $1.03$ times QML's except under the constant-elevation filters, where it reaches $1.10$. These factors shrink towards unity at higher noise. The pure pseudo-$C_\ell$ gap comes from apodisation and from harmonic $E/B$ separation; pixel-space separation closes it, except under the constant-elevation filters, which attenuate modes rather than remove them and widen the gap. QML stays within a few per cent of the optimal error bars without the tuning and simulated transfer functions of the pseudo-$C_\ell$ pipelines. Thus, CosmoForge provides a robust framework for power-spectrum estimation and inference on filtered maps. (Abridged)

</details>


### [248] [Validation of harmonic-space likelihood methods for LiteBIRD B-mode polarisation analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18036)
*G. Galloni,P. Campeti,C. Gimeno-Amo,A. Carones,A. Rizzieri,Y. Chinone,N. Krachmalnicoff,E. Martínez-González,L. Pagano,D. Adak,E. Allys,A. Anand,K. Aryan,J. Aumont,C. Baccigalupi,A. J. Banday,R. B. Giardiello,E. Gjerløw,M. Gomes,A. Gruppuso,S. E. Harper,M. Hazumi,S. Henrot-Versillé,L. T. Hergt,E. Hivon,K. Ikuma,H. Ishino,K. Kohri,L. Lamagna,M. Lattanzi,C. Leloup,M. Lembo,F. Levrier,A. I. Lonappan,M. López-Caniego,D. Maino,V. Maranchery,S. Masi,S. Matarrese,S. Micheli,M. Migliaccio,M. Monelli,L. Montier,G. Morgante,M. Morshed,L. Mousset,M. Najafi,T. Namikawa,P. Natoli,I. Obata,A. Occhiuzzi,A. Paiella,D. Paoletti,S. Paradiso,G. Pascual-Cisneros,F. Piacentini,G. Piccirilli,M. Pinchera,G. Polenta,L. Porcelli,M. Reinecke,M. Remazeilles,A. Ritacco,J. A. Rubiño-Martín,M. Ruiz-Granda,Y. Sakurai,L. Salvati,J. Sanghavi,D. Scott,D. Shenoy,M. Shiraishi,G. Signorelli,S. Stellati,R. M. Sullivan,K. Tassis,L. Terenzi,M. Tomasi,H. Tran,L. Vacher,B. van Tent,P. Vielva,S. Vinzl,D. J. Watts,I. K. Wehus,G. Weymann-Despres,for the LiteBIRD Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文验证了LiteBIRD卫星任务中用于CMB B模极化分析的谐波空间似然方法，通过20,000次端到端模拟对比像素空间基准似然，发现偏移Hamimeche & Lewis (oHL) 方法在计算效率与精度间取得良好平衡，适用于全ℓ范围（ℓ≤128）的r参数约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiteBIRD任务需高精度测量张量标量比r，而准确建模全尺度范围的似然函数是获得稳健r约束的关键前提。

Method: 使用20,000次包含CMB、仪器噪声和银河系前景残差的端到端模拟，对比去偏像素似然（大尺度ℓ≤32基准）与全范围适用的偏移Hamimeche & Lewis (oHL) 谐波似然；系统检验协方差模拟数与光束窗函数选择对结果的影响。

Result: 在ℓ≤128全范围内，oHL似然给出的r 68%上限比混合基准方法高19%–29%，差异主要取决于光束窗函数选择；oHL方法被证实为计算高效且可行的基线分析工具。

Conclusion: oHL似然方法在保持合理精度的同时显著提升计算效率，适合作为LiteBIRD数据处理与B模宇宙学推断的标准似然框架。

Abstract: The LiteBIRD satellite mission aims to measure the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$ with unprecedented sensitivity through observations of the cosmic microwave background (CMB) $B$-mode polarisation. Robust constraints on $r$ require accurate likelihood modelling across a wide range of angular scales. In this work, we validate harmonic-space likelihood methods against pixel-based benchmarks using 20\,000 end-to-end simulations that include CMB, instrumental noise, and Galactic foreground residuals processed through a realistic component-separation pipeline. We compare a debiased pixel-based likelihood, which serves as our benchmark on large scales ($\ell\leq32$), with an offset Hamimeche \& Lewis (oHL) likelihood applicable across the full multipole range. We systematically explore the space of analysis settings, identifying the number of simulations used for covariance estimation and the choice of beam window function as the most critical factors. Over the full multipole range considered ($\ell\leq128$), where the benchmark combines the pixel-based likelihood at $\ell\leq32$ with a harmonic one at smaller scales, the 68\% upper bound on $r$ from the oHL likelihood is $19$--$29\%$ larger than the benchmark one, depending on the beam window function. This demonstrates its viability as a computationally efficient baseline for LiteBIRD data analysis and cosmological inference from the polarisation $B$-mode field.

</details>


### [249] [Structure and evolution of cosmic voids within DESI DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18348)
*Óscar Monllor-Berbegal,Susana Planelles,Vicent Quilis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI DR1数据，通过预处理和新开发的AVISM空洞探测器，构建了两个不同红shift区间的宇宙空洞星表，并分析其统计性质与演化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用DESI等新一代巡天数据，深入研究宇宙空洞的结构、演化及其对宇宙学模型的约束能力。

Method: 采用DESI BGS和LRG样本，经红移空间畸变校正与速度场重建后，使用新开发的AVISM空洞探测器识别空洞，并在两个红移区间（0.1<z<0.3 和 0.4<z<0.8）内构建空洞星表。

Result: 成功识别出1114个（BGS）和216个（LRG）高质量空洞；获得空洞尺寸函数、三维形状、密度剖面和线性偏置等统计量；密度剖面显示空洞随红移演化存在质量耗尽；BGS和LRG空洞的线性偏置分别为1.31±0.02和2.14±0.04。

Conclusion: 所构建的空洞星表与理论预期一致，验证了AVISM方法的有效性，为后续空洞宇宙学研究提供了可靠数据基础。

Abstract: Ongoing stage IV galaxy surveys such as DESI or Euclid offer an unprecedented amount of information to study the large-scale structure of the Universe by mapping tens of millions of galaxies. Within the context of cosmic voids, already released and future catalogues will help better understand the void structure and evolution as well as the cosmological model constraints derived from their statistics. With the recent DESI Data Release 1, our aim is to obtain a new void catalogue that allows us to study the properties of cosmic voids at different redshifts, hence providing insight not only on their structure but also on their evolution.
  We select the DESI BGS and LRG samples for this study. Before the void-finding process, the data goes through a preprocessing pipeline tackling redshift space distortions and reconstructing the mildly non-linear velocity field. After that, the new AVISM void finder is applied to the preprocessed data, obtaining two void catalogues in different redshift bins (0.1 < z < 0.3 and 0.4 < z < 0.8), both within a 1400^3 cMpc^3 cube. We successfully recover N_BGS = 1114 and N_LRG = 216 clean voids with sizes up to ~ 65 cMpc. The void size function, 3D shape variables, density profiles, and linear bias are obtained and analysed. Both the size function and the density profiles obtained are in agreement with previous theoretical studies. The expected mass depletion in void evolution is shown by the evolution of density profiles. We obtain a roughly constant linear bias inside r < Re with mean values of 1.31 +/- 0.02 and 2.14 +/- 0.04 for BGS and LRG, respectively. Both void catalogues are publicly released.

</details>


### [250] [Constraints on the cosmic reheating history from PTA data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18460)
*Qi-Lin Yang,Shi-Jie Wang,Nan Li*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用NANOGrav 15年脉冲星计时阵列数据，通过贝叶斯推断对宇宙再加热时期的热历史进行参数约束，重点分析再加热频率f_reh和状态方程参数w对原初黑洞（PBH）丰度与标量诱导引力波（SIGW）谱的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙再加热时期影响原初黑洞丰度和标量诱导引力波谱，其能量尺度与物质成分蕴含在再加热频率f_reh和状态方程参数w中，需用观测数据对其进行约束。

Method: 采用全参数空间贝叶斯推断方法，以NANOGrav 15年数据为依据，将f_reh和w作为自由参数，系统研究δ函数型、窄型和宽型对数正态原初功率谱情形下的约束效果。

Result: 当前PTA数据可将SIGW谱峰频约束在f_p ~ 10^{-7.6}–10^{-7.0} Hz，对应f_reh ~ 10^{-9.0}–10^{-8.3} Hz、w ~ 0.19–0.79（68%置信水平），前提是功率谱宽度Δ ≲ 1；宽谱会抹平SIGW谱中的特征拐点，导致再加热信息丢失；δ函数与窄谱下关联PBH质量约为10^{-6}–10^{-4} M⊙，与OGLE微引力透镜事件暗示的暗物质候选体质量一致。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了纳赫兹引力波背景与行星质量级原初黑洞暗物质候选体之间的自洽物理联系，为利用PTA探测早期宇宙再加热物理提供了关键参数约束框架。

Abstract: The cosmic reheating is an important epoch in the very early Universe, and has significant impacts on the primordial black hole (PBH) abundance and the scalar-induced gravitational wave (SIGW) spectrum. The relevant energy scale and the constituents of cosmic medium are encoded in the reheating frequency $f_{\mathrm{reh}}$ and the equation of state parameter $w$. In this work, we perform a full parameter space Bayesian inference, using the NANOGrav 15-yr dataset to constrain the thermal history of reheating. Treating $f_{\mathrm{reh}}$ and $w$ as free parameters, we systematically investigate the Dirac $δ$, narrow and broad log-normal primordial power spectra. Current pulsar timing array data can effectively constrain the peak frequency of the SIGW spectrum as $f_{\rm p}\sim 10^{-7.6}$--$10^{-7.0}$ Hz, along with $f_{\rm reh}\sim 10^{-9.0}$--$10^{-8.3}$ Hz and $w\sim 0.19$--$0.79$ (the $68\%$ confidence level), when the power spectrum has a relatively narrow width $Δ\lesssim 1$. A broader power spectrum introduces a smoothing effect that obscures the characteristic frequency knee in the SIGW spectrum, leading to the loss of information about cosmic reheating. Furthermore, for the Dirac $δ$ and narrow spectra, the PBH masses associated with the SIGWs are anchored at the planetary mass scale around $10^{-6}$--$10^{-4} M_{\odot}$. This establishes a consistent physical framework that naturally bridges the nanohertz gravitational wave background with the dark matter candidates implied by the Optical Gravitational Lensing Experiment microlensing events.

</details>


### [251] [Testing the 4-Point Correlation Function as a Probe of Parity Violation with the Quijote-Odd Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18707)
*William Ortolá Leonard,Zachary Slepian,Alex Krolewski,Simon May*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Quijote和Quijote-Odd模拟星系晕样本，通过4点相关函数（4PCF）研究宇称破缺信号，发现测量结果主要受泊松噪声主导，未检测到统计显著的宇称奇信号，并厘清了噪声对宇称敏感4PCF统计量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索宇宙大尺度结构中可能存在的宇称破缺现象，验证4PCF作为宇称敏感探针在实际观测中的可行性与鲁棒性。

Method: 使用含初代宇称破缺四谱（primordial trispectrum）的Quijote-Odd模拟及对照组Quijote模拟，计算星系晕的4PCF自相关与互相关，复现BOSS与DESI中使用的宇称破缺搜寻方法。

Result: 未在星系晕4PCF中发现统计显著的宇称奇信号；测量结果主要由泊松噪声主导，且该噪声特征可与真实宇称奇信号响应明确区分。

Conclusion: 在当前模拟精度与信噪比下，4PCF对宇称破缺的探测受限于泊松噪声；该工作为未来巡天中利用高阶相关函数开展宇称破缺搜索提供了噪声建模与解释的重要基准。

Abstract: We investigate parity violation searches with the 4-Point Correlation Function (4PCF) using the Quijote and Quijote-Odd halo catalogs. Specifically, we explore the auto- and cross-correlation searching methodologies that have been previously used in the Baryon Oscillation Spectroscopic Survey (BOSS) and the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) searches. Although the Quijote-Odd simulations are initialized with a parity-violating primordial trispectrum, we find no statistically detectable parity-odd signal in the halo 4PCF. Instead, we find that the halo measurements are dominated by Poisson noise. We are able to distinguish this noise-dominated behavior from the response expected for a parity-odd signal. These results clarify how parity-sensitive 4PCF statistics respond to noise in a controlled simulation setting.

</details>


### [252] [Enabling Polyspectra via Stochastic Realizations of Inverse Decay Curvature Perturbations in Axion Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18748)
*Matthew A. Reinhard,Zachary Slepian,Atsushi Taruya*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合数值方法，用于高效生成轴子通胀模型中逆衰变原初曲率扰动的实现，并计算其功率谱和双谱，发现与先前使用近似模式函数的工作存在高达4倍的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前和未来的星系红移巡天为约束暴胀物理提供了新途径，但现有方法在计算原初关联函数时存在精度或效率问题，特别是对含轴子-规范场耦合的暴胀模型。

Method: 采用混合方法：在Bunch-Davies真空初始化场，再通过直接数值积分进行时间演化；针对不同ξ参数（2, 2.5, 3）生成逆衰变原初曲率扰动实现，并计算其功率谱与 bispectrum。

Result: 相比以往基于规范场模式函数近似的工作，本方法所得 bispectrum 在ξ=2时差异高达约4倍；单核CPU平均运行时间为数小时，可快速生成高阶多谱及大尺度结构模拟的初始条件。

Conclusion: 该方法兼具精度与效率，显著改进了轴子通胀原初扰动的数值模拟，为利用星系巡天数据精确检验暴胀模型提供了更可靠的理论工具。

Abstract: Current and upcoming galaxy-redshift surveys offer a promising path for constraining inflationary physics. These constraints are often imposed by relating correlations of the primordial curvature perturbation to the late-time galaxy correlation functions. The primordial correlators can either be computed analytically, or via realizations of the primordial curvature perturbations that are initialized on a grid and evolved numerically according to the classical equations of motion. In this work we take a hybrid approach, initializing the fields in the Bunch-Davies vacuum; and then imposing time evolution through direct numerical integration. Our method efficiently generates realizations of the inverse decay primordial curvature perturbations for axion inflation---an inflationary model in which a pseudoscalar inflaton field couples to a $U(1)$ gauge field via a Chern-Simons interaction. Depending on the choice of parameter $ξ\in\{2, 2.5, 3\}$, which controls gauge field particle production in this model, our method generates realizations with an average runtime of a few hours on 1 CPU core. We compute the power spectrum and bispectrum from these realizations and find a surprisingly large discrepancy between our result and previous work that used an approximation for the gauge field mode functions---up to a factor of $\sim4$ difference for the bispectrum in the case of $ξ=2$. This new method enables not only rapid computation of higher order polyspectra, but also generation of initial conditions for large-scale structure analyses.

</details>
